==== build.gradle ====
plugins {
	id 'net.fabricmc.fabric-loom' version "${loom_version}"
}

base {
	archivesName = 'karlsoncraft'
}

loom {
	mods {
		"karlson" {
			sourceSet sourceSets.main
		}
	}
}

fabricApi {
	configureTests {
		createSourceSet = true
		modId = "karlson-test"
		enableGameTests = false
		enableClientGameTests = true
		eula = true
		clearRunDirectory = false
	}
}

dependencies {
	minecraft "com.mojang:minecraft:${project.minecraft_version}"
	implementation "net.fabricmc:fabric-loader:${project.loader_version}"
	implementation "net.fabricmc.fabric-api:fabric-api:${project.fabric_api_version}"
}

processResources {
	def version = project.version
	inputs.property "version", version

	filesMatching("fabric.mod.json") {
		expand "version": version
	}
}

tasks.withType(JavaCompile).configureEach {
	it.options.release = 25
	it.options.encoding = 'UTF-8'
}

java {
	withSourcesJar()

	sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_25
	targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_25
}

==== src/gametest/java/karlson/test/KarlsonGameTest.java ====
package karlson.test;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.client.GrappleController;
import karlson.client.KarlsonMovement;
import karlson.client.Ragdolls;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.gametest.v1.FabricClientGameTest;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.gametest.v1.TestInput;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.gametest.v1.context.ClientGameTestContext;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.gametest.v1.context.TestServerContext;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.gametest.v1.context.TestSingleplayerContext;
import net.minecraft.client.CameraType;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public class KarlsonGameTest implements FabricClientGameTest {
	private final List<String> report = new ArrayList<>();
	private ClientGameTestContext ctx;
	private TestServerContext server;
	private TestInput input;

	private void log(String format, Object... args) {
		String line = String.format(Locale.ROOT, format, args);
		report.add(line);
		System.out.println("[karlson-test] " + line);
	}

	private Vec3 pos() {
		return ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> mc.player.position());
	}

	private double eye() {
		return ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> Double.valueOf(mc.player.getEyeHeight()));
	}

	private double bbHeight() {
		return ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> Double.valueOf(mc.player.getBbHeight()));
	}

	private boolean sprinting() {
		return ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> mc.player.isSprinting());
	}

	private boolean gliding() {
		return ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> mc.player.isFallFlying());
	}

	private Vec3 vel() {
		return ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> mc.player.getDeltaMovement().scale(20.0));
	}

	private double hspeed() {
		Vec3 v = vel();
		return Math.sqrt(v.x * v.x + v.z * v.z);
	}

	private void cmd(String command) {
		server.runCommand(command);
	}

	private void settle(double x, double y, double z, float yaw, float pitch) {
		releaseAll();
		cmd(String.format(Locale.ROOT, "tp @a %.2f %.2f %.2f %.1f %.1f", x, y, z, yaw, pitch));
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		ctx.runOnClient(mc -> mc.player.setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO));
		ctx.waitTicks(8);
	}

	private void releaseAll() {
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyDown);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyLeft);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyRight);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyJump);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyShift);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keySprint);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyUse);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
	}

	private void hold(String item) {
		cmd("item replace entity @a weapon.mainhand with " + item);
		ctx.waitTicks(4);
	}

	private float health() {
		return server.computeOnServer(s -> s.getPlayerList().getPlayers().get(0).getHealth());
	}

	@Override
	public void runTest(ClientGameTestContext context) {
		ctx = context;
		input = context.getInput();
		try (TestSingleplayerContext singleplayer = context.worldBuilder().create()) {
			server = singleplayer.getServer();
			singleplayer.getConnection().waitForChunksRender();
			ctx.waitTicks(5);
			String kit = server.computeOnServer(s -> {
				StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
				var inventory = s.getPlayerList().getPlayers().get(0).getInventory();
				for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
					if (!inventory.getItem(i).isEmpty()) {
						out.append(i).append('=').append(inventory.getItem(i).getItem()).append(' ');
					}
				}
				return out.toString();
			});
			log("starter kit: %s", kit);
			guide();
			cmd("gamemode survival @a");
			cmd("time set noon");
			cmd("kill @e[type=!player]");
			try {
				run();
			} catch (Throwable t) {
				log("EXCEPTION %s", t);
				throw t;
			} finally {
				releaseAll();
				try {
					Files.write(Path.of("karlson-test-report.txt"), report);
				} catch (IOException e) {
					throw new RuntimeException(e);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void run() {
		log("constants: maxSpeed=%.2f gravity=%.2f jumpDv=%.2f", KarlsonConst.MAX_SPEED, KarlsonConst.GRAVITY, KarlsonConst.JUMP_DV * 2);
		double ground = -60;

		settle(0.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		hold("minecraft:air");
		ctx.waitTicks(12);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("00_empty_hand");
		hold("minecraft:diamond_pickaxe");
		ctx.waitTicks(12);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("00_pickaxe");
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		ctx.waitTicks(25);
		log("pickaxe in hand: karlson active=%s speed=%.2f (Karlson runs at 11.12)", KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.active, hspeed());
		releaseAll();
		hold("karlson:pistol");

		settle(0.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		log("start pos %s active=%s grounded=%s", pos(), KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.active, KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.grounded);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		for (int i = 1; i <= 30; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			if (i <= 6 || i % 6 == 0) {
				log("run t=%d speed=%.2f z=%.2f", i, hspeed(), pos().z);
			}
		}
		ctx.takeScreenshot("01_running");
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		for (int i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			log("stop t=%d speed=%.2f", i, hspeed());
		}

		settle(0.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyJump);
		double top = ground;
		int landed = -1;
		for (int i = 1; i <= 30; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			if (i == 2) {
				input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyJump);
			}
			double y = pos().y;
			top = Math.max(top, y);
			if (landed < 0 && i > 3 && y <= ground + 1.0e-3) {
				landed = i;
			}
		}
		log("jump apex=%.2f blocks, landed after %d ticks (expected apex %.2f)", top - ground, landed,
				Math.pow(KarlsonConst.JUMP_DV * 2, 2) / (2 * KarlsonConst.GRAVITY));

		settle(0.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		ctx.waitTicks(25);
		double before = hspeed();
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyShift);
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		log("slide: speed before=%.2f after boost=%.2f crouching=%s", before, hspeed(), KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.crouching);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("02_sliding");
		for (int i = 1; i <= 40; i += 8) {
			ctx.waitTicks(8);
			log("slide t=%d speed=%.2f", i + 9, hspeed());
		}
		releaseAll();

		settle(0.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		double standingEye = eye();
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		ctx.waitTicks(25);
		before = hspeed();
		double slideStart = pos().z;
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keySprint);
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		log("ctrl slide: speed before=%.2f after boost=%.2f crouching=%s eye %.2f -> %.2f height=%.2f sprinting=%s", before, hspeed(),
				KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.crouching, standingEye, eye(),
				bbHeight(), sprinting());
		ctx.waitTicks(6);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("02_ctrl_slide");
		ctx.waitTicks(50);
		log("ctrl slide: slid %.2f blocks, speed now %.2f", pos().z - slideStart, hspeed());
		releaseAll();
		ctx.waitTicks(5);
		log("after slide: eye %.2f", eye());
		cmd("fill -2 -59 20 3 -59 22 minecraft:stone");
		settle(0.5, ground, 6.5, 0, 0);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		ctx.waitTicks(18);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keySprint);
		ctx.waitTicks(30);
		log("slide under a 1 block gap: z=%.2f (the gap is z 20..23)", pos().z);
		releaseAll();
		cmd("fill -2 -59 20 3 -59 22 minecraft:air");

		cmd("fill 13 -60 0 13 -48 70 minecraft:stone");
		settle(11.5, ground, 2.5, 0, 0);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		ctx.waitTicks(14);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyLeft);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyJump);
		boolean ran = false;
		double wallTop = ground;
		int wallTicks = 0;
		for (int i = 1; i <= 60; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			KarlsonMovement m = KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE;
			if (i == 3) {
				input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyJump);
			}
			if (m.wallRunning) {
				wallTicks++;
				if (!ran) {
					ran = true;
					log("wallrun started t=%d pos=%s tilt=%.1f", i, pos(), m.wallRunRotation);
				}
				if (wallTicks == 12) {
					ctx.takeScreenshot("03_wallrun");
					log("wallrun t=%d pos=%s speed=%.2f tilt=%.1f", i, pos(), hspeed(), m.wallRunRotation);
				}
			}
			wallTop = Math.max(wallTop, pos().y);
		}
		log("wallrun: ran=%s ticks=%d maxHeight=%.2f endPos=%s", ran, wallTicks, wallTop - ground, pos());
		settle(11.5, ground, 2.5, 0, 0);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		ctx.waitTicks(14);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyLeft);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyJump);
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyJump);
		for (int i = 0; i < 40 && !KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.wallRunning; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
		}
		ctx.waitTicks(8);
		Vec3 preJump = vel();
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyJump);
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		log("wall jump: before=%s after=%s", preJump, vel());
		releaseAll();
		cmd("fill 13 -60 0 13 -48 70 minecraft:air");

		settle(0.5, ground + 30, 0.5, 0, 0);
		ctx.waitTicks(60);
		log("fall from 30 blocks: health=%.1f y=%.2f", health(), pos().y);

		settle(30.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		String[] guns = {"pistol", "uzi", "shotgun", "boomer", "grappler"};
		for (String gun : guns) {
			hold("karlson:" + gun);
			ctx.takeScreenshot("10_hold_" + gun);
		}
		ctx.runOnClient(mc -> mc.options.setCameraType(CameraType.THIRD_PERSON_BACK));
		hold("karlson:shotgun");
		ctx.takeScreenshot("11_third_person_shotgun");
		ctx.runOnClient(mc -> mc.options.setCameraType(CameraType.THIRD_PERSON_FRONT));
		hold("karlson:pistol");
		ctx.takeScreenshot("11_third_person_front_pistol");
		ctx.runOnClient(mc -> mc.options.setCameraType(CameraType.FIRST_PERSON));

		hold("minecraft:air");
		String[] poses = {"90f", "150f", "0f"};
		for (String gun : new String[] {"pistol", "shotgun", "grappler"}) {
			for (int i = 0; i < poses.length; i++) {
				cmd(String.format(Locale.ROOT, "summon minecraft:mannequin %.1f -60 3.5 {Rotation:[%s,0f],equipment:{mainhand:{id:\"karlson:%s\",count:1}}}",
						28.7 + i * 1.8, poses[i], gun));
			}
			ctx.waitTicks(10);
			ctx.takeScreenshot("11_mannequin_" + gun);
			cmd("tp @e[type=minecraft:mannequin] 28 -300 3");
			ctx.waitTicks(3);
		}
		cmd("kill @e[type=item]");

		cmd("summon minecraft:husk 30.5 -60 8.5 {NoAI:1b,PersistenceRequired:1b}");
		ctx.waitTicks(5);
		hold("karlson:pistol");
		input.lookAt(0, 8);
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("12_pistol_shot");
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		log("pistol: husk health after 1 shot = %s", huskHealth());
		ctx.waitTicks(12);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(25);
		log("pistol: husk health after holding fire = %s", huskHealth());
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(10);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(6);
		log("pistol: husk health after a second burst = %s", huskHealth());
		ctx.takeScreenshot("12_ragdoll_falling");
		log("ragdoll: %s", Ragdolls.debug());
		ctx.waitTicks(40);
		log("ragdoll after 2s: %s", Ragdolls.debug());
		ctx.takeScreenshot("12_ragdoll_settled");
		Vec3 body = Ragdolls.newestCentre();
		if (body != null) {
			hold("minecraft:air");
			settle(body.x, ground, body.z - 2.2, 0, (float) Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(ground + 1.62 - body.y, 2.2)));
			ctx.takeScreenshot("12_ragdoll_close");
			input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
			ctx.waitTicks(10);
			log("grab: grabbing=%s %s", Ragdolls.isGrabbing(), Ragdolls.debug());
			input.lookAt(0, -20);
			ctx.waitTicks(12);
			ctx.takeScreenshot("12_ragdoll_held");
			log("held up: %s", Ragdolls.debug());
			input.lookAt(-70, -30);
			ctx.waitTicks(3);
			input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
			ctx.waitTicks(4);
			log("thrown: grabbing=%s %s", Ragdolls.isGrabbing(), Ragdolls.debug());
			ctx.takeScreenshot("12_ragdoll_thrown");
			ctx.waitTicks(40);
			log("landed: %s", Ragdolls.debug());
			settle(30.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		}
		cmd("summon minecraft:husk 29.3 -60 10.5 {NoAI:1b}");
		cmd("summon minecraft:cow 30.5 -60 9.5 {NoAI:1b}");
		cmd("summon minecraft:pig 31.7 -60 10.5 {NoAI:1b}");
		cmd("summon minecraft:spider 30.5 -60 11.8 {NoAI:1b}");
		cmd("summon minecraft:creeper 31.6 -60 9.0 {NoAI:1b}");
		cmd("summon minecraft:chicken 29.5 -60 9.0 {NoAI:1b}");
		ctx.waitTicks(5);
		hold("karlson:boomer");
		input.lookAt(0, 3);
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(1);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		for (int i = 1; i <= 24; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			if (i == 9 || i == 11 || i == 14 || i == 18 || i == 24) {
				ctx.takeScreenshot("16_explosion_t" + i);
			}
		}
		log("blown up: %s", Ragdolls.debug());
		ctx.waitTicks(40);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("16_ragdolls_after_blast");
		log("blown up, 2s later: %s", Ragdolls.debug());
		Vec3 heap = Ragdolls.newestCentre();
		if (heap != null) {
			settle(30.5, ground + 5, heap.z - 9, 0, 38);
			ctx.waitTicks(3);
			ctx.takeScreenshot("16_ragdolls_from_above");
			settle(30.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		}
		cmd("kill @e[type=!player]");
		cmd("fill 20 -61 0 40 -61 20 minecraft:grass_block");
		cmd("fill 20 -60 0 40 -50 20 minecraft:air");
		settle(30.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);

		cmd("fill 28 -60 6 33 -57 6 minecraft:smooth_stone");
		hold("karlson:uzi");
		input.lookAt(0, 2);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(9);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("13_uzi_impacts");
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		cmd("fill 28 -60 6 33 -57 6 minecraft:air");
		ctx.waitTicks(20);

		hold("karlson:uzi");
		input.lookAt(0, -3);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(7);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("13_uzi_tracers");
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);

		settle(30.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 90);
		hold("karlson:shotgun");
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(1);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		double boostTop = ground;
		for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			boostTop = Math.max(boostTop, pos().y);
		}
		log("shotgun boost straight down: rose %.2f blocks (expected about %.2f)", boostTop - ground,
				Math.pow(30 / KarlsonConst.MASS * KarlsonConst.SCALE, 2) / (2 * KarlsonConst.GRAVITY));

		settle(30.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 25);
		hold("karlson:boomer");
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(1);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		double maxSpeed = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			maxSpeed = Math.max(maxSpeed, vel().length());
			if (i == 6) {
				ctx.takeScreenshot("14_boomer");
			}
		}
		ctx.takeScreenshot("15_boomer_crater");
		log("boomer: max player speed after blast=%.2f health=%.1f pos=%s", maxSpeed, health(), pos());

		cmd("fill 48 -60 12 52 -57 12 minecraft:glass");
		settle(50.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUp);
		ctx.waitTicks(40);
		releaseAll();
		boolean glassGone = server.computeOnServer(s -> s.overworld().getBlockState(new BlockPos(50, -60, 12)).isAir());
		log("glass: ran through=%s z=%.2f", glassGone, pos().z);

		cmd("fill 68 -44 14 72 -44 18 minecraft:stone");
		settle(70.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		hold("karlson:grappler");
		input.lookAt(new BlockPos(70, -44, 16));
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("20_grapple_aim");
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		log("grapple: active=%s point=%s", GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling(), GrappleController.INSTANCE.point());
		ctx.takeScreenshot("21_grapple_rope_start");
		double grappleTop = ground;
		for (int i = 1; i <= 80; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			grappleTop = Math.max(grappleTop, pos().y);
			if (i == 12) {
				ctx.takeScreenshot("22_grapple_swing");
			}
			if (i % 10 == 0) {
				log("grapple t=%d pos=%s speed=%.2f", i, pos(), vel().length());
			}
		}
		ctx.runOnClient(mc -> mc.options.setCameraType(CameraType.THIRD_PERSON_BACK));
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("23_grapple_third_person");
		ctx.runOnClient(mc -> mc.options.setCameraType(CameraType.FIRST_PERSON));
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		log("grapple: max height %.2f, released active=%s", grappleTop - ground, GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling());

		cmd("effect give @a minecraft:instant_health 1 10");
		cmd("effect give @a minecraft:resistance 40 1");
		log("food before the enemy test: %s", food());
		boolean musicPlaying = ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.isPlaying());
		log("music: playing=%s volume=%.1f", musicPlaying, karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.volume());
		hold("minecraft:air");
		settle(100.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		cmd("summon karlson:enemy 100.5 -60 7.5");
		ctx.waitTicks(40);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("26_enemy");
		cmd("kill @e[type=karlson:enemy]");
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		cmd("kill @e[type=item]");
		cmd("summon karlson:enemy 105.5 -60 4.5 {NoAI:1b,Rotation:[90f,0f]}");
		cmd("summon karlson:enemy 102.5 -60 4.5 {NoAI:1b,Rotation:[180f,0f]}");
		cmd("item replace entity @e[type=karlson:enemy,nbt={NoAI:1b}] weapon.mainhand with karlson:pistol");
		settle(104.0, ground, 0.5, 0, 5);
		ctx.waitTicks(6);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("26_enemy_idle");
		karlson.entity.KarlsonEnemy.debugAnimation = 1;
		ctx.waitTicks(11);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("26_enemy_run_a");
		ctx.waitTicks(5);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("26_enemy_run_b");
		karlson.entity.KarlsonEnemy.debugAnimation = 2;
		ctx.waitTicks(12);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("26_enemy_aim");

		cmd("item replace entity @a hotbar.7 with karlson:shotgun");
		cmd("item replace entity @a hotbar.8 with karlson:boomer");
		ctx.waitTicks(12);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("26_spawn_egg");
		hold("minecraft:air");
		settle(100.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		ctx.waitTicks(70);
		log("enemy: %s player health=%.1f", enemyInfo(), health());
		hold("karlson:uzi");
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(30);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(12);
		log("enemy after the uzi: %s ragdolls=%s", enemyInfo(), Ragdolls.debug());
		ctx.takeScreenshot("26_enemy_dead");

		cmd("effect give @a minecraft:instant_health 1 10");
		cmd("effect clear @a minecraft:resistance");

		cmd("summon item 110.2 -59.8 2.4 {Item:{id:\"karlson:uzi\",count:1},PickupDelay:32767}");
		cmd("summon item 109.6 -59.8 3.2 {Item:{id:\"karlson:shotgun\",count:1},PickupDelay:32767}");
		cmd("summon item 110.5 -59.8 3.3 {Item:{id:\"karlson:boomer\",count:1},PickupDelay:32767}");
		cmd("summon item 108.9 -59.8 3.1 {Item:{id:\"karlson:grappler\",count:1},PickupDelay:32767}");
		settle(108.5, ground, 0.8, -35, 38);
		ctx.waitTicks(20);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("27_dropped_guns");
		cmd("summon item 109.9 -58.2 2.8 {Item:{id:\"karlson:pistol\",count:1},PickupDelay:32767}");
		ctx.waitTicks(5);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("27_falling_gun");
		cmd("kill @e[type=item]");
		for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
			cmd("summon karlson:enemy " + (116 + i % 6) + " -60 " + (8 + i / 6) + " {NoAI:1b}");
		}
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		cmd("kill @e[type=karlson:enemy]");
		ctx.waitTicks(25);
		log("30 dead enemies dropped: %s", droppedItems());
		cmd("kill @e[type=item]");

		hold("karlson:grappler");
		cmd("summon minecraft:iron_golem 70.5 -60 8.5 {NoAI:1b}");
		settle(70.5, ground, 0.5, 0, -2);
		ctx.waitTicks(5);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(8);
		log("grapple on a mob: grappling=%s anchor=%s point=%s", GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling(),
				ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> String.valueOf(GrappleController.INSTANCE.anchorEntity())), GrappleController.INSTANCE.point());
		ctx.takeScreenshot("28_grapple_mob");
		cmd("tp @e[type=minecraft:iron_golem] 74.5 -60 10.5");
		ctx.waitTicks(8);
		log("grapple on a mob, after it moved: point=%s", GrappleController.INSTANCE.point());
		cmd("kill @e[type=minecraft:iron_golem]");
		ctx.waitTicks(4);
		log("grapple on a mob, after it died: grappling=%s", GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling());
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(25);
		cmd("kill @e[type=item]");

		hold("karlson:grappler");
		settle(70.5, ground, -10.5, 0, 0);
		input.lookAt(new BlockPos(70, -44, 16));
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyUse);
		for (int i = 1; i <= 40; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			if (i % 5 == 0) {
				log("reel t=%d retracting=%s pos=%s speed=%.2f rope=%.2f", i, GrappleController.INSTANCE.isRetracting(), pos(), vel().length(),
						GrappleController.INSTANCE.point().distanceTo(pos()));
			}
			if (i == 12) {
				ctx.takeScreenshot("24_grapple_reel");
			}
		}
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyUse);
		ctx.waitTicks(20);
		log("reel released, still hanging: grappling=%s pos=%s speed=%.2f", GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling(), pos(), vel().length());
		releaseAll();

		cmd("item replace entity @a armor.chest with minecraft:elytra");
		hold("karlson:grappler");
		settle(70.5, ground + 30, -20.5, 0, 0);
		ctx.waitTicks(4);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyJump);
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyJump);
		ctx.waitTicks(8);
		boolean gliding = gliding();
		Vec3 glide = vel();
		input.lookAt(new BlockPos(70, -44, 16));
		ctx.waitTicks(2);
		input.holdKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		ctx.waitTicks(3);
		log("elytra: gliding=%s karlsonActive=%s grappling=%s pos=%s vel=%s", gliding, KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.active,
				GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling(), pos(), glide);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("25_elytra_grapple");
		for (int i = 1; i <= 30; i++) {
			ctx.waitTick();
			if (i % 10 == 0) {
				log("elytra grapple t=%d gliding=%s pos=%s speed=%.2f", i, gliding(), pos(), vel().length());
			}
		}
		input.releaseKey(o -> o.keyAttack);
		cmd("item replace entity @a armor.chest with minecraft:air");

		settle(70.5, ground, 0.5, 0, 0);
		input.lookAt(new BlockPos(67, -44, 16));
		ctx.waitTicks(5);
		ctx.takeScreenshot("24_grapple_aim_assist");
		log("health at end=%.1f food=%s", health(), food());

		log("music: playing=%s track=%s started=%d", ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.isPlaying()),
				karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.track(), karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.started());
		for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
			ctx.runOnClient(mc -> karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.skip());
			ctx.waitTicks(10);
			log("music after a skip: playing=%s track=%s started=%d", ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.isPlaying()),
					karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.track(), karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.started());
		}
		cmd("gamemode creative @a");
		cmd("execute in minecraft:the_nether run tp @a 0.5 140 0.5");
		ctx.waitTicks(100);
		log("music in the nether: dimension=%s playing=%s track=%s started=%d",
				ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> mc.level == null ? "none" : mc.level.dimension().identifier().toString()),
				ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.isPlaying()), karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.track(), karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.started());
		cmd("execute in minecraft:overworld run tp @a 100.5 -59 0.5");
		ctx.waitTicks(60);
		log("music back in the overworld: playing=%s track=%s started=%d", ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.isPlaying()),
				karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.track(), karlson.client.KarlsonMusic.started());
	}

	private void guide() {
		String pages = ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> {
			StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
			for (net.minecraft.network.chat.Component page : karlson.server.Guide.pages()) {
				out.append(mc.font.split(page, 114).size()).append(' ');
			}
			return out.toString();
		});
		log("guide: lines per page (14 fit): %s", pages);
		int count = karlson.server.Guide.pages().size();
		for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
			int page = i;
			ctx.runOnClient(mc -> {
				net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.BookViewScreen screen = new net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.BookViewScreen(
						net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.BookViewScreen.BookAccess.fromItem(mc.player.getInventory().getItem(0)));
				mc.gui.setScreen(screen);
				screen.setPage(page);
			});
			ctx.waitTicks(3);
			ctx.takeScreenshot("00_guide_page_" + (i + 1));
		}
		ctx.runOnClient(mc -> mc.player.connection.sendUnattendedCommand("karlson guns", null));
		ctx.waitTicks(10);
		log("guide: after the button: %s (screen closed=%s)", carried(), ctx.computeOnClient(mc -> mc.gui.screen() == null));
		ctx.takeScreenshot("00_guide_received");
		ctx.runOnClient(mc -> mc.player.connection.sendUnattendedCommand("karlson guns", null));
		ctx.waitTicks(10);
		log("guide: after a second click: %s", carried());
		ctx.takeScreenshot("00_guide_already");
	}

	private String carried() {
		return server.computeOnServer(s -> {
			java.util.Map<String, Integer> counts = new java.util.TreeMap<>();
			var inventory = s.getPlayerList().getPlayers().get(0).getInventory();
			for (int i = 0; i < inventory.getContainerSize(); i++) {
				if (!inventory.getItem(i).isEmpty()) {
					counts.merge(inventory.getItem(i).getItem().toString(), inventory.getItem(i).getCount(), Integer::sum);
				}
			}
			return counts.toString();
		});
	}

	private String food() {
		return server.computeOnServer(s -> {
			var data = s.getPlayerList().getPlayers().get(0).getFoodData();
			return data.getFoodLevel() + " saturation " + data.getSaturationLevel();
		});
	}

	private String droppedItems() {
		return server.computeOnServer(s -> {
			java.util.Map<String, Integer> counts = new java.util.TreeMap<>();
			for (Entity entity : s.overworld().getAllEntities()) {
				if (entity instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.item.ItemEntity item) {
					counts.merge(item.getItem().getItem().toString(), item.getItem().getCount(), Integer::sum);
				}
			}
			return counts.toString();
		});
	}

	private String enemyInfo() {
		return server.computeOnServer(s -> {
			StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
			for (Entity entity : s.overworld().getAllEntities()) {
				if (entity instanceof karlson.entity.KarlsonEnemy enemy) {
					out.append("health ").append(enemy.getHealth()).append(" holding ").append(enemy.getMainHandItem().getItem()).append(' ');
				}
			}
			return out.isEmpty() ? "none" : out.toString();
		});
	}

	private String huskHealth() {
		return server.computeOnServer(s -> {
			StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
			for (Entity entity : s.overworld().getAllEntities()) {
				if (entity instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Enemy && entity instanceof LivingEntity living) {
					out.append(living.getHealth()).append(living.isAlive() ? " alive " : " dead ");
				}
			}
			return out.isEmpty() ? "none (dead and gone)" : out.toString();
		});
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/GunGeometry.java ====
package karlson;

import java.util.Map;

import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public final class GunGeometry {
	private GunGeometry() {
	}

	public static final double VIEW_SCALE = KarlsonConst.SCALE;

	public static final double HOLD_RIGHT = 0.692;
	public static final double HOLD_UP = -0.7;
	public static final double HOLD_FORWARD = 1.189;

	private static final Map<String, Vec3> TIPS = Map.of(
			"pistol", new Vec3(0.0, 0.0967, -0.2101),
			"uzi", new Vec3(0.0, 0.0, -0.1652),
			"shotgun", new Vec3(0.0, -0.0059, -0.1736),
			"boomer", new Vec3(0.0, 0.0, -0.2615),
			"grappler", new Vec3(0.0, 0.0817, -0.2291));

	public static String name(Item item) {
		return BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.getKey(item).getPath();
	}

	public static Vec3 tip(String name) {
		return TIPS.getOrDefault(name, new Vec3(0, 0, -0.2));
	}

	public static Vec3 muzzle(Item item) {
		Vec3 tip = tip(name(item)).scale(1.0 / VIEW_SCALE);
		return new Vec3(HOLD_RIGHT + tip.x, HOLD_UP + tip.y, HOLD_FORWARD + 0.25 - tip.z).scale(KarlsonConst.SCALE);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/Karlson.java ====
package karlson;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.function.Predicate;

import karlson.net.Payloads;
import karlson.server.KarlsonServer;
import net.fabricmc.api.ModInitializer;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;

public class Karlson implements ModInitializer {
	public static final String MOD_ID = "karlson";

	public static Predicate<Player> clientKarlson = player -> false;

	private static Boolean music;

	public static synchronized boolean hasMusic() {
		if (music == null) {
			try (InputStream in = Karlson.class.getResourceAsStream("/assets/" + MOD_ID + "/sounds.json")) {
				music = in != null && new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8).contains("\"music.");
			} catch (IOException e) {
				music = false;
			}
		}
		return music;
	}

	public static Identifier id(String path) {
		return Identifier.fromNamespaceAndPath(MOD_ID, path);
	}

	public static boolean isKarlson(Player player) {
		return player.level().isClientSide() ? clientKarlson.test(player) : KarlsonServer.isKarlson(player);
	}

	@Override
	public void onInitialize() {
		KarlsonSounds.init();
		KarlsonItems.init();
		KarlsonEntities.init();
		Payloads.register();
		KarlsonServer.init();
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/KarlsonConst.java ====
package karlson;

public final class KarlsonConst {
	private KarlsonConst() {
	}

	public static final double SCALE = 1.8 / 3.4;
	public static final double FIXED_DT = 0.02;
	public static final double MASS = 1.5;

	public static final double WORLD_GRAVITY = 32.0;

	public static final double GRAVITY = (32.0 + 10.0 * FIXED_DT / MASS) * SCALE;

	public static final double MOVE_ACCEL = 4500.0 * FIXED_DT / MASS * SCALE;
	public static final double MAX_SPEED = 20.0 * SCALE;

	public static final double COUNTER_FACTOR_PER_STEP = 4500.0 * FIXED_DT * 0.16 / MASS * FIXED_DT;
	public static final double COUNTER_THRESHOLD = 0.01 * SCALE;

	public static final double SLIDE_DECEL = 4500.0 * FIXED_DT * 0.2 / MASS * SCALE;
	public static final double CROUCH_DOWN_ACCEL = 3000.0 * FIXED_DT / MASS * SCALE;
	public static final double SLIDE_BOOST = 400.0 * FIXED_DT / MASS * SCALE;

	public static final double JUMP_DV = 550.0 * FIXED_DT / MASS * SCALE;
	public static final double JUMP_COOLDOWN = 0.25;
	public static final double JUMP_RESET_Y = 0.5 * SCALE;

	public static final double WALLRUN_START_DV = 20.0 / MASS * SCALE;
	public static final double WALLRUN_STICK_ACCEL = MOVE_ACCEL;
	public static final double WALLRUN_UP_ACCEL = 100.0 * FIXED_DT * SCALE;
	public static final double WALLRUN_CANCEL_DV = 600.0 * FIXED_DT / MASS * SCALE;
	public static final float WALLRUN_TILT = 15.0f;

	public static final double CONTACT_GRACE = 3.0 * FIXED_DT;

	public static final double GRAPPLE_RANGE = 128.0;
	public static final double GRAPPLE_SPRING = 4.5;
	public static final double GRAPPLE_DAMPER = 7.0;
	public static final double GRAPPLE_INV_MASS = 4.5 / MASS;
	public static final double GRAPPLE_MAX_FRAC = 0.8;
	public static final double GRAPPLE_MIN_FRAC = 0.25;
	public static final double REEL_ACCEL = 45.0;
	public static final double REEL_SPEED = 32.0;
	public static final double REEL_MIN = 1.5;
	public static final double RUN_EXHAUSTION = 0.015;
	public static final float BOOMER_POWER = 3.2f;

	public static final double GRAB_RANGE = 8.0 * SCALE;
	public static final double GRAB_HOLD = 5.5 * SCALE;
	public static final double GRAB_SPRING = 40.0;
	public static final double GRAB_DAMPER = 4.0;
	public static final double GRAB_MASS_SCALE = 5.0;

	public static final double EXPLOSION_RADIUS = 11.3 * SCALE;
	public static final double EXPLOSION_IMPULSE = 450.0 / MASS * SCALE * SCALE;
	public static final double EXPLOSION_MIN_DIST = 5.0 * SCALE;

	public static final double BOOMER_GRAVITY = (32.0 - 1000.0 * FIXED_DT) * SCALE;

	public static final double SLAM_MIN_SPEED = 3.0;

	public static final double GLASS_BREAK_SPEED = 6.0;
	public static final double DOOR_BREAK_SPEED = 18.0 * SCALE;
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/KarlsonEntities.java ====
package karlson;

import karlson.entity.KarlsonEnemy;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.biome.v1.BiomeModifications;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.biome.v1.BiomeSelectors;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.object.builder.v1.entity.FabricDefaultAttributeRegistry;
import net.minecraft.core.Registry;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.MobCategory;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.SpawnPlacementTypes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.SpawnPlacements;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Monster;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.Heightmap;

public final class KarlsonEntities {
	private KarlsonEntities() {
	}

	private static final int SPAWN_WEIGHT = 25;

	public static final ResourceKey<EntityType<?>> ENEMY_KEY = ResourceKey.create(Registries.ENTITY_TYPE, Karlson.id("enemy"));
	public static final EntityType<KarlsonEnemy> ENEMY = Registry.register(BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE, ENEMY_KEY,
			EntityType.Builder.of(KarlsonEnemy::new, MobCategory.MONSTER)
					.sized(0.6f, 1.95f)
					.eyeHeight(1.74f)
					.clientTrackingRange(8)
					.noLootTable()
					.build(ENEMY_KEY));

	public static void init() {
		FabricDefaultAttributeRegistry.register(ENEMY, KarlsonEnemy.createAttributes());
		SpawnPlacements.register(ENEMY, SpawnPlacementTypes.ON_GROUND, Heightmap.Types.MOTION_BLOCKING_NO_LEAVES,
				Monster::checkSurfaceMonstersSpawnRules);
		BiomeModifications.addSpawn(BiomeSelectors.foundInOverworld(), MobCategory.MONSTER, ENEMY, SPAWN_WEIGHT, 1, 2);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/KarlsonItems.java ====
package karlson;

import java.util.function.Function;

import karlson.item.GrapplerItem;
import karlson.item.GunItem;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.creativetab.v1.FabricCreativeModeTab;
import net.minecraft.core.Registry;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.world.item.CreativeModeTab;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.SpawnEggItem;

public final class KarlsonItems {
	private KarlsonItems() {
	}

	public static final Item PISTOL = register("pistol", p -> new GunItem(p, GunItem.PISTOL));
	public static final Item UZI = register("uzi", p -> new GunItem(p, GunItem.UZI));
	public static final Item SHOTGUN = register("shotgun", p -> new GunItem(p, GunItem.SHOTGUN));
	public static final Item BOOMER = register("boomer", p -> new GunItem(p, GunItem.BOOMER));
	public static final Item GRAPPLER = register("grappler", GrapplerItem::new);
	public static final Item ENEMY_SPAWN_EGG = register("enemy_spawn_egg", p -> new SpawnEggItem(p.stacksTo(64).spawnEgg(KarlsonEntities.ENEMY)));

	public static final ResourceKey<CreativeModeTab> TAB = ResourceKey.create(Registries.CREATIVE_MODE_TAB, Karlson.id("karlson"));

	private static Item register(String name, Function<Item.Properties, Item> factory) {
		ResourceKey<Item> key = ResourceKey.create(Registries.ITEM, Karlson.id(name));
		Item item = factory.apply(new Item.Properties().setId(key).stacksTo(1));
		return Registry.register(BuiltInRegistries.ITEM, key, item);
	}

	public static void init() {
		Registry.register(BuiltInRegistries.CREATIVE_MODE_TAB, TAB, FabricCreativeModeTab.builder()
				.title(Component.translatable("itemGroup.karlson"))
				.icon(() -> new ItemStack(GRAPPLER))
				.displayItems((parameters, output) -> {
					output.accept(GRAPPLER);
					output.accept(PISTOL);
					output.accept(UZI);
					output.accept(SHOTGUN);
					output.accept(BOOMER);
					output.accept(ENEMY_SPAWN_EGG);
				})
				.build());
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/KarlsonSounds.java ====
package karlson;

import net.minecraft.core.Registry;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent;

public final class KarlsonSounds {
	private KarlsonSounds() {
	}

	public static final SoundEvent SHOOT_BASS = register("shoot_bass");
	public static final SoundEvent SHOOT_HIGH = register("shoot_high");
	public static final SoundEvent SHOOT_LOW = register("shoot_low");
	public static final SoundEvent THUMP = register("thump");
	public static final SoundEvent EXPLOSION = register("explosion");
	public static final SoundEvent EXPLOSION_HIGH = register("explosion_high");
	public static final SoundEvent EXPLOSION_MAGIC = register("explosion_magic");
	public static final SoundEvent FOOTSTEP = register("footstep");
	public static final SoundEvent MUSIC = register("music");
	public static final SoundEvent BULLET_HIT = register("bullet_hit");
	public static final SoundEvent ENEMY_HIT = register("enemy_hit");
	public static final SoundEvent HITMARKER = register("hitmarker");
	public static final SoundEvent GRAPPLE = register("grapple");
	public static final SoundEvent GUN_PICKUP = register("gun_pickup");
	public static final SoundEvent JUMP = register("jump");
	public static final SoundEvent SLIDE = register("slide");
	public static final SoundEvent SLIDE_START = register("slide_start");
	public static final SoundEvent WALL = register("wall");
	public static final SoundEvent WINDOW_BREAK = register("window_break");
	public static final SoundEvent DESTRUCTION = register("destruction");

	private static SoundEvent register(String name) {
		Identifier id = Karlson.id(name);
		return Registry.register(BuiltInRegistries.SOUND_EVENT, id, SoundEvent.createVariableRangeEvent(id));
	}

	public static void init() {
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/CameraFx.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;

public final class CameraFx {
	private CameraFx() {
	}

	private static final float SHAKE_SCALE = 1.0f;
	private static final float MAX_MAGNITUDE = 14.0f;

	private static final Random RANDOM = new Random();
	private static final List<Shake> SHAKES = new ArrayList<>();

	private static float roll;
	private static float rollVel;
	private static float shakePitch;
	private static float shakeYaw;
	private static float shakeRoll;
	private static long lastFrame;
	private static float frameDt;
	private static double speedShakeTimer;

	private static final class Shake {
		final float magnitude;
		final float roughness;
		final float fadeIn;
		final float fadeOut;
		float tick = RANDOM.nextFloat() * 200f - 100f;
		float fade;
		boolean sustain = true;

		Shake(float magnitude, float roughness, float fadeIn, float fadeOut) {
			this.magnitude = magnitude;
			this.roughness = roughness;
			this.fadeIn = fadeIn;
			this.fadeOut = fadeOut;
			if (fadeIn <= 0) {
				fade = 1;
				sustain = false;
			}
		}
	}

	public static void shakeOnce(float magnitude, float roughness, float fadeIn, float fadeOut) {
		magnitude = Math.min(magnitude * SHAKE_SCALE, MAX_MAGNITUDE);
		if (magnitude > 0.01f && SHAKES.size() < 32) {
			SHAKES.add(new Shake(magnitude, roughness, fadeIn, fadeOut));
		}
	}

	public static float frameDt() {
		return frameDt;
	}

	public static float roll() {
		return (roll + shakeRoll) * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD;
	}

	public static float pitch() {
		return shakePitch * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD;
	}

	public static float yaw() {
		return shakeYaw * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD;
	}

	public static void tick() {
		KarlsonMovement movement = KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE;
		if (!movement.active) {
			return;
		}
		speedShakeTimer -= 0.05;
		if (speedShakeTimer <= 0) {
			speedShakeTimer = 0.2;
			float magnitude = (float) (movement.velocity.length() / karlson.KarlsonConst.SCALE / 9.0);
			shakeOnce(magnitude, 0.1f * magnitude, 0.25f, 0.2f);
		}
	}

	public static void frame() {
		long now = System.nanoTime();
		frameDt = lastFrame == 0 ? 0 : Mth.clamp((now - lastFrame) / 1.0e9f, 0f, 0.1f);
		lastFrame = now;
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		if (mc.isPaused()) {
			frameDt = 0;
			Viewmodel.frame(0);
			return;
		}

		KarlsonMovement movement = KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE;
		float target = movement.active ? movement.wallRunRotation : 0f;
		float[] damped = smoothDamp(roll, target, rollVel, 0.2f, frameDt);
		roll = damped[0];
		rollVel = damped[1];

		shakePitch = 0;
		shakeYaw = 0;
		shakeRoll = 0;
		for (Iterator<Shake> it = SHAKES.iterator(); it.hasNext();) {
			Shake shake = it.next();
			if (shake.sustain) {
				shake.fade += frameDt / shake.fadeIn;
				if (shake.fade >= 1) {
					shake.fade = 1;
					shake.sustain = false;
				}
				shake.tick += frameDt * shake.roughness;
			} else {
				shake.fade -= shake.fadeOut <= 0 ? 1 : frameDt / shake.fadeOut;
				if (shake.fade <= 0) {
					it.remove();
					continue;
				}
				shake.tick += frameDt * shake.roughness * shake.fade;
			}
			float amount = shake.magnitude * shake.fade;
			shakePitch += (noise(shake.tick, 11) - 0.5f) * amount;
			shakeYaw += (noise(shake.tick, 47) - 0.5f) * amount;
			shakeRoll += (noise(shake.tick, 83) - 0.5f) * amount;
		}
		Viewmodel.frame(frameDt);
	}

	public static float[] smoothDamp(float current, float target, float velocity, float smoothTime, float dt) {
		if (dt <= 0) {
			return new float[] {current, velocity};
		}
		smoothTime = Math.max(1.0e-4f, smoothTime);
		float omega = 2f / smoothTime;
		float x = omega * dt;
		float exp = 1f / (1f + x + 0.48f * x * x + 0.235f * x * x * x);
		float change = current - target;
		float temp = (velocity + omega * change) * dt;
		float newVelocity = (velocity - omega * temp) * exp;
		float value = target + (change + temp) * exp;
		if ((target - current > 0) == (value > target)) {
			value = target;
			newVelocity = 0;
		}
		return new float[] {value, newVelocity};
	}

	private static float noise(float x, int seed) {
		int i = Mth.floor(x);
		float f = x - i;
		float t = f * f * (3f - 2f * f);
		return Mth.lerp(t, hash(i, seed), hash(i + 1, seed));
	}

	private static float hash(int i, int seed) {
		int h = i * 374761393 + seed * 668265263;
		h = (h ^ (h >>> 13)) * 1274126177;
		h ^= h >>> 16;
		return (h & 0xFFFF) / 65535f;
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/ClientBullets.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.net.Payloads;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.level.LevelRenderContext;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.rendertype.RenderTypes;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.CameraRenderState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ClipContext;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.CollisionContext;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public final class ClientBullets {
	private ClientBullets() {
	}

	private static final double U = KarlsonConst.SCALE;
	private static final double SERVER_TICK = 0.05;
	private static final double TRAIL_TIME = 0.2;
	private static final int TRAIL_SEGMENTS = 8;
	private static final double BULLET_SIZE = 0.1 * U;
	private static final double ROCKET_SIZE = 0.2815 * U;
	private static final double BLEND_TIME = 0.25;
	private static final int WHITE = Draw.argb(255, 255, 255, 255);

	private static final class Tracer {
		final int ownerId;
		final Vec3 origin;
		final Vec3 velocity;
		final boolean explosive;
		final Vec3 offset;
		double age;
		double endTime = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		double clipTime = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		Payloads.BulletEnd end;

		Tracer(Payloads.BulletSpawn spawn, Vec3 offset) {
			this.ownerId = spawn.ownerId();
			this.origin = spawn.pos();
			this.velocity = spawn.velocity();
			this.explosive = spawn.explosive();
			this.offset = offset;
		}

		Vec3 at(double t) {
			Vec3 p = origin.add(velocity.scale(t));
			if (explosive) {
				p = p.add(0, -0.5 * KarlsonConst.BOOMER_GRAVITY * (t * t + SERVER_TICK * t), 0);
			}
			if (offset != null && t < BLEND_TIME) {
				p = p.add(offset.scale(1.0 - t / BLEND_TIME));
			}
			return p;
		}

		double head() {
			return Math.min(age, Math.min(endTime, clipTime));
		}
	}

	private static final Map<Integer, Tracer> TRACERS = new HashMap<>();

	public static void clear() {
		TRACERS.clear();
	}

	public static void spawn(Payloads.BulletSpawn spawn) {
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		Vec3 offset = null;
		Vec3 flash = spawn.pos();
		double flashScale = 1.0;
		if (mc.player != null && spawn.ownerId() == mc.player.getId()) {
			Vec3 muzzle = Fx.visualMuzzle();
			if (muzzle != null) {
				offset = muzzle.subtract(spawn.pos());
				flash = muzzle;
				flashScale = Fx.viewScale();
			}
		}
		TRACERS.put(spawn.id(), new Tracer(spawn, offset));
		Fx.muzzle(flash, flashScale);
	}

	public static int end(Payloads.BulletEnd end) {
		Tracer tracer = TRACERS.get(end.id());
		if (tracer == null) {
			if (end.kind() != 2) {
				Fx.impact(end.pos(), end.normal());
			}
			return -1;
		}
		if (end.kind() != 2 && end.entityId() >= 0) {
			Ragdolls.noteBullet(end.entityId(), end.normal().scale(-1.0), end.pos());
		}
		if (tracer.explosive || end.kind() == 2) {
			TRACERS.remove(end.id());
			return tracer.ownerId;
		}
		tracer.end = end;
		double speedSq = tracer.velocity.lengthSqr();
		tracer.endTime = speedSq < 1.0e-9 ? 0.0 : Math.max(0.0, end.pos().subtract(tracer.origin).dot(tracer.velocity) / speedSq);
		return tracer.ownerId;
	}

	public static void render(LevelRenderContext context) {
		if (TRACERS.isEmpty()) {
			return;
		}
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		ClientLevel level = mc.level;
		if (level == null) {
			return;
		}
		CameraRenderState camera = context.levelState().cameraRenderState;
		Vec3 camPos = camera.pos;
		Vec3 camRight = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(1, 0, 0)));
		Vec3 camUp = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 1, 0)));
		float dt = CameraFx.frameDt();

		List<Vec3[]> trails = new ArrayList<>();
		List<double[]> heads = new ArrayList<>();
		for (Iterator<Tracer> it = TRACERS.values().iterator(); it.hasNext();) {
			Tracer tracer = it.next();
			double before = tracer.head();
			tracer.age += dt;
			if (tracer.age >= tracer.endTime) {
				Fx.impact(tracer.end.pos(), tracer.end.normal());
				it.remove();
				continue;
			}
			if (tracer.age > 6.0) {
				it.remove();
				continue;
			}
			if (tracer.clipTime == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY && tracer.age > before) {
				Vec3 from = tracer.origin.add(tracer.velocity.scale(before));
				Vec3 to = tracer.origin.add(tracer.velocity.scale(tracer.age));
				if (tracer.explosive) {
					from = from.add(0, -0.5 * KarlsonConst.BOOMER_GRAVITY * (before * before + SERVER_TICK * before), 0);
					to = to.add(0, -0.5 * KarlsonConst.BOOMER_GRAVITY * (tracer.age * tracer.age + SERVER_TICK * tracer.age), 0);
				}
				BlockHitResult hit = level.clip(new ClipContext(from, to, ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER, ClipContext.Fluid.NONE, CollisionContext.empty()));
				if (hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS) {
					double span = from.distanceTo(to);
					double frac = span < 1.0e-9 ? 0.0 : hit.getLocation().distanceTo(from) / span;
					tracer.clipTime = before + (tracer.age - before) * frac;
				}
			}

			double head = tracer.head();
			Vec3 headPos = tracer.at(head).subtract(camPos);
			heads.add(new double[] {headPos.x, headPos.y, headPos.z, tracer.explosive ? ROCKET_SIZE : BULLET_SIZE});
			Vec3[] points = new Vec3[TRAIL_SEGMENTS + 1];
			points[0] = headPos;
			for (int i = 1; i <= TRAIL_SEGMENTS; i++) {
				points[i] = tracer.at(Math.max(0.0, head - TRAIL_TIME * i / TRAIL_SEGMENTS)).subtract(camPos);
			}
			trails.add(points);
		}
		if (heads.isEmpty()) {
			return;
		}
		context.submitNodeCollector().submitCustomGeometry(context.poseStack(), RenderTypes.debugQuads(), (pose, buffer) -> {
			for (Vec3[] points : trails) {
				for (int i = 0; i < TRAIL_SEGMENTS; i++) {
					double f0 = (double) i / TRAIL_SEGMENTS;
					double f1 = (double) (i + 1) / TRAIL_SEGMENTS;
					Draw.beam(pose, buffer, points[i], points[i + 1], trailWidth(f0), trailWidth(f1), trailColor(f0), trailColor(f1));
				}
			}
			for (double[] head : heads) {
				Draw.circle(pose, buffer, new Vec3(head[0], head[1], head[2]), camRight, camUp, head[3], WHITE, 12);
			}
		});
	}

	private static double trailWidth(double f) {
		double w = f < 0.762 ? 0.125 + (0.08 - 0.125) * f / 0.762 : 0.08 * (1.0 - (f - 0.762) / (1.0 - 0.762));
		return w * 1.5 * U;
	}

	private static int trailColor(double f) {
		return Draw.argb((int) (255 * (1.0 - (1.0 - 0.404) * f)), 0, 0, 255);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/Draw.java ====
package karlson.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public final class Draw {
	private Draw() {
	}

	public static void beam(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 a, Vec3 b, double widthA, double widthB, int colorA, int colorB) {
		Vec3 dir = b.subtract(a);
		Vec3 side = dir.cross(a.add(b).scale(0.5));
		double length = side.length();
		if (length < 1.0e-9) {
			return;
		}
		side = side.scale(1.0 / length);
		Vec3 sa = side.scale(widthA * 0.5);
		Vec3 sb = side.scale(widthB * 0.5);
		vertex(pose, buffer, a.subtract(sa), colorA);
		vertex(pose, buffer, a.add(sa), colorA);
		vertex(pose, buffer, b.add(sb), colorB);
		vertex(pose, buffer, b.subtract(sb), colorB);
	}

	public static void dot(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 center, Vec3 camRight, Vec3 camUp, double size, int color) {
		quad(pose, buffer, center, camRight.scale(size * 0.5), camUp.scale(size * 0.5), color);
	}

	public static void quad(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 center, Vec3 halfA, Vec3 halfB, int color) {
		vertex(pose, buffer, center.subtract(halfA).subtract(halfB), color);
		vertex(pose, buffer, center.add(halfA).subtract(halfB), color);
		vertex(pose, buffer, center.add(halfA).add(halfB), color);
		vertex(pose, buffer, center.subtract(halfA).add(halfB), color);
	}

	public static void disc(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 center, Vec3 camRight, Vec3 camUp, double diameter, int color) {
		circle(pose, buffer, center, camRight, camUp, diameter, color, 8);
	}

	public static void circle(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 center, Vec3 camRight, Vec3 camUp, double diameter, int color, int segments) {
		fan(pose, buffer, center, camRight, camUp, diameter, color, color, segments);
	}

	public static void glow(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 center, Vec3 camRight, Vec3 camUp, double diameter, int color) {
		fan(pose, buffer, center, camRight, camUp, diameter, color, color & 0xFFFFFF, 16);
	}

	private static void fan(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 center, Vec3 camRight, Vec3 camUp, double diameter, int color, int rim, int segments) {
		double radius = diameter * 0.5;
		Vec3 previous = center.add(camRight.scale(radius));
		for (int i = 0; i < segments; i += 2) {
			double a1 = Math.PI * 2.0 * (i + 1) / segments;
			double a2 = Math.PI * 2.0 * (i + 2) / segments;
			Vec3 middle = center.add(camRight.scale(Math.cos(a1) * radius)).add(camUp.scale(Math.sin(a1) * radius));
			Vec3 next = center.add(camRight.scale(Math.cos(a2) * radius)).add(camUp.scale(Math.sin(a2) * radius));
			vertex(pose, buffer, center, color);
			vertex(pose, buffer, previous, rim);
			vertex(pose, buffer, middle, rim);
			vertex(pose, buffer, next, rim);
			previous = next;
		}
	}

	private static void vertex(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, Vec3 p, int argb) {
		buffer.addVertex(pose, (float) p.x, (float) p.y, (float) p.z).setColor(argb);
	}

	public static int argb(int a, int r, int g, int b) {
		return (a & 255) << 24 | (r & 255) << 16 | (g & 255) << 8 | (b & 255);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/EnemyGunLayer.java ====
package karlson.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import net.minecraft.client.model.HumanoidModel;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.SubmitNodeCollector;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.RenderLayerParent;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.layers.RenderLayer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.HumanoidRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.texture.OverlayTexture;

public class EnemyGunLayer extends RenderLayer<HumanoidRenderState, HumanoidModel<HumanoidRenderState>> {
	public EnemyGunLayer(RenderLayerParent<HumanoidRenderState, HumanoidModel<HumanoidRenderState>> parent) {
		super(parent);
	}

	@Override
	public void submit(PoseStack poseStack, SubmitNodeCollector collector, int light, HumanoidRenderState state, float yRot, float xRot) {
		if (!(state instanceof EnemyRenderState enemy) || enemy.frame == null || enemy.gun == null) {
			return;
		}
		GunMesh mesh = enemy.gun;
		poseStack.pushPose();
		poseStack.mulPose(enemy.frame.gun);
		collector.submitCustomGeometry(poseStack, GunSpecialRenderer.RENDER_TYPE, (pose, buffer) -> mesh.emit(pose, buffer, light, OverlayTexture.NO_OVERLAY));
		poseStack.popPose();
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/EnemyMesh.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;

public final class EnemyMesh {
	private static final String PATH = "/assets/karlson/enemymesh/enemy.txt";
	private static final int STRIDE = 8;
	private static final float PIXEL = 1f / 16f;
	private static EnemyMesh instance;

	public final float armLength;
	public final List<Part> parts = new ArrayList<>();

	public record Part(String name, float[] pivot, float[] min, float[] max, float[] data) {
		public void emit(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, int light, int overlay, int color) {
			for (int i = 0; i < data.length; i += STRIDE * 3) {
				vertex(pose, buffer, i, light, overlay, color);
				vertex(pose, buffer, i + STRIDE, light, overlay, color);
				vertex(pose, buffer, i + STRIDE * 2, light, overlay, color);
				vertex(pose, buffer, i + STRIDE * 2, light, overlay, color);
			}
		}

		private void vertex(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, int o, int light, int overlay, int color) {
			buffer.addVertex(pose, data[o], data[o + 1], data[o + 2])
					.setColor(color >> 16 & 255, color >> 8 & 255, color & 255, color >>> 24)
					.setUv(data[o + 6], data[o + 7])
					.setOverlay(overlay)
					.setLight(light)
					.setNormal(pose, data[o + 3], data[o + 4], data[o + 5]);
		}
	}

	private EnemyMesh(BufferedReader reader) throws IOException {
		armLength = Float.parseFloat(reader.readLine().trim().split(" ")[2]);
		String line;
		while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
			String[] head = line.trim().split(" ");
			int vertices = Integer.parseInt(head[11]) * 3;
			float[] data = new float[vertices * STRIDE];
			for (int i = 0; i < vertices; i++) {
				String[] values = reader.readLine().trim().split(" ");
				for (int j = 0; j < STRIDE; j++) {
					float value = Float.parseFloat(values[j]);
					data[i * STRIDE + j] = j < 3 ? value * PIXEL : value;
				}
			}
			parts.add(new Part(head[1], floats(head, 2), floats(head, 5), floats(head, 8), data));
		}
	}

	private static float[] floats(String[] values, int from) {
		return new float[] {Float.parseFloat(values[from]), Float.parseFloat(values[from + 1]), Float.parseFloat(values[from + 2])};
	}

	public static synchronized EnemyMesh get() {
		if (instance == null) {
			try (InputStream in = EnemyMesh.class.getResourceAsStream(PATH)) {
				if (in == null) {
					throw new IllegalStateException("Missing enemy mesh " + PATH);
				}
				instance = new EnemyMesh(new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
			} catch (IOException e) {
				throw new IllegalStateException("Could not read enemy mesh " + PATH, e);
			}
		}
		return instance;
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/EnemyModel.java ====
package karlson.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import net.minecraft.client.model.HumanoidModel;
import net.minecraft.client.model.geom.ModelPart;
import net.minecraft.client.model.geom.PartPose;
import net.minecraft.client.model.geom.builders.CubeListBuilder;
import net.minecraft.client.model.geom.builders.LayerDefinition;
import net.minecraft.client.model.geom.builders.MeshDefinition;
import net.minecraft.client.model.geom.builders.PartDefinition;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.HumanoidRenderState;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.HumanoidArm;

public class EnemyModel extends HumanoidModel<HumanoidRenderState> {
	private static final float VANILLA_HAND = 10f;

	private final EnemyMesh mesh;
	private EnemyRig.Frame frame;

	public EnemyModel() {
		this(EnemyMesh.get());
	}

	private EnemyModel(EnemyMesh mesh) {
		super(bake(mesh));
		this.mesh = mesh;
	}

	private static ModelPart bake(EnemyMesh mesh) {
		MeshDefinition definition = new MeshDefinition();
		PartDefinition root = definition.getRoot();
		for (EnemyMesh.Part part : mesh.parts) {
			float[] min = part.min();
			float[] max = part.max();
			float[] pivot = part.pivot();
			PartDefinition child = root.addOrReplaceChild(part.name(),
					CubeListBuilder.create().addBox(min[0], min[1], min[2], max[0] - min[0], max[1] - min[1], max[2] - min[2]),
					PartPose.offset(pivot[0], pivot[1], pivot[2]));
			if (part.name().equals("head")) {
				child.addOrReplaceChild("hat", CubeListBuilder.create(), PartPose.ZERO);
			}
		}
		return LayerDefinition.create(definition, 64, 64).bakeRoot();
	}

	@Override
	public void setupAnim(HumanoidRenderState state) {
		super.setupAnim(state);
		frame = state instanceof EnemyRenderState enemy ? enemy.frame : null;
	}

	public void renderMesh(PoseStack poseStack, VertexConsumer buffer, int light, int overlay, int color) {
		ModelPart root = root();
		poseStack.pushPose();
		root.translateAndRotate(poseStack);
		if (frame != null) {
			EnemyRig.get().emit(frame, poseStack.last(), buffer, light, overlay, color);
			poseStack.popPose();
			return;
		}
		for (EnemyMesh.Part part : mesh.parts) {
			ModelPart modelPart = root.getChild(part.name());
			if (!modelPart.visible) {
				continue;
			}
			poseStack.pushPose();
			modelPart.translateAndRotate(poseStack);
			part.emit(poseStack.last(), buffer, light, overlay, color);
			poseStack.popPose();
		}
		poseStack.popPose();
	}

	@Override
	public void translateToHand(HumanoidRenderState state, HumanoidArm arm, PoseStack poseStack) {
		super.translateToHand(state, arm, poseStack);
		poseStack.translate(0f, (mesh.armLength - VANILLA_HAND) / 16f, 0f);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/EnemyRenderState.java ====
package karlson.client;

import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.HumanoidRenderState;

public class EnemyRenderState extends HumanoidRenderState {
	public EnemyRig.Frame frame;
	public GunMesh gun;
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/EnemyRenderer.java ====
package karlson.client;

import karlson.Karlson;
import karlson.entity.KarlsonEnemy;
import net.minecraft.client.model.HumanoidModel;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.EntityRendererProvider;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.HumanoidMobRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.HumanoidRenderState;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.HumanoidArm;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;

public class EnemyRenderer extends HumanoidMobRenderer<KarlsonEnemy, HumanoidRenderState, HumanoidModel<HumanoidRenderState>> {
	private static final Identifier TEXTURE = Karlson.id("textures/entity/enemy.png");

	public EnemyRenderer(EntityRendererProvider.Context context) {
		super(context, new EnemyModel(), 0.5f);
		addLayer(new EnemyGunLayer(this));
	}

	@Override
	public HumanoidRenderState createRenderState() {
		return new EnemyRenderState();
	}

	@Override
	public void extractRenderState(KarlsonEnemy mob, HumanoidRenderState state, float partialTicks) {
		super.extractRenderState(mob, state, partialTicks);
		if (!(state instanceof EnemyRenderState enemy)) {
			return;
		}
		state.rightHandItemState.clear();
		state.leftHandItemState.clear();
		Item item = mob.getMainHandItem().getItem();
		enemy.gun = KarlsonClient.isWeapon(item) ? GunMesh.get(BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.getKey(item).getPath()) : null;
		EnemyRig rig = EnemyRig.get();
		if (!(mob.animFrame instanceof EnemyRig.Frame)) {
			mob.animFrame = rig.newFrame();
		}
		EnemyRig.Frame frame = (EnemyRig.Frame) mob.animFrame;
		enemy.frame = frame;
		float time = state.ageInTicks / 20f + (mob.getId() & 15) * 0.137f;
		rig.pose(frame, time, Mth.lerp(partialTicks, mob.runAmountO, mob.runAmount), Mth.lerp(partialTicks, mob.aimAmountO, mob.aimAmount),
				state.yRot, state.xRot);
	}

	@Override
	public Identifier getTextureLocation(HumanoidRenderState state) {
		return TEXTURE;
	}

	@Override
	protected HumanoidModel.ArmPose getArmPose(KarlsonEnemy mob, HumanoidArm arm) {
		if (arm == mob.getMainArm() && KarlsonClient.isWeapon(mob.getMainHandItem().getItem())) {
			return HumanoidModel.ArmPose.BOW_AND_ARROW;
		}
		return super.getArmPose(mob, arm);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/EnemyRig.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public final class EnemyRig {
	private static final String PATH = "/assets/karlson/enemymesh/rig.txt";
	private static final int VERTEX = 8;
	private static final int FRAME = 6;
	private static EnemyRig instance;

	public static final class Frame {
		final float[] data;
		public final Matrix4f gun = new Matrix4f();

		Frame(int vertices) {
			data = new float[vertices * FRAME];
		}
	}

	private record Clip(int frames, float seconds, int[] bound, float[][] keys) {
	}

	private final int transforms;
	private final int vertices;
	private final int gun;
	private final int head;
	private final int rightArm;
	private final int leftArm;
	private final float gunScale;
	private final Matrix4f root;
	private final int[] parent;
	private final float[] restPos;
	private final float[] restRot;
	private final float[] scale;
	private final int[] boneXform;
	private final Matrix4f[] bind;
	private final float[] vertex;
	private final int[] vertBone;
	private final float[] vertWeight;
	private final int[] tris;
	private Clip idle;
	private Clip walk;
	private Clip aim;

	private final float[] pos;
	private final float[] rot;
	private final float[] tgtPos;
	private final float[] tgtRot;
	private final Matrix4f[] world;
	private final float[] skin;
	private final Matrix4f local = new Matrix4f();
	private final Matrix4f matrix = new Matrix4f();
	private final Quaternionf parentRot = new Quaternionf();
	private final Quaternionf worldRot = new Quaternionf();
	private final Quaternionf look = new Quaternionf();
	private final Vector3f point = new Vector3f();

	private EnemyRig(BufferedReader reader) throws IOException {
		String[] head = reader.readLine().trim().split(" ");
		transforms = Integer.parseInt(head[1]);
		int bones = Integer.parseInt(head[2]);
		vertices = Integer.parseInt(head[3]);
		int triangleCount = Integer.parseInt(head[4]);
		gun = Integer.parseInt(head[5]);
		this.head = Integer.parseInt(head[6]);
		rightArm = Integer.parseInt(head[7]);
		leftArm = Integer.parseInt(head[8]);
		gunScale = Float.parseFloat(head[9]);
		root = rows(floats(reader.readLine(), 1, 12), 0);

		parent = new int[transforms];
		restPos = new float[transforms * 3];
		restRot = new float[transforms * 4];
		scale = new float[transforms * 3];
		for (int i = 0; i < transforms; i++) {
			String line = reader.readLine();
			parent[i] = Integer.parseInt(line.trim().split(" ")[1]);
			float[] values = floats(line, 3, 10);
			System.arraycopy(values, 0, restPos, i * 3, 3);
			System.arraycopy(values, 3, restRot, i * 4, 4);
			System.arraycopy(values, 7, scale, i * 3, 3);
		}
		boneXform = new int[bones];
		bind = new Matrix4f[bones];
		for (int i = 0; i < bones; i++) {
			String line = reader.readLine();
			boneXform[i] = Integer.parseInt(line.trim().split(" ")[1]);
			bind[i] = rows(floats(line, 2, 12), 0);
		}
		vertex = new float[vertices * VERTEX];
		vertBone = new int[vertices * 4];
		vertWeight = new float[vertices * 4];
		for (int i = 0; i < vertices; i++) {
			float[] values = floats(reader.readLine(), 1, 16);
			System.arraycopy(values, 0, vertex, i * VERTEX, VERTEX);
			for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
				vertBone[i * 4 + j] = (int) values[8 + j];
				vertWeight[i * 4 + j] = values[12 + j];
			}
		}
		tris = new int[triangleCount * 3];
		for (int i = 0; i < triangleCount; i++) {
			float[] values = floats(reader.readLine(), 1, 3);
			for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
				tris[i * 3 + j] = (int) values[j];
			}
		}
		String line;
		while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
			String[] parts = line.trim().split(" ");
			if (!parts[0].equals("clip")) {
				continue;
			}
			int frames = Integer.parseInt(parts[2]);
			int[] bound = new int[parts.length - 4];
			for (int i = 0; i < bound.length; i++) {
				bound[i] = Integer.parseInt(parts[4 + i]);
			}
			float[][] keys = new float[frames][];
			for (int i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
				keys[i] = floats(reader.readLine(), 1, bound.length * 7);
			}
			Clip clip = new Clip(frames, Float.parseFloat(parts[3]), bound, keys);
			switch (parts[1]) {
				case "Idle" -> idle = clip;
				case "Walk" -> walk = clip;
				case "Aim" -> aim = clip;
				default -> {
				}
			}
		}
		if (idle == null || walk == null || aim == null) {
			throw new IllegalStateException("The enemy rig lacks one of its animations");
		}

		pos = new float[transforms * 3];
		rot = new float[transforms * 4];
		tgtPos = new float[transforms * 3];
		tgtRot = new float[transforms * 4];
		world = new Matrix4f[transforms];
		for (int i = 0; i < transforms; i++) {
			world[i] = new Matrix4f();
		}
		skin = new float[bones * 12];
	}

	private static float[] floats(String line, int from, int count) {
		String[] parts = line.trim().split(" ");
		float[] out = new float[count];
		for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
			out[i] = Float.parseFloat(parts[from + i]);
		}
		return out;
	}

	private static Matrix4f rows(float[] v, int o) {
		return new Matrix4f(v[o], v[o + 4], v[o + 8], 0f, v[o + 1], v[o + 5], v[o + 9], 0f, v[o + 2], v[o + 6], v[o + 10], 0f,
				v[o + 3], v[o + 7], v[o + 11], 1f);
	}

	public static synchronized EnemyRig get() {
		if (instance == null) {
			try (InputStream in = EnemyRig.class.getResourceAsStream(PATH)) {
				if (in == null) {
					throw new IllegalStateException("Missing enemy rig " + PATH);
				}
				instance = new EnemyRig(new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
			} catch (IOException e) {
				throw new IllegalStateException("Could not read enemy rig " + PATH, e);
			}
		}
		return instance;
	}

	public Frame newFrame() {
		return new Frame(vertices);
	}

	public void pose(Frame out, float time, float run, float aiming, float yaw, float pitch) {
		System.arraycopy(restPos, 0, pos, 0, pos.length);
		System.arraycopy(restRot, 0, rot, 0, rot.length);
		blend(idle, time, 1f);
		blend(aim, time, aiming);
		blend(walk, time, run);

		look.identity().rotateY((float) Math.toRadians(yaw)).rotateX((float) Math.toRadians(pitch));
		for (int i = 0; i < transforms; i++) {
			int up = parent[i];
			float qx = rot[i * 4];
			float qy = rot[i * 4 + 1];
			float qz = rot[i * 4 + 2];
			float qw = rot[i * 4 + 3];
			if (aiming > 0f && up >= 0 && (i == head || i == rightArm || i == leftArm)) {
				world[up].getUnnormalizedRotation(parentRot);
				parentRot.mul(worldRot.set(qx, qy, qz, qw), worldRot);
				worldRot.slerp(look, aiming);
				parentRot.conjugate().mul(worldRot, worldRot);
				qx = worldRot.x;
				qy = worldRot.y;
				qz = worldRot.z;
				qw = worldRot.w;
			}
			local.translationRotateScale(pos[i * 3], pos[i * 3 + 1], pos[i * 3 + 2], qx, qy, qz, qw, scale[i * 3], scale[i * 3 + 1], scale[i * 3 + 2]);
			if (up < 0) {
				world[i].set(local);
			} else {
				world[up].mul(local, world[i]);
			}
		}

		for (int b = 0; b < boneXform.length; b++) {
			root.mul(world[boneXform[b]], matrix).mul(bind[b]);
			int o = b * 12;
			skin[o] = matrix.m00();
			skin[o + 1] = matrix.m10();
			skin[o + 2] = matrix.m20();
			skin[o + 3] = matrix.m30();
			skin[o + 4] = matrix.m01();
			skin[o + 5] = matrix.m11();
			skin[o + 6] = matrix.m21();
			skin[o + 7] = matrix.m31();
			skin[o + 8] = matrix.m02();
			skin[o + 9] = matrix.m12();
			skin[o + 10] = matrix.m22();
			skin[o + 11] = matrix.m32();
		}
		float[] data = out.data;
		for (int i = 0; i < vertices; i++) {
			int v = i * VERTEX;
			float x = vertex[v];
			float y = vertex[v + 1];
			float z = vertex[v + 2];
			float nx = vertex[v + 3];
			float ny = vertex[v + 4];
			float nz = vertex[v + 5];
			float px = 0f;
			float py = 0f;
			float pz = 0f;
			float ox = 0f;
			float oy = 0f;
			float oz = 0f;
			for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
				float w = vertWeight[i * 4 + j];
				if (w <= 0f) {
					continue;
				}
				int o = vertBone[i * 4 + j] * 12;
				px += w * (skin[o] * x + skin[o + 1] * y + skin[o + 2] * z + skin[o + 3]);
				py += w * (skin[o + 4] * x + skin[o + 5] * y + skin[o + 6] * z + skin[o + 7]);
				pz += w * (skin[o + 8] * x + skin[o + 9] * y + skin[o + 10] * z + skin[o + 11]);
				ox += w * (skin[o] * nx + skin[o + 1] * ny + skin[o + 2] * nz);
				oy += w * (skin[o + 4] * nx + skin[o + 5] * ny + skin[o + 6] * nz);
				oz += w * (skin[o + 8] * nx + skin[o + 9] * ny + skin[o + 10] * nz);
			}
			float length = (float) Math.sqrt(ox * ox + oy * oy + oz * oz);
			if (length < 1.0e-8f) {
				length = 1f;
			}
			int f = i * FRAME;
			data[f] = px;
			data[f + 1] = py;
			data[f + 2] = pz;
			data[f + 3] = ox / length;
			data[f + 4] = oy / length;
			data[f + 5] = oz / length;
		}

		Matrix4f hand = world[gun];
		hand.getTranslation(point);
		hand.getUnnormalizedRotation(worldRot);
		out.gun.set(root).translate(point).rotate(worldRot).scale(gunScale)
				.mul(matrix.set(0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f));
	}

	private void blend(Clip clip, float time, float weight) {
		if (weight <= 0f) {
			return;
		}
		System.arraycopy(restPos, 0, tgtPos, 0, tgtPos.length);
		System.arraycopy(restRot, 0, tgtRot, 0, tgtRot.length);
		float at = clip.seconds <= 0f ? 0f : (time / clip.seconds % 1f) * clip.frames;
		int first = Math.min((int) at, clip.frames - 1);
		float[] a = clip.keys[first];
		float[] b = clip.keys[(first + 1) % clip.frames];
		float t = at - first;
		for (int j = 0; j < clip.bound.length; j++) {
			int i = clip.bound[j];
			int k = j * 7;
			for (int c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
				tgtPos[i * 3 + c] = a[k + c] + (b[k + c] - a[k + c]) * t;
			}
			nlerp(a, k + 3, b, k + 3, t, tgtRot, i * 4);
		}
		if (weight >= 1f) {
			System.arraycopy(tgtPos, 0, pos, 0, pos.length);
			System.arraycopy(tgtRot, 0, rot, 0, rot.length);
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < pos.length; i++) {
			pos[i] += (tgtPos[i] - pos[i]) * weight;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < transforms; i++) {
			nlerp(rot, i * 4, tgtRot, i * 4, weight, rot, i * 4);
		}
	}

	private static void nlerp(float[] a, int ao, float[] b, int bo, float t, float[] out, int oo) {
		float dot = a[ao] * b[bo] + a[ao + 1] * b[bo + 1] + a[ao + 2] * b[bo + 2] + a[ao + 3] * b[bo + 3];
		float s = dot < 0f ? -t : t;
		float x = a[ao] * (1f - t) + b[bo] * s;
		float y = a[ao + 1] * (1f - t) + b[bo + 1] * s;
		float z = a[ao + 2] * (1f - t) + b[bo + 2] * s;
		float w = a[ao + 3] * (1f - t) + b[bo + 3] * s;
		float length = (float) Math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z + w * w);
		if (length < 1.0e-8f) {
			length = 1f;
		}
		out[oo] = x / length;
		out[oo + 1] = y / length;
		out[oo + 2] = z / length;
		out[oo + 3] = w / length;
	}

	public void emit(Frame frame, PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, int light, int overlay, int color) {
		float[] data = frame.data;
		for (int i = 0; i < tris.length; i += 3) {
			corner(data, tris[i], pose, buffer, light, overlay, color);
			corner(data, tris[i + 1], pose, buffer, light, overlay, color);
			corner(data, tris[i + 2], pose, buffer, light, overlay, color);
			corner(data, tris[i + 2], pose, buffer, light, overlay, color);
		}
	}

	private void corner(float[] data, int index, PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, int light, int overlay, int color) {
		int f = index * FRAME;
		int v = index * VERTEX;
		buffer.addVertex(pose, data[f], data[f + 1], data[f + 2])
				.setColor(color >> 16 & 255, color >> 8 & 255, color & 255, color >>> 24)
				.setUv(vertex[v + 6], vertex[v + 7])
				.setOverlay(overlay)
				.setLight(light)
				.setNormal(pose, data[f + 3], data[f + 4], data[f + 5]);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/Fx.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.level.LevelRenderContext;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.rendertype.RenderTypes;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.CameraRenderState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ClipContext;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.CollisionContext;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public final class Fx {
	private Fx() {
	}

	private static final double U = KarlsonConst.SCALE;
	private static final int MAX_PARTICLES = 6000;
	private static final double HOT_PULL = 3.0;

	private static final int DISC = 0;
	private static final int GLOW = 1;
	private static final int SQUARE = 2;

	private static final int CURVE_BURST = 0;
	private static final int CURVE_SHRINK = 1;
	private static final int CURVE_SPARK = 2;

	private static final class Particle {
		double x;
		double y;
		double z;
		double vx;
		double vy;
		double vz;
		float size;
		float life;
		float age;
		float delay;
		int color;
		int shape;
		int curve;
		float gravity;
		float limit;
		float dampen;
		float noise;
		float noiseFreq;
		boolean collide;
		float bounce;
		float keep = 1f;
		float rot;
		float rotVel;
		boolean hot;
		double camDist;
		float drawSize;
	}

	private static final List<Particle> PARTICLES = new ArrayList<>();
	private static final Random RANDOM = new Random();

	private static Vec3 camPos = Vec3.ZERO;
	private static Vec3 camRight = new Vec3(1, 0, 0);
	private static Vec3 camUp = new Vec3(0, 1, 0);
	private static Vec3 camFwd = new Vec3(0, 0, 1);
	private static boolean firstPerson;
	private static double handFit = 1.0;
	private static double fovScale = 1.0;

	public static void clear() {
		PARTICLES.clear();
	}

	private static float range(float min, float max) {
		return min + RANDOM.nextFloat() * (max - min);
	}

	private static Vec3 randomDirection() {
		double y = RANDOM.nextDouble() * 2.0 - 1.0;
		double a = RANDOM.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2.0;
		double r = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0.0, 1.0 - y * y));
		return new Vec3(Math.cos(a) * r, y, Math.sin(a) * r);
	}

	private static int mix(int a, int b, float t) {
		int aa = (int) (((a >>> 24) & 255) + (((b >>> 24) & 255) - ((a >>> 24) & 255)) * t);
		int rr = (int) (((a >> 16) & 255) + (((b >> 16) & 255) - ((a >> 16) & 255)) * t);
		int gg = (int) (((a >> 8) & 255) + (((b >> 8) & 255) - ((a >> 8) & 255)) * t);
		int bb = (int) ((a & 255) + ((b & 255) - (a & 255)) * t);
		return Draw.argb(aa, rr, gg, bb);
	}

	private static int rgb(double r, double g, double b) {
		return Draw.argb(255, (int) Math.round(r * 255), (int) Math.round(g * 255), (int) Math.round(b * 255));
	}

	private static void add(Particle ptc) {
		if (PARTICLES.size() < MAX_PARTICLES) {
			PARTICLES.add(ptc);
		}
	}

	public static Vec3 visualMuzzle() {
		if (!firstPerson || !Viewmodel.isActive()) {
			return null;
		}
		Vector3f tip = Viewmodel.tipViewPosition();
		return camPos.add(camRight.scale(tip.x * handFit)).add(camUp.scale(tip.y * handFit)).add(camFwd.scale(-tip.z + 0.25 * U));
	}

	public static double viewScale() {
		return fovScale;
	}

	private static void burst(Vec3 center, float delay, int count, float lifeMin, float lifeMax, float speedMin, float speedMax,
			float sizeMin, float sizeMax, int colorA, int colorB, float gravity, int shape, float simSpeed) {
		for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
			Particle p = new Particle();
			Vec3 dir = randomDirection();
			double radius = Math.cbrt(RANDOM.nextDouble());
			p.x = center.x + dir.x * radius * U;
			p.y = center.y + dir.y * radius * U;
			p.z = center.z + dir.z * radius * U;
			double speed = range(speedMin, speedMax) * simSpeed * U;
			p.vx = dir.x * speed;
			p.vy = dir.y * speed;
			p.vz = dir.z * speed;
			p.size = (float) (range(sizeMin, sizeMax) * U);
			p.life = range(lifeMin, lifeMax) / simSpeed;
			p.delay = delay;
			p.color = grade(mix(colorA, colorB, RANDOM.nextFloat()));
			if (shape == GLOW) {
				p.color = hot(p.color);
				p.hot = true;
			}
			p.shape = shape;
			p.curve = CURVE_BURST;
			p.gravity = (float) (gravity * KarlsonConst.WORLD_GRAVITY * simSpeed * simSpeed * U);
			p.limit = (float) (4.0 * simSpeed * U);
			p.dampen = 0.05f;
			p.noise = (float) (3.06 * simSpeed * U);
			p.noiseFreq = 0.5f;
			p.collide = true;
			add(p);
		}
	}

	private static double toLinear(double c) {
		return c <= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : Math.pow((c + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
	}

	private static int toSrgb(double c) {
		c = Math.max(0.0, Math.min(1.0, c));
		return (int) Math.round((c <= 0.0031308 ? c * 12.92 : 1.055 * Math.pow(c, 1.0 / 2.4) - 0.055) * 255.0);
	}

	static int grade(int argb) {
		double r = toLinear(((argb >> 16) & 255) / 255.0) * 0.896;
		double g = toLinear(((argb >> 8) & 255) / 255.0) * 0.896;
		double b = toLinear((argb & 255) / 255.0) * 0.896;
		double grey = Math.pow(0.5, 2.2);
		r = ((r - grey) * 1.363 + grey) * 1.05;
		g = (g - grey) * 1.363 + grey;
		b = (b - grey) * 1.363 + grey;
		double luma = 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
		r = luma + (r - luma) * 1.23;
		g = luma + (g - luma) * 1.23;
		b = luma + (b - luma) * 1.23;
		return Draw.argb(argb >>> 24, toSrgb(r), toSrgb(g), toSrgb(b));
	}

	private static int hot(int argb) {
		return mix(argb, Draw.argb(255, 255, 244, 200), 0.45f) & 0xFFFFFF | 120 << 24;
	}

	private static void bloom(Vec3 center) {
		for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
			Particle p = new Particle();
			p.x = center.x;
			p.y = center.y;
			p.z = center.z;
			p.size = (float) ((9.0 + i * 4.5) * U);
			p.life = 0.35f + i * 0.1f;
			p.color = Draw.argb(70 - i * 15, 255, 170, 80);
			p.shape = GLOW;
			p.curve = CURVE_SHRINK;
