			p.hot = true;
			add(p);
		}
	}

	public static void explosion(Vec3 pos) {
		bloom(pos);
		burst(pos, 0f, 40, 0.6f, 1.0f, 10f, 25f, 0.6f, 7f, rgb(1.0, 0.661, 0.221), rgb(1.0, 0.532, 0.150), 0.1f, GLOW, 1f);
		burst(pos, 0f, 40, 0.5f, 0.7f, 12f, 30f, 0.7f, 7f, rgb(0.604, 0.279, 0.0), rgb(1.0, 0.163, 0.0), 0.15f, DISC, 1f);
		burst(pos, 0.05f, 40, 0.5f, 0.7f, 12f, 30f, 0.7f, 7f, rgb(1.0, 0.583, 0.0), rgb(1.0, 0.766, 0.0), 0.15f, DISC, 1f);
		burst(pos, 0.1f, 30, 1.0f, 2.0f, 8f, 20f, 0.4f, 2.5f, rgb(0.642, 0.642, 0.642), rgb(0.783, 0.783, 0.783), 0f, DISC, 1f);
		burst(pos, 0f, 30, 0.5f, 0.7f, 7f, 15f, 0.5f, 5f, rgb(0.604, 0.279, 0.0), rgb(1.0, 0.163, 0.0), 0.15f, DISC, 1.2f);
		burst(pos, 0.4f, 30, 0.5f, 0.7f, 7f, 15f, 0.4f, 5f, rgb(1.0, 0.958, 0.0), rgb(1.0, 0.418, 0.0), 0.15f, DISC, 1f);
	}

	public static void muzzle(Vec3 pos, double scale) {
		double s = 0.35 * U * scale;
		for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
			boolean yellow = i < 20;
			Particle p = new Particle();
			Vec3 dir = randomDirection();
			double radius = Math.cbrt(RANDOM.nextDouble()) * 0.18 * s;
			p.x = pos.x + dir.x * radius;
			p.y = pos.y + dir.y * radius;
			p.z = pos.z + dir.z * radius;
			float simSpeed = yellow ? 1.29f : 1f;
			double speed = (yellow ? range(3f, 7f) : range(2f, 5f)) * simSpeed * s;
			p.vx = dir.x * speed;
			p.vy = dir.y * speed;
			p.vz = dir.z * speed;
			p.size = (float) ((yellow ? range(2f, 4f) : range(0.2f, 1f)) * s);
			p.life = (yellow ? range(0.05f, 0.1f) : range(0.05f, 0.2f)) / simSpeed;
			p.color = yellow ? Draw.argb(109, 249, 255, 85) : Draw.argb(123, 255, 69, 0);
			p.shape = GLOW;
			p.curve = CURVE_SHRINK;
			p.hot = true;
			add(p);
		}
	}

	public static void impact(Vec3 pos, Vec3 normal) {
		if (normal.lengthSqr() < 1.0e-6) {
			normal = new Vec3(0, 1, 0);
		}
		normal = normal.normalize();
		Vec3 side = normal.cross(Math.abs(normal.y) > 0.9 ? new Vec3(1, 0, 0) : new Vec3(0, 1, 0)).normalize();
		Vec3 other = normal.cross(side);
		Vec3 origin = pos.add(normal.scale(0.05 * U));
		double cone = Math.toRadians(36.71);
		for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
			Particle p = new Particle();
			double a = RANDOM.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2.0;
			double spread = Math.sqrt(RANDOM.nextDouble());
			double tilt = cone * spread;
			Vec3 radial = side.scale(Math.cos(a)).add(other.scale(Math.sin(a)));
			Vec3 dir = normal.scale(Math.cos(tilt)).add(radial.scale(Math.sin(tilt)));
			p.x = origin.x + radial.x * spread * 0.06 * U;
			p.y = origin.y + radial.y * spread * 0.06 * U;
			p.z = origin.z + radial.z * spread * 0.06 * U;
			double speed = range(5f, 10f) * U;
			p.vx = dir.x * speed;
			p.vy = dir.y * speed;
			p.vz = dir.z * speed;
			p.size = (float) (range(0.03f, 0.1f) * U);
			p.life = range(0.6f, 1.2f);
			p.color = Draw.argb(255, 0, 0, 255);
			p.shape = SQUARE;
			p.curve = CURVE_SPARK;
			p.gravity = (float) (0.9 * KarlsonConst.WORLD_GRAVITY * U);
			p.noise = (float) (1.0 * U);
			p.noiseFreq = 0.41f;
			p.collide = true;
			p.bounce = 0.12f;
			p.keep = 1f - 0.51f;
			p.rot = (float) Math.toRadians(range(0f, 90f));
			p.rotVel = range(0.785f, 9.42f);
			add(p);
		}
	}

	private static float curve(int curve, float t) {
		switch (curve) {
			case CURVE_BURST:
				if (t < 0.168f) {
					float k = t / 0.168f;
					return k * (2f - k);
				}
				if (t < 0.697f) {
					return 1f - 0.295f * (t - 0.168f) / (0.697f - 0.168f);
				}
				return 0.705f * (1f - (t - 0.697f) / (1f - 0.697f));
			case CURVE_SPARK:
				return t < 0.015f ? t / 0.015f : 1f - (t - 0.015f) / 0.985f;
			default:
				return 1f - t;
		}
	}

	private static double swirl(double a, double b, double seed) {
		return Math.sin(a * 2.3 + seed) * 0.6 + Math.sin(b * 3.7 + seed * 1.7) * 0.4;
	}

	private static void step(ClientLevel level, Particle p, float dt) {
		p.vy -= p.gravity * dt;
		if (p.limit > 0f) {
			double speed = Math.sqrt(p.vx * p.vx + p.vy * p.vy + p.vz * p.vz);
			if (speed > p.limit) {
				double excess = (speed - p.limit) * Math.pow(1.0 - p.dampen, dt * 30.0);
				double k = (p.limit + excess) / speed;
				p.vx *= k;
				p.vy *= k;
				p.vz *= k;
			}
		}
		double dx = p.vx * dt;
		double dy = p.vy * dt;
		double dz = p.vz * dt;
		if (p.noise > 0f) {
			double f = p.noiseFreq / U;
			double nx = swirl(p.y * f, p.z * f, 0.0);
			double ny = swirl(p.z * f, p.x * f, 2.1);
			double nz = swirl(p.x * f, p.y * f, 4.2);
			dx += nx * p.noise * dt;
			dy += ny * p.noise * dt;
			dz += nz * p.noise * dt;
		}
		if (p.collide && dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz > 1.0e-10) {
			Vec3 from = new Vec3(p.x, p.y, p.z);
			Vec3 to = new Vec3(p.x + dx, p.y + dy, p.z + dz);
			BlockHitResult hit = level.clip(new ClipContext(from, to, ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER, ClipContext.Fluid.NONE, CollisionContext.empty()));
			if (hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS) {
				if (hit.isInside()) {
					p.x = to.x;
					p.y = to.y;
					p.z = to.z;
					return;
				}
				Vec3 n = Vec3.atLowerCornerOf(hit.getDirection().getUnitVec3i());
				Vec3 at = hit.getLocation().add(n.scale(0.004));
				p.x = at.x;
				p.y = at.y;
				p.z = at.z;
				double vn = p.vx * n.x + p.vy * n.y + p.vz * n.z;
				if (vn < 0) {
					p.vx -= n.x * vn * (1.0 + p.bounce);
					p.vy -= n.y * vn * (1.0 + p.bounce);
					p.vz -= n.z * vn * (1.0 + p.bounce);
					p.vx *= p.keep;
					p.vy *= p.keep;
					p.vz *= p.keep;
				}
				return;
			}
		}
		p.x += dx;
		p.y += dy;
		p.z += dz;
	}

	public static void render(LevelRenderContext context) {
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		CameraRenderState camera = context.levelState().cameraRenderState;
		camPos = camera.pos;
		camRight = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(1, 0, 0)));
		camUp = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 1, 0)));
		camFwd = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 0, -1)));
		firstPerson = camera.isFirstPerson;
		double worldTan = Math.tan(Math.toRadians(mc.gameRenderer.mainCamera().getFov() * 0.5));
		handFit = Viewmodel.FOV_FIT * worldTan / Math.tan(Math.toRadians(camera.hudFov * 0.5));
		fovScale = worldTan / Math.tan(Math.toRadians(40.0));

		ClientLevel level = mc.level;
		if (PARTICLES.isEmpty() || level == null) {
			return;
		}
		float dt = CameraFx.frameDt();
		List<Particle> visible = new ArrayList<>(PARTICLES.size());
		for (Iterator<Particle> it = PARTICLES.iterator(); it.hasNext();) {
			Particle p = it.next();
			if (dt > 0f) {
				if (p.delay > 0f) {
					p.delay -= dt;
					continue;
				}
				p.age += dt;
				if (p.age >= p.life) {
					it.remove();
					continue;
				}
				step(level, p, dt);
				p.rot += p.rotVel * dt;
			} else if (p.delay > 0f) {
				continue;
			}
			p.drawSize = p.size * curve(p.curve, p.age / p.life);
			if (p.drawSize <= 1.0e-4f) {
				continue;
			}
			double rx = p.x - camPos.x;
			double ry = p.y - camPos.y;
			double rz = p.z - camPos.z;
			p.camDist = rx * camFwd.x + ry * camFwd.y + rz * camFwd.z;
			if (p.camDist < -p.drawSize || p.camDist > 256.0) {
				continue;
			}
			visible.add(p);
		}
		if (visible.isEmpty()) {
			return;
		}
		visible.sort((a, b) -> Double.compare(b.camDist, a.camDist));
		Vec3 cam = camPos;
		Vec3 right = camRight;
		Vec3 up = camUp;
		context.submitNodeCollector().submitCustomGeometry(context.poseStack(), RenderTypes.debugQuads(), (pose, buffer) -> {
			for (Particle p : visible) {
				Vec3 center = new Vec3(p.x - cam.x, p.y - cam.y, p.z - cam.z);
				switch (p.shape) {
					case GLOW -> {
						double pull = p.hot ? Math.max(0.3, 1.0 - HOT_PULL / Math.max(center.length(), 1.0e-3)) : 1.0;
						Draw.glow(pose, buffer, center.scale(pull), right, up, p.drawSize * pull, p.color);
					}
					case SQUARE -> {
						double c = Math.cos(p.rot) * p.drawSize * 0.5;
						double s = Math.sin(p.rot) * p.drawSize * 0.5;
						Draw.quad(pose, buffer, center, right.scale(c).add(up.scale(s)), up.scale(c).subtract(right.scale(s)), p.color);
					}
					default -> Draw.circle(pose, buffer, center, right, up, p.drawSize, p.color, p.drawSize > 0.4f ? 24 : 12);
				}
			}
		});
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/GrappleController.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.Map;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.item.GrapplerItem;
import karlson.net.Payloads;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.networking.v1.ClientPlayNetworking;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.level.LevelRenderContext;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.rendertype.RenderTypes;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.CameraRenderState;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.ProjectileUtil;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ClipContext;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.CollisionContext;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public final class GrappleController {
	public static final GrappleController INSTANCE = new GrappleController();

	private static final int ROPE_POINTS = 100;
	private static final int ASSIST_RINGS = 10;
	private static final int ASSIST_RAYS = 24;
	private static final double ASSIST_STEP = 0.035;
	private static final int ROPE_COLOR = Draw.argb(255, 14, 14, 16);

	private static final class Rope {
		Vec3 point;
		Vec3 end;
		float offset = 2f;
		float offsetVel;

		Rope(Vec3 point, Vec3 start) {
			this.point = point;
			this.end = start;
		}
	}

	private boolean grappling;
	private Vec3 point = Vec3.ZERO;
	private BlockPos anchor = BlockPos.ZERO;
	private Entity anchorEntity;
	private Vec3 anchorOff = Vec3.ZERO;
	private double maxDist;
	private double minDistance;
	private Rope rope;
	private Vec3 nearestPoint;
	private Vec3 aimPos;
	private float aimScale;
	private boolean wasDown;
	private boolean retracting;
	private final Map<Integer, Rope> remote = new HashMap<>();

	private GrappleController() {
	}

	public boolean isGrappling() {
		return grappling;
	}

	public boolean isRetracting() {
		return retracting;
	}

	public Vec3 point() {
		return point;
	}

	public Entity anchorEntity() {
		return grappling ? anchorEntity : null;
	}

	private static BlockHitResult cast(Level level, Vec3 from, Vec3 dir) {
		Vec3 to = from.add(dir.normalize().scale(KarlsonConst.GRAPPLE_RANGE));
		return level.clip(new ClipContext(from, to, ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER, ClipContext.Fluid.NONE, CollisionContext.empty()));
	}

	public void tick(Minecraft mc, LocalPlayer player, boolean attackDown, boolean useDown) {
		boolean holding = player.getMainHandItem().getItem() instanceof GrapplerItem;
		if (grappling) {
			Level level = player.level();
			boolean anchorGone;
			if (anchorEntity != null) {
				anchorGone = !anchorEntity.isAlive() || anchorEntity.level() != level;
				if (!anchorGone) {
					point = anchorEntity.position().add(anchorOff);
					if (rope != null) {
						rope.point = point;
					}
				}
			} else {
				anchorGone = level.getBlockState(anchor).getCollisionShape(level, anchor).isEmpty();
			}
			if (!holding || !attackDown || !player.isAlive() || anchorGone) {
				stop();
			}
		}
		retracting = grappling && useDown;
		if (!holding) {
			nearestPoint = null;
			aimPos = null;
			wasDown = attackDown;
			return;
		}
		if (!grappling) {
			onAim(player);
			if (attackDown && !wasDown) {
				start(player);
			}
		}
		wasDown = attackDown;
	}

	private void onAim(LocalPlayer player) {
		Level level = player.level();
		Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
		Vec3 forward = player.getLookAngle();
		if (cast(level, eye, forward).getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS) {
			nearestPoint = null;
			return;
		}
		Vec3 right = forward.cross(new Vec3(0, 1, 0));
		right = right.lengthSqr() < 1.0e-6 ? new Vec3(1, 0, 0) : right.normalize();
		Vec3 up = right.cross(forward);
		Vec3 best = null;
		double bestDist = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		for (int ring = 1; ring < ASSIST_RINGS && best == null; ring++) {
			for (int ray = 0; ray < ASSIST_RAYS; ray++) {
				double angle = Math.PI * 2.0 / ASSIST_RAYS * ray;
				Vec3 offset = right.scale(Math.cos(angle)).add(up.scale(Math.sin(angle))).scale(ASSIST_STEP * ring);
				BlockHitResult hit = cast(level, eye, forward.add(offset));
				if (hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS) {
					double dist = hit.getLocation().distanceToSqr(eye);
					if (dist < bestDist) {
						bestDist = dist;
						best = hit.getLocation();
					}
				}
			}
		}
		nearestPoint = best;
	}

	private void start(LocalPlayer player) {
		Level level = player.level();
		Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
		BlockHitResult hit = cast(level, eye, player.getLookAngle());
		Vec3 target;
		double reach = hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS ? hit.getLocation().distanceTo(eye) : KarlsonConst.GRAPPLE_RANGE;
		Vec3 end = eye.add(player.getLookAngle().scale(reach));
		EntityHitResult mob = ProjectileUtil.getEntityHitResult(player, eye, end, new AABB(eye, end).inflate(1.0),
				entity -> entity instanceof LivingEntity && entity.isAlive() && !entity.isSpectator(), reach * reach);
		anchorEntity = null;
		if (mob != null) {
			target = mob.getLocation();
			anchorEntity = mob.getEntity();
			anchorOff = target.subtract(anchorEntity.position());
		} else if (hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS) {
			target = hit.getLocation();
			anchor = hit.getBlockPos();
		} else if (nearestPoint != null) {
			target = nearestPoint;
			anchor = BlockPos.containing(target.add(target.subtract(eye).normalize().scale(0.01)));
		} else {
			return;
		}
		grappling = true;
		point = target;
		Vec3 center = player.position().add(0, player.getBbHeight() * 0.5, 0);
		Vec3 delta = point.subtract(center);
		double dist = Math.sqrt((delta.x * delta.x + delta.z * delta.z) * 0.5 + delta.y * delta.y);
		maxDist = dist * KarlsonConst.GRAPPLE_MAX_FRAC;
		minDistance = dist * KarlsonConst.GRAPPLE_MIN_FRAC;
		rope = new Rope(point, eye.add(player.getLookAngle().scale(0.6)));
		aimPos = null;
		ClientPlayNetworking.send(new Payloads.Grapple(true, point));
	}

	public void stop() {
		if (grappling) {
			grappling = false;
			retracting = false;
			anchorEntity = null;
			rope = null;
			if (Minecraft.getInstance().getConnection() != null) {
				ClientPlayNetworking.send(new Payloads.Grapple(false, point));
			}
		}
	}

	public void clear() {
		grappling = false;
		anchorEntity = null;
		retracting = false;
		rope = null;
		nearestPoint = null;
		aimPos = null;
		remote.clear();
	}

	public Vec3 springDv(Vec3 center, Vec3 velocity, double dt) {
		return springDv(center, velocity, dt, false);
	}

	public Vec3 springDv(Vec3 center, Vec3 velocity, double dt, boolean pullOnly) {
		if (!grappling) {
			return Vec3.ZERO;
		}
		Vec3 delta = point.subtract(center);
		double dist = delta.length();
		if (dist < 1.0e-6) {
			return Vec3.ZERO;
		}
		Vec3 n = delta.scale(1.0 / dist);
		double vn = velocity.dot(n);
		double reel = 0;
		if (retracting) {
			minDistance = Math.min(minDistance, KarlsonConst.REEL_MIN);
			maxDist = Math.max(Math.min(maxDist, dist), minDistance);
			if (dist > KarlsonConst.REEL_MIN) {
				reel = Math.max(0.0, Math.min(KarlsonConst.REEL_ACCEL * dt, KarlsonConst.REEL_SPEED - vn));
			}
			pullOnly = true;
		}
		double error;
		if (dist > maxDist) {
			error = dist - maxDist;
		} else if (dist < minDistance) {
			error = dist - minDistance;
		} else {
			return n.scale(reel);
		}
		double w = KarlsonConst.GRAPPLE_INV_MASS;
		double k = KarlsonConst.GRAPPLE_SPRING;
		double c = KarlsonConst.GRAPPLE_DAMPER;
		double dvn = dt * w * (k * error - c * vn) / (1.0 + dt * w * (c + dt * k));
		if (pullOnly && dvn * error < 0) {
			dvn = 0;
		}
		return n.scale(dvn + reel);
	}

	public void remote(int entityId, boolean active, Vec3 target) {
		if (!active) {
			remote.remove(entityId);
			return;
		}
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		Entity entity = mc.level == null ? null : mc.level.getEntity(entityId);
		remote.put(entityId, new Rope(target, entity == null ? target : entity.getEyePosition()));
	}

	public void render(LevelRenderContext context) {
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		LocalPlayer player = mc.player;
		if (player == null || mc.level == null) {
			return;
		}
		CameraRenderState camera = context.levelState().cameraRenderState;
		Vec3 camPos = camera.pos;
		Vec3 camRight = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(1, 0, 0)));
		Vec3 camUp = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 1, 0)));
		Vec3 camFwd = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 0, -1)));
		float partial = camera.cameraEntityPartialTicks;
		float dt = CameraFx.frameDt();
		PoseStack poseStack = context.poseStack();

		if (rope != null) {
			Vec3 tip;
			if (mc.options.getCameraType().isFirstPerson()) {
				Vector3f t = Viewmodel.tipViewPosition();
				double fit = Viewmodel.FOV_FIT * Math.tan(Math.toRadians(mc.gameRenderer.mainCamera().getFov() * 0.5)) / Math.tan(Math.toRadians(camera.hudFov * 0.5));
				tip = camPos.add(camRight.scale(t.x * fit)).add(camUp.scale(t.y * fit)).add(camFwd.scale(-t.z));
			} else {
				tip = handPos(player, partial);
			}
			drawRope(context, poseStack, rope, tip, camPos, dt);
		}
		for (Iterator<Map.Entry<Integer, Rope>> it = remote.entrySet().iterator(); it.hasNext();) {
			Map.Entry<Integer, Rope> entry = it.next();
			Entity entity = mc.level.getEntity(entry.getKey());
			if (!(entity instanceof Player other) || !other.isAlive() || !(other.getMainHandItem().getItem() instanceof GrapplerItem)) {
				it.remove();
				continue;
			}
			drawRope(context, poseStack, entry.getValue(), handPos(other, partial), camPos, dt);
		}

		if (!grappling && nearestPoint != null && mc.options.getCameraType().isFirstPerson()) {
			if (aimPos == null) {
				aimPos = nearestPoint;
				aimScale = 0;
			}
			aimPos = aimPos.lerp(nearestPoint, Math.min(1.0, dt / 0.05));
			float targetScale = (float) (0.025 * nearestPoint.distanceTo(camPos));
			aimScale += (targetScale - aimScale) * Math.min(1f, dt / 0.1f);
			Vec3 center = aimPos.subtract(camPos);
			Vec3 lifted = center.scale(1.0 - Math.min(0.5, 0.06 / Math.max(center.length(), 0.01)));
			float size = aimScale;
			context.submitNodeCollector().submitCustomGeometry(poseStack, RenderTypes.debugQuads(), (pose, buffer) -> {
				Draw.disc(pose, buffer, lifted, camRight, camUp, size, Draw.argb(255, 207, 0, 0));
			});
		} else if (nearestPoint == null) {
			aimPos = null;
		}
	}

	private static Vec3 handPos(Player player, float partial) {
		Vec3 pos = player.getPosition(partial);
		float yaw = Mth.lerp(partial, player.yBodyRotO, player.yBodyRot) * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD;
		Vec3 forward = new Vec3(-Mth.sin(yaw), 0, Mth.cos(yaw));
		Vec3 right = new Vec3(-Mth.cos(yaw), 0, -Mth.sin(yaw));
		return pos.add(0, player.getBbHeight() * 0.62, 0).add(right.scale(0.38)).add(forward.scale(0.45));
	}

	private void drawRope(LevelRenderContext context, PoseStack poseStack, Rope rope, Vec3 tip, Vec3 camPos, float dt) {
		rope.end = rope.end.lerp(rope.point, Math.min(1.0, dt * 15.0));
		float[] damped = CameraFx.smoothDamp(rope.offset, 0f, rope.offsetVel, 0.1f, dt);
		rope.offset = damped[0];
		rope.offsetVel = damped[1];

		Vec3 start = tip;
		Vec3 end = rope.end;
		Vec3 span = end.subtract(start);
		double length = span.length();
		if (length < 1.0e-3) {
			return;
		}
		Vec3 dir = span.scale(1.0 / length);
		Vec3 perp = new Vec3(-dir.y, dir.x, 0);
		double offset = rope.offset;
		double waveLength = length / KarlsonConst.SCALE;
		double spread = Math.cos(offset * Math.PI / 2.0);

		Vec3[] points = new Vec3[ROPE_POINTS];
		points[0] = start.subtract(camPos);
		points[ROPE_POINTS - 1] = end.subtract(camPos);
		for (int i = 1; i < ROPE_POINTS - 1; i++) {
			double t = (double) i / ROPE_POINTS;
			double phase = t * offset;
			double wave = (Math.sin(phase * waveLength) - 0.5) * (phase * 2.0);
			double sag = Math.sin(t * Math.PI);
			Vec3 p = start.add(span.scale(t))
					.add(perp.scale(spread * wave * KarlsonConst.SCALE))
					.add(0, -offset * sag * KarlsonConst.SCALE, 0);
			points[i] = p.subtract(camPos);
		}
		context.submitNodeCollector().submitCustomGeometry(poseStack, RenderTypes.debugQuads(), (pose, buffer) -> {
			for (int i = 0; i < ROPE_POINTS - 1; i++) {
				Draw.beam(pose, buffer, points[i], points[i + 1], 0.05, 0.05, ROPE_COLOR, ROPE_COLOR);
			}
		});
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/GunDropState.java ====
package karlson.client;

public interface GunDropState {
	GunMesh karlson$gun();

	void karlson$setGun(GunMesh mesh);

	boolean karlson$resting();

	void karlson$setResting(boolean resting);
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/GunDrops.java ====
package karlson.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.SubmitNodeCollector;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.ItemEntityRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.rendertype.RenderTypes;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.CameraRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.texture.OverlayTexture;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public final class GunDrops {
	private GunDrops() {
	}

	private static final float SCALE = 1.5f;
	private static final float OUTLINE_PIXELS = 3.5f;
	private static final int DIRECTIONS = 8;
	private static final double OUTLINE_RANGE_SQ = 48.0 * 48.0;
	private static final int WHITE = 0xFFFFFFFF;
	private static final float TUMBLE = 0.45f;

	public static void submit(ItemEntityRenderState state, PoseStack poseStack, SubmitNodeCollector collector, CameraRenderState camera) {
		GunDropState drop = (GunDropState) state;
		GunMesh mesh = drop.karlson$gun();
		Vector3f min = mesh.min();
		Vector3f max = mesh.max();
		float thickX = max.x - min.x;
		float thickZ = max.z - min.z;
		boolean thinX = thickX < thickZ;
		float thickness = Math.min(thickX, thickZ) * SCALE;

		poseStack.pushPose();
		if (drop.karlson$resting()) {
			poseStack.translate(0f, thickness * 0.5f + 0.02f, 0f);
			poseStack.rotate(Axis.YP, state.bobOffset);
		} else {
			poseStack.translate(0f, 0.125f, 0f);
			poseStack.rotate(Axis.YP, state.bobOffset);
			poseStack.rotate(Axis.XP, state.ageInTicks * TUMBLE + state.bobOffset);
		}
		poseStack.rotateDegrees(thinX ? Axis.ZP : Axis.XP, thinX ? 90f : -90f);
		poseStack.scale(SCALE, SCALE, SCALE);
		poseStack.translate(-(min.x + max.x) * 0.5f, -(min.y + max.y) * 0.5f, -(min.z + max.z) * 0.5f);

		int light = state.lightCoords;
		collector.submitCustomGeometry(poseStack, GunSpecialRenderer.RENDER_TYPE, (pose, buffer) -> mesh.emit(pose, buffer, light, OverlayTexture.NO_OVERLAY));
		if (state.distanceToCameraSq < OUTLINE_RANGE_SQ) {
			Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
			Vector3f right = camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(1, 0, 0));
			Vector3f up = camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 1, 0));
			Vector3f forward = camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 0, -1));
			float height = Math.max(1, mc.getWindow().getHeight());
			float pixel = (float) (2.0 * Math.tan(Math.toRadians(mc.gameRenderer.mainCamera().getFov() * 0.5)) / height);
			float radius = Math.max(1.5f, OUTLINE_PIXELS * height / 1080f) * pixel;
			collector.submitCustomGeometry(poseStack, RenderTypes.debugQuads(), (pose, buffer) -> outline(mesh, pose.pose(), buffer, right, up, forward, radius));
		}
		poseStack.popPose();
	}

	private static void outline(GunMesh mesh, Matrix4f matrix, VertexConsumer buffer, Vector3f right, Vector3f up, Vector3f forward, float radius) {
		Vector3f min = mesh.min();
		Vector3f max = mesh.max();
		float bottom = Float.MAX_VALUE;
		float top = -Float.MAX_VALUE;
		float far = 0f;
		Vector3f p = new Vector3f();
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			matrix.transformPosition((i & 1) == 0 ? min.x : max.x, (i & 2) == 0 ? min.y : max.y, (i & 4) == 0 ? min.z : max.z, p);
			bottom = Math.min(bottom, p.y);
			top = Math.max(top, p.y);
			far = Math.max(far, p.length());
		}
		far += 0.05f;
		float plane = bottom > 0f ? top : top < 0f ? bottom : Float.NaN;

		float[] dx = new float[DIRECTIONS * 3];
		for (int k = 0; k < DIRECTIONS; k++) {
			float c = (float) Math.cos(Math.PI * 2.0 * k / DIRECTIONS) * radius;
			float s = (float) Math.sin(Math.PI * 2.0 * k / DIRECTIONS) * radius;
			dx[k * 3] = right.x * c + up.x * s;
			dx[k * 3 + 1] = right.y * c + up.y * s;
			dx[k * 3 + 2] = right.z * c + up.z * s;
		}

		int count = mesh.vertexCount();
		float[] tri = new float[9];
		float[] depth = new float[3];
		for (int i = 0; i < count; i += 3) {
			boolean visible = true;
			for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
				mesh.position(i + j, p);
				matrix.transformPosition(p);
				tri[j * 3] = p.x;
				tri[j * 3 + 1] = p.y;
				tri[j * 3 + 2] = p.z;
				depth[j] = p.dot(forward);
				if (depth[j] < 0.05f) {
					visible = false;
				}
			}
			if (!visible) {
				continue;
			}
			for (int k = 0; k < DIRECTIONS; k++) {
				for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
					int v = Math.min(j, 2);
					float x = tri[v * 3] + dx[k * 3] * depth[v];
					float y = tri[v * 3 + 1] + dx[k * 3 + 1] * depth[v];
					float z = tri[v * 3 + 2] + dx[k * 3 + 2] * depth[v];
					float length = (float) Math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z);
					float t = far / length;
					if (!Float.isNaN(plane) && y * plane > 1.0e-6f) {
						t = Math.min(t, plane / y);
					}
					t = Math.max(t, 1f);
					buffer.addVertex(x * t, y * t, z * t).setColor(WHITE);
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/GunMesh.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import org.joml.Vector3f;
import org.joml.Vector3fc;

public final class GunMesh {
	private static final Map<String, GunMesh> CACHE = new HashMap<>();
	private static final int STRIDE = 9;

	private final float[] data;
	private final int vertices;
	private final Vector3f min = new Vector3f(Float.MAX_VALUE);
	private final Vector3f max = new Vector3f(-Float.MAX_VALUE);

	private GunMesh(float[] data, int vertices) {
		this.data = data;
		this.vertices = vertices;
		for (int i = 0; i < vertices; i++) {
			Vector3f p = new Vector3f(data[i * STRIDE], data[i * STRIDE + 1], data[i * STRIDE + 2]);
			min.min(p);
			max.max(p);
		}
	}

	public static synchronized GunMesh get(String name) {
		return CACHE.computeIfAbsent(name, GunMesh::load);
	}

	private static GunMesh load(String name) {
		String path = "/assets/karlson/gunmesh/" + name + ".txt";
		try (InputStream in = GunMesh.class.getResourceAsStream(path)) {
			if (in == null) {
				throw new IllegalStateException("Missing gun mesh " + path);
			}
			BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
			int vertices = Integer.parseInt(reader.readLine().trim()) * 3;
			float[] data = new float[vertices * STRIDE];
			for (int i = 0; i < vertices; i++) {
				String[] parts = reader.readLine().trim().split(" ");
				for (int j = 0; j < STRIDE; j++) {
					data[i * STRIDE + j] = Float.parseFloat(parts[j]);
				}
			}
			return new GunMesh(data, vertices);
		} catch (IOException e) {
			throw new IllegalStateException("Could not read gun mesh " + path, e);
		}
	}

	public void emit(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, int light, int overlay) {
		for (int i = 0; i < vertices; i += 3) {
			vertex(pose, buffer, i, light, overlay);
			vertex(pose, buffer, i + 1, light, overlay);
			vertex(pose, buffer, i + 2, light, overlay);
			vertex(pose, buffer, i + 2, light, overlay);
		}
	}

	private void vertex(PoseStack.Pose pose, VertexConsumer buffer, int index, int light, int overlay) {
		int o = index * STRIDE;
		buffer.addVertex(pose, data[o], data[o + 1], data[o + 2])
				.setColor((int) data[o + 6], (int) data[o + 7], (int) data[o + 8], 255)
				.setUv(0.5f, 0.5f)
				.setOverlay(overlay)
				.setLight(light)
				.setNormal(pose, data[o + 3], data[o + 4], data[o + 5]);
	}

	public Vector3f min() {
		return min;
	}

	public Vector3f max() {
		return max;
	}

	public int vertexCount() {
		return vertices;
	}

	public void position(int index, Vector3f out) {
		out.set(data[index * STRIDE], data[index * STRIDE + 1], data[index * STRIDE + 2]);
	}

	public void extents(Consumer<Vector3fc> output, float offset) {
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			output.accept(new Vector3f(
					((i & 1) == 0 ? min.x : max.x) + offset,
					((i & 2) == 0 ? min.y : max.y) + offset,
					((i & 4) == 0 ? min.z : max.z) + offset));
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/GunSpecialRenderer.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.util.function.Consumer;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import karlson.Karlson;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.SubmitNodeCollector;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.rendertype.RenderType;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.rendertype.RenderTypes;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.special.NoDataSpecialModelRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.special.SpecialModelRenderer;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;
import org.joml.Vector3fc;

public class GunSpecialRenderer implements NoDataSpecialModelRenderer {
	private static final Identifier WHITE = Karlson.id("textures/entity/white.png");
	static final RenderType RENDER_TYPE = RenderTypes.entityCutout(WHITE);

	private final GunMesh mesh;

	public GunSpecialRenderer(GunMesh mesh) {
		this.mesh = mesh;
	}

	@Override
	public void submit(PoseStack poseStack, SubmitNodeCollector submitNodeCollector, int lightCoords, int overlayCoords, boolean hasFoil, int outlineColor) {
		poseStack.pushPose();
		poseStack.translate(0.5f, 0.5f, 0.5f);
		submitNodeCollector.submitCustomGeometry(poseStack, RENDER_TYPE, (pose, buffer) -> mesh.emit(pose, buffer, lightCoords, overlayCoords));
		poseStack.popPose();
	}

	@Override
	public void getExtents(Consumer<Vector3fc> output) {
		mesh.extents(output, 0.5f);
	}

	public record Unbaked(String gun) implements NoDataSpecialModelRenderer.Unbaked {
		public static final MapCodec<Unbaked> MAP_CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.mapCodec(
				i -> i.group(Codec.STRING.fieldOf("gun").forGetter(Unbaked::gun)).apply(i, Unbaked::new));

		@Override
		public MapCodec<Unbaked> type() {
			return MAP_CODEC;
		}

		@Override
		public SpecialModelRenderer<Void> bake(SpecialModelRenderer.BakingContext context) {
			return new GunSpecialRenderer(GunMesh.get(gun));
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/KarlsonClient.java ====
package karlson.client;

import karlson.Karlson;
import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.KarlsonEntities;
import karlson.KarlsonSounds;
import karlson.item.GrapplerItem;
import karlson.item.GunItem;
import karlson.net.Payloads;
import net.fabricmc.api.ClientModInitializer;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.event.lifecycle.v1.ClientTickEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.networking.v1.ClientPlayConnectionEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.networking.v1.ClientPlayNetworking;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.level.LevelRenderEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.client.player.ClientPreAttackCallback;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.player.UseBlockCallback;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.player.UseItemCallback;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.EntityRenderers;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.special.SpecialModelRenderers;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionResult;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.ProjectileUtil;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ClipContext;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public class KarlsonClient implements ClientModInitializer {
	private static final double AIM_RANGE = 100.0;

	public static boolean serverSupported;
	public static boolean karlsonMode;

	@Override
	public void onInitializeClient() {
		Karlson.clientKarlson = player -> player instanceof LocalPlayer && karlsonMode;
		SpecialModelRenderers.ID_MAPPER.put(Karlson.id("gun"), GunSpecialRenderer.Unbaked.MAP_CODEC);
		EntityRenderers.register(KarlsonEntities.ENEMY, EnemyRenderer::new);
		KarlsonMusic.init();

		ClientPlayConnectionEvents.JOIN.register((listener, sender, client) -> {
			serverSupported = ClientPlayNetworking.canSend(Payloads.Mode.TYPE);
			clearWorldState();
			karlsonMode = false;
			if (serverSupported) {
				sender.sendPacket(new Payloads.Mode(false));
			}
		});
		ClientPlayConnectionEvents.DISCONNECT.register((listener, client) -> {
			serverSupported = false;
			karlsonMode = false;
			clearWorldState();
		});

		ClientPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.BulletSpawn.TYPE, (pkt, context) -> ClientBullets.spawn(pkt));
		ClientPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.BulletEnd.TYPE, (pkt, context) -> {
			int owner = ClientBullets.end(pkt);
			LocalPlayer player = context.client().player;
			if (pkt.kind() == 1 && player != null && owner == player.getId()) {
				player.level().playLocalSound(player, KarlsonSounds.HITMARKER, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 1.0f);
			}
		});
		ClientPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.Blast.TYPE, (pkt, context) -> blast(context.client(), pkt));
		ClientPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.GrappleSync.TYPE,
				(pkt, context) -> GrappleController.INSTANCE.remote(pkt.entityId(), pkt.active(), pkt.point()));

		ClientTickEvents.END_CLIENT_TICK.register(KarlsonClient::tick);
		ClientPreAttackCallback.EVENT.register((client, player, clickCount) -> {
			if (isWeapon(player.getMainHandItem().getItem())) {
				return true;
			}
			if (Ragdolls.isGrabbing()) {
				return true;
			}
			return clickCount > 0 && player.getMainHandItem().isEmpty() && Ragdolls.tryGrab(client, player);
		});
		UseItemCallback.EVENT.register((player, level, hand) -> level.isClientSide() && GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling()
				? InteractionResult.FAIL : InteractionResult.PASS);
		UseBlockCallback.EVENT.register((player, level, hand, hit) -> level.isClientSide() && GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling()
				? InteractionResult.FAIL : InteractionResult.PASS);
		LevelRenderEvents.COLLECT_SUBMITS.register(context -> {
			Ragdolls.render(context);
			GrappleController.INSTANCE.render(context);
			Fx.render(context);
			ClientBullets.render(context);
			SpeedLines.render(context);
		});
	}

	private static void clearWorldState() {
		GrappleController.INSTANCE.clear();
		ClientBullets.clear();
		Fx.clear();
		Ragdolls.clear();
		SpeedLines.clear();
		KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.reset();
	}

	public static boolean karlsonView() {
		return karlsonMode || Viewmodel.isActive();
	}

	public static boolean isWeapon(Item item) {
		return item instanceof GunItem || item instanceof GrapplerItem;
	}

	private static void tick(Minecraft mc) {
		LocalPlayer player = mc.player;
		if (player == null || mc.level == null) {
			return;
		}
		boolean mode = serverSupported;
		if (mode != karlsonMode) {
			karlsonMode = mode;
			ClientPlayNetworking.send(new Payloads.Mode(mode));
			player.refreshDimensions();
		}
		if (mc.isPaused()) {
			return;
		}
		if (!KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.shouldControl(player)) {
			KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.active = false;
		}

		boolean attackDown = mc.gui.screen() == null && mc.options.keyAttack.isDown()
				&& player.isAlive() && !player.isSpectator();
		if (serverSupported) {
			GrappleController.INSTANCE.tick(mc, player, attackDown, mc.gui.screen() == null && mc.options.keyUse.isDown());
			ItemStack stack = player.getMainHandItem();
			if (attackDown && stack.getItem() instanceof GunItem gun && !player.getCooldowns().isOnCooldown(stack)) {
				shoot(player, stack, gun.stats);
			}
		}
		if (Ragdolls.isGrabbing() && (!attackDown || !player.getMainHandItem().isEmpty())) {
			Ragdolls.release();
		}
		Ragdolls.tick(mc);
		CameraFx.tick();
	}

	private static void shoot(LocalPlayer player, ItemStack stack, GunItem.Stats stats) {
		Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
		Vec3 look = player.getLookAngle();
		Vec3 far = eye.add(look.scale(AIM_RANGE));
		BlockHitResult blockHit = player.level().clip(new ClipContext(eye, far, ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER, ClipContext.Fluid.NONE, player));
		Vec3 target = blockHit.getType() == HitResult.Type.MISS ? far : blockHit.getLocation();
		EntityHitResult entHit = ProjectileUtil.getEntityHitResult(player, eye, target, new AABB(eye, target).inflate(1.0),
				e -> !e.isSpectator() && e.isPickable(), eye.distanceToSqr(target));
		if (entHit != null) {
			target = entHit.getLocation();
		}
		ClientPlayNetworking.send(new Payloads.Shoot(target));
		player.getCooldowns().addCooldown(stack, stats.cooldownTicks());

		if (stats.boostRecoil() > 0) {
			player.setDeltaMovement(player.getDeltaMovement().add(look.scale(-stats.boostDv() / 20.0)));
		}
		Viewmodel.shoot((float) stats.recoil());
	}

	private static void blast(Minecraft mc, Payloads.Blast pkt) {
		Fx.explosion(pkt.pos());
		Ragdolls.blast(pkt.pos());
		LocalPlayer player = mc.player;
		if (player == null) {
			return;
		}
		Vec3 impulse = pkt.impulse();
		if (impulse.lengthSqr() > 0 && !player.getAbilities().flying && !player.isPassenger()) {
			player.setDeltaMovement(player.getDeltaMovement().add(impulse.scale(1.0 / 20.0)));
			KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.explode();
		}
		double dist = player.position().distanceTo(pkt.pos()) / KarlsonConst.SCALE;
		float ratio = (float) (10.0 / Math.max(dist, 0.5));
		if (ratio < 0.1f) {
			ratio = 0f;
		}
		CameraFx.shakeOnce(20f * ratio, 2f, 0.4f, 0.5f);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/KarlsonMovement.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.KarlsonSounds;
import karlson.mixin.client.EntityAccessor;
import karlson.net.Payloads;
import karlson.server.KarlsonServer;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.networking.v1.ClientPlayNetworking;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.MoverType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Enemy;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public final class KarlsonMovement {
	public static final KarlsonMovement INSTANCE = new KarlsonMovement();

	private static final int SUBSTEPS = 3;
	private static final double DT = 0.05 / SUBSTEPS;
	private static final double COUNTER_RATE = (1.0 - Math.pow(1.0 - KarlsonConst.COUNTER_FACTOR_PER_STEP, DT / KarlsonConst.FIXED_DT)) / DT;
	private static final Vec3 UP = new Vec3(0, 1, 0);

	public boolean enabled = true;

	public boolean grounded;
	public boolean wallRunning;
	public boolean crouching;
	public float wallRunRotation;
	public Vec3 velocity = Vec3.ZERO;
	public boolean active;

	private boolean onWall;
	private boolean readyToJump = true;
	private boolean readyToWallrun = true;
	private boolean cancelling;
	private boolean wasCrouchKey;
	private double sinceFloor = 1.0;
	private double sinceWall = 1.0;
	private double jumpTimer;
	private double wallrunReadyTimer;
	private double cancelTimer;
	private double explodedTimer;
	private double stepDistance;
	private Vec3 wallNorm = UP;
	private long lastTravelTick = Long.MIN_VALUE;
	private final Map<Integer, Long> slamCooldowns = new HashMap<>();

	private KarlsonMovement() {
	}

	public boolean shouldControl(LocalPlayer player) {
		return enabled
				&& KarlsonClient.serverSupported
				&& player.isAlive()
				&& !player.isSpectator()
				&& !player.getAbilities().flying
				&& !player.isPassenger()
				&& !player.isFallFlying()
				&& !player.isInLiquid()
				&& !player.onClimbable()
				&& !player.isSleeping()
				&& !player.noPhysics;
	}

	public void explode() {
		explodedTimer = 0.1;
	}

	public void reset() {
		active = false;
		grounded = false;
		wallRunning = false;
		onWall = false;
		wallRunRotation = 0;
		sinceFloor = 1.0;
		sinceWall = 1.0;
		cancelling = false;
	}

	private static void play(LocalPlayer player, SoundEvent sound, float volume, float pitch) {
		player.level().playLocalSound(player, sound, SoundSource.PLAYERS, volume, pitch);
	}

	private static void slideSounds(LocalPlayer player) {
		play(player, KarlsonSounds.SLIDE_START, 1.0f, 1.0f);
		play(player, KarlsonSounds.SLIDE, 1.0f, 0.8f);
	}

	public void travel(LocalPlayer player) {
		long tick = player.level().getGameTime();
		if (!active || tick - lastTravelTick > 2) {
			reset();
			grounded = player.onGround();
			sinceFloor = grounded ? 0 : 1.0;
			wasCrouchKey = player.input.keyPresses.shift();
		}
		active = true;
		lastTravelTick = tick;

		Input keys = player.input.keyPresses;
		float x = (keys.right() ? 1 : 0) - (keys.left() ? 1 : 0);
		float y = (keys.forward() ? 1 : 0) - (keys.backward() ? 1 : 0);
		boolean jumping = keys.jump();
		boolean crouchKey = keys.shift();

		float yaw = player.getYRot() * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD;
		Vec3 forward = new Vec3(-Mth.sin(yaw), 0, Mth.cos(yaw));
		Vec3 right = new Vec3(-Mth.cos(yaw), 0, -Mth.sin(yaw));

		Vec3 v = player.getDeltaMovement().scale(20.0);

		crouching = crouchKey;
		if (crouchKey && !wasCrouchKey) {
			v = startCrouch(player, v, forward);
		}
		wasCrouchKey = crouchKey;

		for (int i = 0; i < SUBSTEPS; i++) {
			v = step(player, v, x, y, jumping, forward, right, i == SUBSTEPS - 1);
		}

		v = updateWallRun(player, v, x, y, forward, right);
		slamEnemies(player, v);

		player.setDeltaMovement(v.scale(1.0 / 20.0));
		player.resetFallDistance();
		velocity = v;
	}

	private Vec3 startCrouch(LocalPlayer player, Vec3 v, Vec3 forward) {
		if (v.length() > 0.1 * KarlsonConst.SCALE && grounded) {
			slideSounds(player);
			return v.add(forward.scale(KarlsonConst.SLIDE_BOOST));
		}
		return v;
	}

	private Vec3 step(LocalPlayer player, Vec3 v, float x, float y, boolean jumping, Vec3 forward, Vec3 right, boolean last) {
		Vec3 acc = new Vec3(0, -KarlsonConst.GRAVITY, 0);

		double horizontal = Math.sqrt(v.x * v.x + v.z * v.z);
		double magX = 0;
		double magY = 0;
		if (horizontal > 1.0e-6) {
			double speed = v.length();
			double ux = v.x / horizontal;
			double uz = v.z / horizontal;
			magX = speed * (ux * right.x + uz * right.z);
			magY = speed * (ux * forward.x + uz * forward.z);
		}

		if (!crouching && (grounded || wallRunning)) {
			stepDistance += Math.min(v.length() / KarlsonConst.SCALE, 20.0) * DT / KarlsonConst.FIXED_DT;
			if (stepDistance > 300.0 / 1.2) {
				play(player, KarlsonSounds.FOOTSTEP, 1.0f, 1.0f);
				stepDistance = 0;
			}
		}

		if (grounded && !jumping && explodedTimer <= 0) {
			double grip = groundGrip(player);
			if (crouching) {
				double speed = v.length();
				if (speed > 1.0e-6) {
					double decel = Math.min(KarlsonConst.SLIDE_DECEL * grip, speed / DT);
					acc = acc.add(v.scale(-decel / speed));
				}
			} else {
				double threshold = KarlsonConst.COUNTER_THRESHOLD;
				if ((Math.abs(magX) > threshold && Math.abs(x) < 0.05f) || (magX < -threshold && x > 0) || (magX > threshold && x < 0)) {
					acc = acc.add(right.scale(-magX * COUNTER_RATE * grip));
				}
				if ((Math.abs(magY) > threshold && Math.abs(y) < 0.05f) || (magY < -threshold && y > 0) || (magY > threshold && y < 0)) {
					acc = acc.add(forward.scale(-magY * COUNTER_RATE * grip));
				}
				if (horizontal > KarlsonConst.MAX_SPEED) {
					Vec3 capped = v.normalize().scale(KarlsonConst.MAX_SPEED);
					v = new Vec3(capped.x, v.y, capped.z);
				}
			}
		}

		if (readyToJump && jumping && (grounded || wallRunning)) {
			readyToJump = false;
			jumpTimer = KarlsonConst.JUMP_COOLDOWN;
			double vy = v.y;
			if (vy < KarlsonConst.JUMP_RESET_Y) {
				vy = 0;
			} else if (vy > 0) {
				vy /= 2;
			}
			v = new Vec3(v.x, vy + KarlsonConst.JUMP_DV * 2.0, v.z);
			if (wallRunning) {
				v = v.add(wallNorm.scale(KarlsonConst.JUMP_DV * 3.0));
				wallRunning = false;
			}
			play(player, KarlsonSounds.JUMP, 1.0f, 1.0f);
		}

		if (crouching && grounded && readyToJump) {
			acc = acc.add(0, -KarlsonConst.CROUCH_DOWN_ACCEL, 0);
		} else {
			float ix = x;
			float iy = y;
			if (ix > 0 && magX > KarlsonConst.MAX_SPEED) ix = 0;
			if (ix < 0 && magX < -KarlsonConst.MAX_SPEED) ix = 0;
			if (iy > 0 && magY > KarlsonConst.MAX_SPEED) iy = 0;
			if (iy < 0 && magY < -KarlsonConst.MAX_SPEED) iy = 0;
			double multiplier = 1.0;
			double multiplierV = 1.0;
			if (!grounded) {
				multiplier = 0.5;
				multiplierV = 0.5;
			}
			if (grounded && crouching) {
				multiplierV = 0;
			}
			if (wallRunning) {
				multiplierV = 0.3;
				multiplier = 0.3;
			}
			acc = acc.add(forward.scale(iy * KarlsonConst.MOVE_ACCEL * multiplier * multiplierV));
			acc = acc.add(right.scale(ix * KarlsonConst.MOVE_ACCEL * multiplier));
		}

		if (wallRunning) {
			acc = acc.add(wallNorm.scale(-KarlsonConst.WALLRUN_STICK_ACCEL)).add(0, KarlsonConst.WALLRUN_UP_ACCEL, 0);
		}

		v = v.add(acc.scale(DT));
		Vec3 center = player.position().add(0, player.getBbHeight() * 0.5, 0);
		v = v.add(GrappleController.INSTANCE.springDv(center, v, DT));

		Vec3 want = v.scale(DT);
		Vec3 before = player.position();
		player.setDeltaMovement(v.scale(1.0 / 20.0));
		player.move(MoverType.SELF, want);
		Vec3 moved = player.position().subtract(before);
		Vec3 after = player.getDeltaMovement().scale(20.0);
		if (!last) {
			float factor = ((EntityAccessor) player).karlson$getBlockSpeedFactor();
			if (factor > 0.0f && factor != 1.0f) {
				after = new Vec3(after.x / factor, after.y, after.z / factor);
			}
		}

		boolean hitX = player.horizontalCollision && Math.abs(want.x) > 1.0e-9 && Math.abs(moved.x - want.x) > 1.0e-7;
		boolean hitZ = player.horizontalCollision && Math.abs(want.z) > 1.0e-9 && Math.abs(moved.z - want.z) > 1.0e-7;
		if (hitX && smash(player, v, new Vec3(Math.signum(want.x), 0, 0))) {
			after = new Vec3(v.x, after.y, after.z);
			hitX = false;
		}
		if (hitZ && smash(player, v, new Vec3(0, 0, Math.signum(want.z)))) {
			after = new Vec3(after.x, after.y, v.z);
			hitZ = false;
		}
		v = after;

		if (player.onGround()) {
			if (wallRunning) {
				wallRunning = false;
			}
			if (!grounded && crouching) {
				slideSounds(player);
			}
			grounded = true;
			sinceFloor = 0;
		}
		if (hitX || hitZ) {
			Vec3 normal;
			if (hitX && (!hitZ || Math.abs(want.x) >= Math.abs(want.z))) {
				normal = new Vec3(-Math.signum(want.x), 0, 0);
			} else {
				normal = new Vec3(0, 0, -Math.signum(want.z));
			}
			if (!onWall) {
				slideSounds(player);
			}
			if (!grounded && readyToWallrun) {
				wallNorm = normal;
				if (!wallRunning) {
					v = new Vec3(v.x, KarlsonConst.WALLRUN_START_DV, v.z);
				}
				wallRunning = true;
			}
			onWall = true;
			sinceWall = 0;
		}

		sinceFloor += DT;
		sinceWall += DT;
		if (sinceFloor > KarlsonConst.CONTACT_GRACE) {
			grounded = false;
		}
		if (sinceWall > KarlsonConst.CONTACT_GRACE) {
			onWall = false;
			wallRunning = false;
		}
		if (!readyToJump && (jumpTimer -= DT) <= 0) {
			readyToJump = true;
		}
		if (!readyToWallrun && (wallrunReadyTimer -= DT) <= 0) {
			readyToWallrun = true;
		}
		if (explodedTimer > 0) {
			explodedTimer -= DT;
		}
		return v;
	}

	private static double groundGrip(LocalPlayer player) {
		BlockPos below = BlockPos.containing(player.getX(), player.getY() - 0.5000001, player.getZ());
		float friction = player.level().getBlockState(below).getBlock().getFriction();
		return Mth.clamp((1.0 - friction) / 0.4, 0.04, 1.0);
	}

	private Vec3 updateWallRun(LocalPlayer player, Vec3 v, float x, float y, Vec3 forward, Vec3 right) {
		if (!wallRunning) {
			wallRunRotation = 0;
			cancelling = false;
			return v;
		}
		double delta = Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(wallNorm.dot(right), wallNorm.dot(forward)));
		wallRunRotation = (float) (-(delta / 90.0) * KarlsonConst.WALLRUN_TILT);
		if (!readyToWallrun) {
			return v;
		}
		boolean lookingAway = Math.abs(wallRunRotation) < 4 && y > 0 && Math.abs(x) < 0.1f;
		boolean backingOff = Math.abs(wallRunRotation) > 22 && y < 0 && Math.abs(x) < 0.1f;
		if (lookingAway || backingOff) {
			if (!cancelling) {
				cancelling = true;
				cancelTimer = 0.2;
			}
			cancelTimer -= 0.05;
			if (cancelTimer <= 0) {
				cancelling = false;
				readyToWallrun = false;
				wallrunReadyTimer = 0.1;
				play(player, KarlsonSounds.WALL, 1.0f, 1.0f);
				return v.add(wallNorm.scale(KarlsonConst.WALLRUN_CANCEL_DV));
			}
		} else {
			cancelling = false;
		}
		return v;
	}

	private boolean smash(LocalPlayer player, Vec3 v, Vec3 axis) {
		double speed = Math.abs(v.x * axis.x + v.z * axis.z);
		if (speed < KarlsonConst.GLASS_BREAK_SPEED) {
			return false;
		}
		Level level = player.level();
		AABB box = player.getBoundingBox().move(axis.scale(0.2)).deflate(1.0e-3);
		List<BlockPos> targets = new ArrayList<>();
		for (BlockPos pos : BlockPos.betweenClosed(BlockPos.containing(box.minX, box.minY, box.minZ), BlockPos.containing(box.maxX, box.maxY, box.maxZ))) {
			BlockState state = level.getBlockState(pos);
			boolean breakable = KarlsonServer.isGlass(state)
					|| (crouching && speed >= KarlsonConst.DOOR_BREAK_SPEED && KarlsonServer.isBreakableDoor(state));
			if (breakable) {
				targets.add(pos.immutable());
			} else if (!state.getCollisionShape(level, pos).isEmpty()) {
				return false;
			}
		}
		boolean smashed = !targets.isEmpty();
		for (BlockPos pos : targets) {
			ClientPlayNetworking.send(new Payloads.Smash(pos));
			level.setBlock(pos, Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState(), 3);
		}
		if (smashed) {
			CameraFx.shakeOnce(5f, 3.5f, 0.3f, 0.2f);
		}
		return smashed;
	}

	private void slamEnemies(LocalPlayer player, Vec3 v) {
		double speed = v.length();
		if ((grounded && !crouching) || speed < KarlsonConst.SLAM_MIN_SPEED) {
			return;
		}
		long now = player.level().getGameTime();
		for (Iterator<Long> it = slamCooldowns.values().iterator(); it.hasNext();) {
			if (now - it.next() > 200) {
				it.remove();
			}
		}
		for (Entity entity : player.level().getEntities(player, player.getBoundingBox().inflate(0.35), e -> e instanceof LivingEntity && e instanceof Enemy && e.isAlive())) {
			Long lastHit = slamCooldowns.get(entity.getId());
			if (lastHit != null && now - lastHit < 10) {
				continue;
			}
			slamCooldowns.put(entity.getId(), now);
			ClientPlayNetworking.send(new Payloads.Slam(entity.getId(), v));
			Ragdolls.noteSlam(entity.getId(), v, grounded && crouching);
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/KarlsonMusic.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.Reader;
import java.io.Writer;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Properties;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import karlson.Karlson;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.event.lifecycle.v1.ClientTickEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.keymapping.v1.KeyMappingHelper;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.minecraft.client.KeyMapping;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.resources.sounds.AbstractTickableSoundInstance;
import net.minecraft.client.resources.sounds.SoundInstance;
import net.minecraft.client.sounds.SoundManager;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

public final class KarlsonMusic {
	private KarlsonMusic() {
	}

	private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger("KarlsonCraft");
	private static final Path CONFIG = FabricLoader.getInstance().getConfigDir().resolve("karlsoncraft.properties");
	private static final String TRACK = "music.";
	private static final float STEP = 0.1f;
	private static final int RETRY = 20;

	private static float volume = 0.6f;
	private static Track current;
	private static int wait;
	private static boolean announce;
	private static int started;
	private static boolean tracks = true;
	private static Identifier last;
	private static final List<Identifier> QUEUE = new ArrayList<>();
	private static KeyMapping louder;
	private static KeyMapping quieter;
	private static KeyMapping next;

	private static final class Track extends AbstractTickableSoundInstance {
		final Identifier id;

		Track(Identifier id) {
			super(SoundEvent.createVariableRangeEvent(id), SoundSource.MUSIC, SoundInstance.createUnseededRandom());
			this.id = id;
			this.volume = KarlsonMusic.volume;
			this.relative = true;
			this.attenuation = Attenuation.NONE;
		}

		@Override
		public void tick() {
			this.volume = KarlsonMusic.volume;
		}

		@Override
		public boolean canStartSilent() {
			return true;
		}
	}

	public static void init() {
		if (!Karlson.hasMusic()) {
			tracks = false;
			return;
		}
		load();
		KeyMapping.Category category = KeyMapping.Category.register(Karlson.id("karlson"));
		louder = KeyMappingHelper.registerKeyMapping(new KeyMapping("key.karlson.music_up", InputConstants.KEY_RBRACKET, category));
		quieter = KeyMappingHelper.registerKeyMapping(new KeyMapping("key.karlson.music_down", InputConstants.KEY_LBRACKET, category));
		next = KeyMappingHelper.registerKeyMapping(new KeyMapping("key.karlson.music_next", InputConstants.KEY_BACKSLASH, category));
		ClientTickEvents.END_CLIENT_TICK.register(KarlsonMusic::tick);
	}

	public static boolean isPlaying() {
		return current != null && Minecraft.getInstance().getSoundManager().isActive(current);
	}

	public static String track() {
		return current == null ? "" : current.id.getPath().substring(TRACK.length());
	}

	public static boolean hasTracks() {
		return tracks;
	}

	public static int started() {
		return started;
	}

	public static float volume() {
		return volume;
	}

	public static void skip() {
		if (!Karlson.hasMusic()) {
			return;
		}
		if (current != null) {
			Minecraft.getInstance().getSoundManager().stop(current);
		}
		current = null;
		wait = 0;
		announce = true;
	}

	private static void tick(Minecraft mc) {
		while (louder.consumeClick()) {
			change(mc, STEP);
		}
		while (quieter.consumeClick()) {
			change(mc, -STEP);
		}
		while (next.consumeClick()) {
			skip();
		}
		SoundManager sounds = mc.getSoundManager();
		if (current != null && sounds.isActive(current)) {
			wait = 0;
			return;
		}
		if (wait > 0) {
			wait--;
			return;
		}
		wait = RETRY;
		Identifier id = nextTrack(sounds);
		if (id == null) {
			return;
		}
		current = new Track(id);
		sounds.play(current);
		started++;
		LOGGER.info("Karlson music: {}", track());
		if (announce && mc.player != null) {
			mc.player.sendOverlayMessage(Component.literal("Karlson music: " + track().replace('_', ' ')));
		}
		announce = false;
	}

	private static Identifier nextTrack(SoundManager sounds) {
		if (QUEUE.isEmpty()) {
			for (Identifier id : sounds.getAvailableSounds()) {
				if (id.getNamespace().equals(Karlson.MOD_ID) && id.getPath().startsWith(TRACK)) {
					QUEUE.add(id);
				}
			}
			Collections.shuffle(QUEUE);
			if (QUEUE.size() > 1 && QUEUE.get(0).equals(last)) {
				Collections.swap(QUEUE, 0, QUEUE.size() - 1);
			}
		}
		tracks = !QUEUE.isEmpty();
		if (QUEUE.isEmpty()) {
			return null;
		}
		last = QUEUE.remove(0);
		return last;
	}

	private static void change(Minecraft mc, float by) {
		volume = Mth.clamp(Math.round((volume + by) * 10f) / 10f, 0f, 1f);
		save();
		if (mc.player != null) {
			String text = "Karlson music: " + Math.round(volume * 100f) + "%";
			if (mc.options.getSoundSourceVolume(SoundSource.MUSIC) <= 0f) {
				text += " (Minecraft's Music slider is off)";
			}
			mc.player.sendOverlayMessage(Component.literal(text));
		}
	}

	private static void load() {
		if (!Files.exists(CONFIG)) {
			return;
		}
		Properties props = new Properties();
		try (Reader reader = Files.newBufferedReader(CONFIG)) {
			props.load(reader);
			volume = Mth.clamp(Float.parseFloat(props.getProperty("musicVolume", "0.6")), 0f, 1f);
		} catch (IOException | NumberFormatException ignored) {
		}
	}

	private static void save() {
		Properties props = new Properties();
		props.setProperty("musicVolume", Float.toString(volume));
		try (Writer writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(CONFIG)) {
			props.store(writer, "KarlsonCraft");
		} catch (IOException ignored) {
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/Ragdolls.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.IdentityHashMap;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Random;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.TreeSet;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.level.LevelRenderContext;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.model.Model;
import net.minecraft.client.model.geom.ModelPart;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.EntityRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.LivingEntityRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.ArmedEntityRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.EntityRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.LivingEntityRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.rendertype.RenderTypes;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.CameraRenderState;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.util.LightCoordsUtil;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.decoration.ArmorStand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ClipContext;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.CollisionContext;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.VoxelShape;
import org.joml.Matrix3d;
import org.joml.Matrix3f;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;
import org.joml.Quaterniond;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3d;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public final class Ragdolls {
	private Ragdolls() {
	}

	private static final double U = KarlsonConst.SCALE;
	private static final double GRAVITY = KarlsonConst.WORLD_GRAVITY * U;
	private static final double STEP = 1.0 / 120.0;
	private static final int MAX_STEPS = 10;
	private static final int MAX_RAGDOLLS = 24;
	private static final double LIFETIME = 90.0;
	private static final double FRICTION = 0.06;
	private static final double BODY_MASS = 0.9375;
	private static final double TOTAL_MASS = 0.9375 * 2 + 0.5625 * 4;

	private static final List<Ragdoll> ALL = new ArrayList<>();
	private static final Map<Object, Ragdoll> BY_STATE = new IdentityHashMap<>();
	private static final Set<Integer> HIDDEN = new HashSet<>();
	private static final Map<Integer, Blow> BLOWS = new HashMap<>();
	private static final List<double[]> BLASTS = new ArrayList<>();
	private static final Random RANDOM = new Random();

	private static double clock;
	private static double accumulator;
	private static Ragdoll grabbed;
	private static Vec3 grabAnchor = Vec3.ZERO;
	private static Vec3 lineObject = Vec3.ZERO;
	private static Vec3 lineHand = Vec3.ZERO;

	private record Blow(Vec3 velocity, Vec3 point, double time) {
	}

	private static final class Limb {
		String[] names;
		Vector3d pivotRest;
		Vector3d tipRest;
		double length;
		double radius;
		double cosLimit;
		double sinLimit;
		Matrix3f toParent;
		Vector3f bindDir;
		final Vector3d tip = new Vector3d();
		final Vector3d prev = new Vector3d();
		final Quaternionf swing = new Quaternionf();
	}

	private static final class Ragdoll {
		@SuppressWarnings("rawtypes")
		EntityRenderer renderer;
		EntityRenderState state;
		Model<?> mainModel;
		Vec3 origin;
		Vec3 submitOffset = Vec3.ZERO;
		Vec3 startVelocity;
		Vec3 startPoint;
		boolean built;
		boolean failed;
		boolean sleeping;
		double still;
		double age;

		final Vector3d[] p = new Vector3d[8];
		final Vector3d[] q = new Vector3d[8];
		final Vector3d[] rest = new Vector3d[8];
		final Vector3d restCentre = new Vector3d();
		final Vector3d centre = new Vector3d();
		final Vector3d lastCentre = new Vector3d();
		final Quaterniond rot = new Quaterniond();
		double radius;
		final List<Limb> limbs = new ArrayList<>();

		Vec3 centreVec() {
			return new Vec3(centre.x, centre.y, centre.z);
		}

		Vec3 velocity() {
			return new Vec3((centre.x - lastCentre.x) / STEP, (centre.y - lastCentre.y) / STEP, (centre.z - lastCentre.z) / STEP);
		}
	}

	public static int count() {
		return ALL.size();
	}

	public static Vec3 newestCentre() {
		for (int i = ALL.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
			if (ALL.get(i).built) {
				return ALL.get(i).centreVec();
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	public static String debug() {
		StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
		for (Ragdoll ragdoll : ALL) {
			out.append(String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "[built=%s failed=%s asleep=%s limbs=%d centre=(%.2f %.2f %.2f) speed=%.2f] ",
					ragdoll.built, ragdoll.failed, ragdoll.sleeping, ragdoll.limbs.size(), ragdoll.centre.x, ragdoll.centre.y, ragdoll.centre.z,
					ragdoll.built ? ragdoll.velocity().length() : 0.0));
		}
		return out.isEmpty() ? "none" : out.toString();
	}

	public static void clear() {
		ALL.clear();
		BY_STATE.clear();
		HIDDEN.clear();
		BLOWS.clear();
		BLASTS.clear();
		grabbed = null;
	}

	private static boolean enabled() {
		return KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.enabled && KarlsonClient.serverSupported;
	}

	public static boolean hides(Entity entity) {
		return !HIDDEN.isEmpty() && HIDDEN.contains(entity.getId());
	}

	public static void noteBullet(int entityId, Vec3 direction, Vec3 point) {
		double speed = (350.0 * 6 + 1500.0) * KarlsonConst.FIXED_DT / TOTAL_MASS * U;
		BLOWS.put(entityId, new Blow(direction.normalize().scale(speed), point, clock));
	}

	public static void noteSlam(int entityId, Vec3 velocity, boolean sliding) {
		Vec3 thrown;
		if (sliding) {
			thrown = velocity.scale(1.2 * 34.0 * 6 * KarlsonConst.FIXED_DT / TOTAL_MASS);
		} else {
			thrown = velocity.normalize().scale(250.0 * 6 * KarlsonConst.FIXED_DT / TOTAL_MASS * U);
		}
		BLOWS.put(entityId, new Blow(thrown, null, clock));
	}

	public static void blast(Vec3 pos) {
		BLASTS.add(new double[] {pos.x, pos.y, pos.z, clock});
		for (Ragdoll ragdoll : ALL) {
			if (!ragdoll.built) {
				continue;
			}
			Vec3 delta = ragdoll.centreVec().subtract(pos);
			double dist = delta.length();
			if (dist > KarlsonConst.EXPLOSION_RADIUS + ragdoll.radius) {
				continue;
			}
			Vec3 dir = dist < 1.0e-4 ? new Vec3(0, 1, 0) : delta.scale(1.0 / dist);
			double speed = 450.0 / Math.max(dist / U, 5.0) / BODY_MASS * U;
			kick(ragdoll, dir.scale(speed), 6.0);
		}
	}

	private static void kick(Ragdoll ragdoll, Vec3 dv, double spin) {
		Vector3d w = new Vector3d(RANDOM.nextDouble() * 2 - 1, RANDOM.nextDouble() * 2 - 1, RANDOM.nextDouble() * 2 - 1).mul(spin);
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			Vector3d r = new Vector3d(ragdoll.p[i]).sub(ragdoll.centre);
			Vector3d v = new Vector3d(w).cross(r).add(dv.x, dv.y, dv.z);
			ragdoll.q[i].sub(v.mul(STEP));
		}
		for (Limb limb : ragdoll.limbs) {
			double k = 1.0 + RANDOM.nextDouble() * 0.6;
			limb.prev.sub(dv.x * k * STEP, dv.y * k * STEP, dv.z * k * STEP);
		}
		ragdoll.sleeping = false;
		ragdoll.still = 0;
	}

	public static void tick(Minecraft mc) {
		ClientLevel level = mc.level;
		if (level == null) {
			return;
		}
		HIDDEN.removeIf(id -> level.getEntity(id) == null);
		BLOWS.values().removeIf(blow -> clock - blow.time() > 3.0);
		BLASTS.removeIf(blast -> clock - blast[3] > 2.0);
		if (!enabled() || mc.player == null) {
			return;
		}
		for (Entity entity : level.entitiesForRendering()) {
			if (entity instanceof LivingEntity living && living.isDeadOrDying() && !(entity instanceof Player) && !(entity instanceof ArmorStand)
					&& !HIDDEN.contains(entity.getId()) && entity.distanceToSqr(mc.player) < 96.0 * 96.0) {
				create(mc, living);
			}
		}
		if (level.getGameTime() % 20 == 0) {
			for (Ragdoll ragdoll : ALL) {
				if (ragdoll.sleeping) {
					ragdoll.sleeping = false;
					ragdoll.still = 0.6;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	@SuppressWarnings({"rawtypes", "unchecked"})
	private static void create(Minecraft mc, LivingEntity entity) {
		HIDDEN.add(entity.getId());
		EntityRenderer renderer = mc.getEntityRenderDispatcher().getRenderer(entity);
		if (!(renderer instanceof LivingEntityRenderer living)) {
			HIDDEN.remove(entity.getId());
			return;
		}
		EntityRenderState state;
		try {
			state = renderer.createRenderState(entity, 1.0f);
		} catch (RuntimeException e) {
			HIDDEN.remove(entity.getId());
			return;
		}
		if (!(state instanceof LivingEntityRenderState livingState)) {
			HIDDEN.remove(entity.getId());
			return;
		}
		livingState.deathTime = 0f;
		livingState.hasRedOverlay = false;
		state.nameTag = null;
		state.scoreText = null;
		state.leashStates = null;
		state.displayFireAnimation = false;
		state.outlineColor = EntityRenderState.NO_OUTLINE;
		if (state instanceof ArmedEntityRenderState armed) {
			armed.rightHandItemState.clear();
			armed.leftHandItemState.clear();
		}
		if (state instanceof EnemyRenderState enemy) {
			enemy.frame = null;
		}

		Ragdoll ragdoll = new Ragdoll();
		ragdoll.renderer = renderer;
		ragdoll.state = state;
		ragdoll.mainModel = living.getModel();
		ragdoll.origin = new Vec3(state.x, state.y, state.z);

		Vec3 centre = entity.position().add(0, entity.getBbHeight() * 0.5, 0);
		Blow blow = BLOWS.remove(entity.getId());
		if (blow != null && clock - blow.time() < 1.5) {
			ragdoll.startVelocity = blow.velocity();
			ragdoll.startPoint = blow.point();
		} else {
			ragdoll.startVelocity = entity.getDeltaMovement().scale(20.0);
			for (double[] blast : BLASTS) {
				Vec3 delta = centre.subtract(blast[0], blast[1], blast[2]);
				if (clock - blast[3] < 1.0 && delta.length() < KarlsonConst.EXPLOSION_RADIUS + 2.0) {
					Vec3 dir = delta.lengthSqr() < 1.0e-6 ? new Vec3(0, 1, 0) : delta.normalize();
					ragdoll.startVelocity = dir.scale(1100.0 * 6 * KarlsonConst.FIXED_DT / TOTAL_MASS * U);
				}
			}
		}
		ALL.add(ragdoll);
		BY_STATE.put(state, ragdoll);
		while (ALL.size() > MAX_RAGDOLLS) {
			remove(ALL.get(0));
		}
	}

	private static void remove(Ragdoll ragdoll) {
		ALL.remove(ragdoll);
		BY_STATE.remove(ragdoll.state);
		if (grabbed == ragdoll) {
			grabbed = null;
		}
	}

	private record CubeRef(String path, Matrix4f matrix, ModelPart.Cube cube) {
	}

	private static ModelPart resolve(ModelPart root, String[] names) {
		ModelPart part = root;
		for (String name : names) {
			if (!part.hasChild(name)) {
				return null;
			}
			part = part.getChild(name);
		}
		return part;
	}

	private static String[] names(String path) {
		return path.isEmpty() ? new String[0] : path.substring(1).split("/");
	}

	private static double volume(ModelPart.Cube cube) {
		return (double) (cube.maxX - cube.minX) * (cube.maxY - cube.minY) * (cube.maxZ - cube.minZ);
	}

	private static List<Vector3f> corners(CubeRef ref, Matrix4f toLocal) {
		List<Vector3f> out = new ArrayList<>(8);
		ModelPart.Cube c = ref.cube();
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			Vector3f v = new Vector3f(((i & 1) == 0 ? c.minX : c.maxX) / 16f, ((i & 2) == 0 ? c.minY : c.maxY) / 16f, ((i & 4) == 0 ? c.minZ : c.maxZ) / 16f);
			ref.matrix().transformPosition(v);
			toLocal.transformPosition(v);
			out.add(v);
		}
		return out;
	}

	private static void build(Ragdoll ragdoll, Model<?> model, Matrix4f drawPose) {
		Matrix4f toLocal = new Matrix4f().translation((float) -ragdoll.submitOffset.x, (float) -ragdoll.submitOffset.y, (float) -ragdoll.submitOffset.z).mul(drawPose);
		List<CubeRef> cubes = new ArrayList<>();
		model.root().visit(new PoseStack(), (pose, path, index, cube) -> cubes.add(new CubeRef(path, new Matrix4f(pose.pose()), cube)));
		if (cubes.isEmpty()) {
			ragdoll.failed = true;
			return;
		}

		String parent = "";
		Set<String> kids;
		boolean direct;
		while (true) {
			String prefix = parent + "/";
			direct = false;
			kids = new TreeSet<>();
			for (CubeRef ref : cubes) {
				if (ref.path().equals(parent)) {
					direct = true;
				} else if (ref.path().startsWith(prefix)) {
					int end = ref.path().indexOf('/', prefix.length());
					kids.add(end < 0 ? ref.path().substring(prefix.length()) : ref.path().substring(prefix.length(), end));
				}
			}
			if (!direct && kids.size() == 1) {
				parent = prefix + kids.iterator().next();
				continue;
			}
			break;
		}
		String trunk = parent;
		if (!direct) {
			double best = -1;
			for (String kid : kids) {
				String path = parent + "/" + kid;
				double total = 0;
				for (CubeRef ref : cubes) {
					if (ref.path().equals(path) || ref.path().startsWith(path + "/")) {
						total += volume(ref.cube());
					}
				}
				if (total > best) {
					best = total;
					trunk = path;
				}
			}
		}

		CubeRef body = null;
		for (CubeRef ref : cubes) {
			if (ref.path().equals(trunk) && (body == null || volume(ref.cube()) > volume(body.cube()))) {
				body = ref;
			}
		}
		if (body == null) {
			for (CubeRef ref : cubes) {
				if (ref.path().startsWith(trunk) && (body == null || volume(ref.cube()) > volume(body.cube()))) {
					body = ref;
				}
			}
		}
		if (body == null) {
			ragdoll.failed = true;
			return;
		}
		List<Vector3f> box = corners(body, toLocal);
		Vec3 origin = ragdoll.origin;
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			Vector3f v = box.get(i);
			ragdoll.p[i] = new Vector3d(origin.x + v.x, origin.y + v.y, origin.z + v.z);
			ragdoll.restCentre.add(ragdoll.p[i]);
		}
		ragdoll.restCentre.div(8.0);
		ragdoll.centre.set(ragdoll.restCentre);
		ragdoll.lastCentre.set(ragdoll.restCentre);
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			ragdoll.rest[i] = new Vector3d(ragdoll.p[i]).sub(ragdoll.restCentre);
			ragdoll.radius = Math.max(ragdoll.radius, ragdoll.rest[i].length());
		}

		String[] parentNames = names(parent);
		PoseStack stack = new PoseStack();
		ModelPart part = model.root();
		part.translateAndRotate(stack);
		for (String name : parentNames) {
			part = part.getChild(name);
			part.translateAndRotate(stack);
		}
		Matrix4f parentMat = new Matrix4f(toLocal).mul(stack.last().pose());
		Matrix3f toParent = new Matrix3f(parentMat).invert();

		for (String kid : kids) {
			String path = parent + "/" + kid;
			if (path.equals(trunk) || !part.hasChild(kid)) {
				continue;
			}
			ModelPart limbPart = part.getChild(kid);
			Vector3f pivot = parentMat.transformPosition(new Vector3f(limbPart.x / 16f, limbPart.y / 16f, limbPart.z / 16f));
			List<Vector3f> points = new ArrayList<>();
			double thickness = Double.MAX_VALUE;
			for (CubeRef ref : cubes) {
				if (ref.path().equals(path) || ref.path().startsWith(path + "/")) {
					points.addAll(corners(ref, toLocal));
					ModelPart.Cube c = ref.cube();
					thickness = Math.min(thickness, Math.min(c.maxX - c.minX, Math.min(c.maxY - c.minY, c.maxZ - c.minZ)));
				}
			}
			if (points.isEmpty()) {
				continue;
			}
			Vector3f mean = new Vector3f();
			for (Vector3f v : points) {
				mean.add(v);
			}
			mean.div(points.size());
			Vector3f dir = new Vector3f(mean).sub(pivot);
			if (dir.length() < 0.04f) {
				continue;
			}
			dir.normalize();
			float length = 0;
			for (Vector3f v : points) {
				length = Math.max(length, new Vector3f(v).sub(pivot).dot(dir));
			}
			Limb limb = new Limb();
			limb.names = names(path);
			limb.length = length;
			float scale = new Vector3f(toLocal.m00(), toLocal.m01(), toLocal.m02()).length();
			limb.radius = Math.max(0.03, thickness / 32.0 * scale);
			double limit = Math.toRadians(kid.contains("head") || kid.contains("neck") ? 50.0 : 105.0);
			limb.cosLimit = Math.cos(limit);
			limb.sinLimit = Math.sin(limit);
			limb.toParent = toParent;
			limb.bindDir = toParent.transform(new Vector3f(dir)).normalize();
			Vector3d pivotWorld = new Vector3d(origin.x + pivot.x, origin.y + pivot.y, origin.z + pivot.z);
			limb.pivotRest = new Vector3d(pivotWorld).sub(ragdoll.restCentre);
			limb.tip.set(pivotWorld).add(dir.x * length, dir.y * length, dir.z * length);
			limb.tipRest = new Vector3d(limb.tip).sub(ragdoll.restCentre);
			ragdoll.limbs.add(limb);
		}

		Vec3 v0 = ragdoll.startVelocity;
		if (v0.length() > 40.0) {
			v0 = v0.normalize().scale(40.0);
		}
		Vector3d spin = new Vector3d(RANDOM.nextDouble() * 2 - 1, RANDOM.nextDouble() * 2 - 1, RANDOM.nextDouble() * 2 - 1).mul(1.5);
		if (ragdoll.startPoint != null) {
			Vector3d arm = new Vector3d(ragdoll.startPoint.x, ragdoll.startPoint.y, ragdoll.startPoint.z).sub(ragdoll.restCentre);
			Vector3d torque = arm.cross(new Vector3d(v0.x, v0.y, v0.z)).mul(2.5);
			if (torque.length() > 9.0) {
				torque.normalize(9.0);
			}
			spin.add(torque);
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			Vector3d v = new Vector3d(spin).cross(ragdoll.rest[i]).add(v0.x, v0.y, v0.z);
			ragdoll.q[i] = new Vector3d(ragdoll.p[i]).sub(v.mul(STEP));
		}
		for (Limb limb : ragdoll.limbs) {
			double k = 1.0 + RANDOM.nextDouble() * 0.5;
			limb.prev.set(limb.tip).sub(v0.x * k * STEP, v0.y * k * STEP, v0.z * k * STEP);
		}
		ragdoll.built = true;
	}

	public static void pose(Object state, Model<?> model, PoseStack poseStack) {
		if (BY_STATE.isEmpty()) {
			return;
		}
		Ragdoll ragdoll = BY_STATE.get(state);
		if (ragdoll == null || ragdoll.failed) {
			return;
		}
		if (!ragdoll.built) {
			if (model == ragdoll.mainModel) {
				try {
					build(ragdoll, model, poseStack.last().pose());
				} catch (RuntimeException e) {
					ragdoll.failed = true;
				}
			}
			return;
		}
		Vector3f euler = new Vector3f();
		for (Limb limb : ragdoll.limbs) {
			ModelPart part = resolve(model.root(), limb.names);
			if (part == null) {
				continue;
			}
			Quaternionf rotation = new Quaternionf(limb.swing).mul(new Quaternionf().rotationZYX(part.zRot, part.yRot, part.xRot));
			rotation.getEulerAnglesZYX(euler);
			part.xRot = euler.x;
			part.yRot = euler.y;
			part.zRot = euler.z;
		}
	}

	private static boolean collide(ClientLevel level, Vector3d a, Vector3d b) {
		double dx = b.x - a.x;
		double dy = b.y - a.y;
		double dz = b.z - a.z;
		if (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz < 1.0e-14) {
			return false;
		}
		BlockHitResult hit = level.clip(new ClipContext(new Vec3(a.x, a.y, a.z), new Vec3(b.x, b.y, b.z), ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER, ClipContext.Fluid.NONE, CollisionContext.empty()));
		if (hit.getType() == HitResult.Type.MISS) {
			return false;
		}
		if (hit.isInside()) {
			BlockPos pos = hit.getBlockPos();
			VoxelShape shape = level.getBlockState(pos).getCollisionShape(level, pos);
			double top = pos.getY() + (shape.isEmpty() ? 1.0 : shape.max(Direction.Axis.Y)) + 0.002;
			b.set(a.x, top, a.z);
			a.set(b);
			return true;
		}
		Vec3 n = Vec3.atLowerCornerOf(hit.getDirection().getUnitVec3i());
		Vec3 at = hit.getLocation().add(n.scale(0.002));
		double vn = dx * n.x + dy * n.y + dz * n.z;
		double keep = 1.0 - FRICTION;
		b.set(at.x, at.y, at.z);
		a.set(at.x - (dx - n.x * vn) * keep, at.y - (dy - n.y * vn) * keep, at.z - (dz - n.z * vn) * keep);
		return true;
	}

	private static void match(Ragdoll ragdoll) {
		Vector3d c = ragdoll.centre.zero();
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			c.add(ragdoll.p[i]);
		}
		c.div(8.0);
		double a00 = 0, a01 = 0, a02 = 0, a10 = 0, a11 = 0, a12 = 0, a20 = 0, a21 = 0, a22 = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			Vector3d r = ragdoll.rest[i];
			double dx = ragdoll.p[i].x - c.x;
			double dy = ragdoll.p[i].y - c.y;
			double dz = ragdoll.p[i].z - c.z;
			a00 += dx * r.x;
			a01 += dx * r.y;
			a02 += dx * r.z;
			a10 += dy * r.x;
			a11 += dy * r.y;
			a12 += dy * r.z;
			a20 += dz * r.x;
			a21 += dz * r.y;
			a22 += dz * r.z;
		}
		Quaterniond rot = ragdoll.rot;
		Matrix3d m = new Matrix3d();
		Vector3d omega = new Vector3d();
		for (int iteration = 0; iteration < 12; iteration++) {
			rot.get(m);
			double ox = (m.m01 * a20 - m.m02 * a10) + (m.m11 * a21 - m.m12 * a11) + (m.m21 * a22 - m.m22 * a12);
			double oy = (m.m02 * a00 - m.m00 * a20) + (m.m12 * a01 - m.m10 * a21) + (m.m22 * a02 - m.m20 * a22);
			double oz = (m.m00 * a10 - m.m01 * a00) + (m.m10 * a11 - m.m11 * a01) + (m.m20 * a12 - m.m21 * a02);
			double denominator = Math.abs(m.m00 * a00 + m.m01 * a10 + m.m02 * a20
					+ m.m10 * a01 + m.m11 * a11 + m.m12 * a21
					+ m.m20 * a02 + m.m21 * a12 + m.m22 * a22) + 1.0e-9;
			omega.set(ox, oy, oz).div(denominator);
			double angle = omega.length();
			if (angle < 1.0e-9) {
				break;
			}
			rot.premul(new Quaterniond().fromAxisAngleRad(omega.x / angle, omega.y / angle, omega.z / angle, angle)).normalize();
		}
		Vector3d goal = new Vector3d();
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			rot.transform(ragdoll.rest[i], goal).add(c);
			ragdoll.p[i].set(goal);
		}
	}

	private static void step(ClientLevel level, Ragdoll ragdoll) {
		double ax = 0;
		double ay = -GRAVITY;
		double az = 0;
		double drag = 0;
		if (grabbed == ragdoll) {
			Vec3 v = ragdoll.velocity();
			double k = KarlsonConst.GRAB_SPRING * KarlsonConst.GRAB_MASS_SCALE / BODY_MASS;
			double d = KarlsonConst.GRAB_DAMPER * KarlsonConst.GRAB_MASS_SCALE / BODY_MASS;
			ax += (grabAnchor.x - ragdoll.centre.x) * k - v.x * d;
			ay += (grabAnchor.y - ragdoll.centre.y) * k - v.y * d;
			az += (grabAnchor.z - ragdoll.centre.z) * k - v.z * d;
			drag = 1.0;
		}
		double keep = 1.0 - drag * STEP;
		double moved = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			Vector3d p = ragdoll.p[i];
			Vector3d q = ragdoll.q[i];
			double vx = (p.x - q.x) * keep;
			double vy = (p.y - q.y) * keep;
			double vz = (p.z - q.z) * keep;
			q.set(p);
			p.add(vx + ax * STEP * STEP, vy + ay * STEP * STEP, vz + az * STEP * STEP);
			collide(level, q, p);
		}
		ragdoll.lastCentre.set(ragdoll.centre);
		match(ragdoll);
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			collide(level, ragdoll.q[i], ragdoll.p[i]);
		}
		match(ragdoll);
		moved = ragdoll.centre.distance(ragdoll.lastCentre);
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			moved = Math.max(moved, ragdoll.p[i].distance(ragdoll.q[i]));
		}

		Vector3d pivot = new Vector3d();
		Vector3d restDir = new Vector3d();
		Vector3d dir = new Vector3d();
		Vector3d low = new Vector3d();
		for (Limb limb : ragdoll.limbs) {
			ragdoll.rot.transform(limb.pivotRest, pivot).add(ragdoll.centre);
			Vector3d tip = limb.tip;
