			double vx = (tip.x - limb.prev.x) * 0.995;
			double vy = (tip.y - limb.prev.y) * 0.995;
			double vz = (tip.z - limb.prev.z) * 0.995;
			limb.prev.set(tip);
			tip.add(vx, vy - GRAVITY * STEP * STEP, vz);
			collide(level, limb.prev, tip);
			low.set(tip.x, tip.y - limb.radius, tip.z);
			Vector3d from = new Vector3d(tip);
			if (collide(level, from, low)) {
				tip.y = low.y + limb.radius;
				limb.prev.y = tip.y;
				limb.prev.x += (tip.x - limb.prev.x) * FRICTION;
				limb.prev.z += (tip.z - limb.prev.z) * FRICTION;
			}
			dir.set(tip).sub(pivot);
			double length = dir.length();
			if (length < 1.0e-9) {
				dir.set(0, -1, 0);
			} else {
				dir.div(length);
			}
			ragdoll.rot.transform(new Vector3d(limb.tipRest).sub(limb.pivotRest), restDir).normalize();
			double cos = dir.dot(restDir);
			if (cos < limb.cosLimit) {
				Vector3d side = new Vector3d(dir).sub(new Vector3d(restDir).mul(cos));
				if (side.length() < 1.0e-9) {
					side.set(restDir.y, restDir.z, restDir.x).cross(restDir);
				}
				side.normalize();
				dir.set(restDir).mul(limb.cosLimit).add(side.mul(limb.sinLimit));
			}
			tip.set(pivot).add(dir.x * limb.length, dir.y * limb.length, dir.z * limb.length);
			moved = Math.max(moved, tip.distance(limb.prev) * 0.5);
		}

		if (moved < 0.0006 && grabbed != ragdoll) {
			ragdoll.still += STEP;
			if (ragdoll.still > 0.75) {
				ragdoll.sleeping = true;
			}
		} else {
			ragdoll.still = 0;
		}
	}

	public static boolean isGrabbing() {
		return grabbed != null;
	}

	public static boolean tryGrab(Minecraft mc, LocalPlayer player) {
		if (!enabled() || ALL.isEmpty() || mc.level == null) {
			return false;
		}
		Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
		Vec3 look = player.getLookAngle();
		Ragdoll best = null;
		double bestT = KarlsonConst.GRAB_RANGE;
		for (Ragdoll ragdoll : ALL) {
			if (!ragdoll.built) {
				continue;
			}
			double t = raySphere(eye, look, ragdoll.centreVec(), Math.max(ragdoll.radius, 0.3));
			for (Limb limb : ragdoll.limbs) {
				t = Math.min(t, raySphere(eye, look, new Vec3(limb.tip.x, limb.tip.y, limb.tip.z), 0.3));
			}
			if (t < bestT) {
				bestT = t;
				best = ragdoll;
			}
		}
		if (best == null) {
			return false;
		}
		if (mc.hitResult instanceof EntityHitResult entHit && entHit.getLocation().distanceTo(eye) < bestT) {
			return false;
		}
		Vec3 target = eye.add(look.scale(Math.max(0.0, bestT - 0.05)));
		if (mc.level.clip(new ClipContext(eye, target, ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER, ClipContext.Fluid.NONE, CollisionContext.empty())).getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS) {
			return false;
		}
		grabbed = best;
		best.sleeping = false;
		best.still = 0;
		grabAnchor = eye.add(look.scale(KarlsonConst.GRAB_HOLD));
		lineObject = best.centreVec();
		lineHand = grabAnchor;
		return true;
	}

	public static void release() {
		grabbed = null;
	}

	private static double raySphere(Vec3 origin, Vec3 dir, Vec3 centre, double radius) {
		Vec3 to = centre.subtract(origin);
		double along = to.dot(dir);
		double distSq = to.lengthSqr() - along * along;
		if (along < 0 || distSq > radius * radius) {
			return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		}
		return Math.max(0.0, along - Math.sqrt(radius * radius - distSq));
	}

	@SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
	public static void render(LevelRenderContext context) {
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		ClientLevel level = mc.level;
		if (level == null || ALL.isEmpty()) {
			return;
		}
		CameraRenderState camera = context.levelState().cameraRenderState;
		Vec3 camPos = camera.pos;
		Vec3 camFwd = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 0, -1)));
		float dt = CameraFx.frameDt();
		clock += dt;

		if (grabbed != null) {
			grabAnchor = camPos.add(camFwd.scale(KarlsonConst.GRAB_HOLD));
		}
		accumulator = Math.min(accumulator + dt, STEP * MAX_STEPS);
		int steps = (int) (accumulator / STEP);
		accumulator -= steps * STEP;
		for (Iterator<Ragdoll> it = ALL.iterator(); it.hasNext();) {
			Ragdoll ragdoll = it.next();
			ragdoll.age += dt;
			boolean gone = ragdoll.failed || ragdoll.age > LIFETIME
					|| (ragdoll.built && (ragdoll.centre.y < level.getMinY() - 32 || ragdoll.centreVec().distanceToSqr(camPos) > 160.0 * 160.0));
			if (gone && grabbed != ragdoll) {
				it.remove();
				BY_STATE.remove(ragdoll.state);
				continue;
			}
			if (ragdoll.built && !ragdoll.sleeping) {
				for (int i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
					step(level, ragdoll);
				}
			}
		}

		PoseStack poseStack = context.poseStack();
		for (Ragdoll ragdoll : ALL) {
			poseStack.pushPose();
			Vec3 offset = ragdoll.origin.subtract(camPos);
			if (!ragdoll.built) {
				ragdoll.submitOffset = offset;
				poseStack.translate(offset.x, offset.y, offset.z);
			} else {
				Vector3d c = ragdoll.centre;
				Vector3d rc = ragdoll.restCentre;
				poseStack.translate(c.x - camPos.x, c.y - camPos.y, c.z - camPos.z);
				poseStack.rotate(new Quaternionf((float) ragdoll.rot.x, (float) ragdoll.rot.y, (float) ragdoll.rot.z, (float) ragdoll.rot.w));
				poseStack.translate(ragdoll.origin.x - rc.x, ragdoll.origin.y - rc.y, ragdoll.origin.z - rc.z);
				updateLimbs(ragdoll);
				ragdoll.state.lightCoords = LightCoordsUtil.getLightCoords(level, BlockPos.containing(c.x, c.y + 0.1, c.z));
			}
			try {
				ragdoll.renderer.submit(ragdoll.state, poseStack, context.submitNodeCollector(), camera);
				if (!ragdoll.built && ragdoll.renderer instanceof LivingEntityRenderer<?, ?, ?> living) {
					ragdoll.mainModel = living.getModel();
				}
			} catch (RuntimeException e) {
				ragdoll.failed = true;
			}
			poseStack.popPose();
		}

		if (grabbed != null && grabbed.built && dt > 0) {
			double k = Math.min(1.0, dt * 45.0);
			lineObject = lineObject.lerp(grabbed.centreVec(), k);
			lineHand = lineHand.lerp(grabAnchor, k);
			double width = 0.0075 * grabbed.velocity().length();
			if (width > 1.0e-4) {
				Vec3 a = lineObject.subtract(camPos);
				Vec3 b = lineHand.subtract(camPos);
				int white = Draw.argb(255, 255, 255, 255);
				context.submitNodeCollector().submitCustomGeometry(poseStack, RenderTypes.debugQuads(), (pose, buffer) -> Draw.beam(pose, buffer, a, b, 0.0, width, white, white));
			}
		}
	}

	private static void updateLimbs(Ragdoll ragdoll) {
		Vector3d pivot = new Vector3d();
		Vector3d dir = new Vector3d();
		Quaterniond inverse = new Quaterniond(ragdoll.rot).conjugate();
		for (Limb limb : ragdoll.limbs) {
			ragdoll.rot.transform(limb.pivotRest, pivot).add(ragdoll.centre);
			dir.set(limb.tip).sub(pivot);
			if (dir.length() < 1.0e-9) {
				continue;
			}
			inverse.transform(dir);
			Vector3f local = limb.toParent.transform(new Vector3f((float) dir.x, (float) dir.y, (float) dir.z));
			if (local.length() < 1.0e-6f) {
				continue;
			}
			limb.swing.rotationTo(limb.bindDir, local.normalize());
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/SpeedLines.java ====
package karlson.client;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.level.LevelRenderContext;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.rendertype.RenderTypes;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.CameraRenderState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public final class SpeedLines {
	private SpeedLines() {
	}

	private static final float SIM_SPEED = 2f;
	private static final float RING_RADIUS = 2.33f;
	private static final float TUBE_RADIUS = 0.2f;
	private static final float RING_DISTANCE = 2.17f;
	private static final float RING_SQUASH = 0.705f;
	private static final float TRAIL_RATIO = 0.14f;
	private static final float TRAIL_WIDTH = 0.09f;
	private static final int MAX_LINES = 400;

	private static final class Line {
		float x;
		float y;
		float vx;
		float vy;
		float life;
		float age;
	}

	private static final Random RANDOM = new Random();
	private static final List<Line> LINES = new ArrayList<>();
	private static float emit;

	public static void clear() {
		LINES.clear();
	}

	public static void render(LevelRenderContext context) {
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		KarlsonMovement movement = KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE;
		LocalPlayer player = mc.player;
		float dt = CameraFx.frameDt() * SIM_SPEED;
		CameraRenderState camera = context.levelState().cameraRenderState;
		Vec3 right = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(1, 0, 0)));
		Vec3 up = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 1, 0)));
		Vec3 forward = new Vec3(camera.orientation.transform(new Vector3f(0, 0, -1)));

		float rate = 0;
		if (player != null && camera.isFirstPerson && movement.enabled && KarlsonClient.serverSupported) {
			Vec3 velocity = null;
			if (movement.active) {
				if (!movement.grounded || movement.wallRunning) {
					velocity = movement.velocity;
				}
			} else if (player.isFallFlying()) {
				velocity = player.getDeltaMovement().scale(20.0);
			}
			if (velocity != null) {
				double speed = velocity.length() / KarlsonConst.SCALE;
				if (speed > 1.0e-3) {
					double cos = velocity.normalize().dot(forward);
					double angle = Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(Math.max(-1.0, Math.min(1.0, cos))));
					rate = (float) (speed / Math.max(angle * 0.15, 1.0));
				}
			}
		}
		emit += rate * dt;
		while (emit >= 1f) {
			emit -= 1f;
			if (LINES.size() < MAX_LINES) {
				Line line = new Line();
				double a = RANDOM.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2.0;
				float radius = RING_RADIUS + (RANDOM.nextFloat() * 2f - 1f) * TUBE_RADIUS;
				float cos = (float) Math.cos(a);
				float sin = (float) Math.sin(a);
				line.x = cos * radius;
				line.y = sin * radius * RING_SQUASH;
				float speed = 3f + RANDOM.nextFloat() * 2f;
				line.vx = cos * speed;
				line.vy = sin * speed * RING_SQUASH;
				line.life = 0.8f + RANDOM.nextFloat() * 0.4f;
				LINES.add(line);
			}
		}
		if (LINES.isEmpty()) {
			return;
		}

		double fit = Math.tan(Math.toRadians(mc.gameRenderer.mainCamera().getFov() * 0.5)) / Math.tan(Math.toRadians(40.0));
		double scale = KarlsonConst.SCALE;
		Vec3 centre = forward.scale(RING_DISTANCE * scale);
		List<Vec3[]> beams = new ArrayList<>();
		List<float[]> looks = new ArrayList<>();
		for (Iterator<Line> it = LINES.iterator(); it.hasNext();) {
			Line line = it.next();
			line.age += dt;
			if (line.age >= line.life) {
				it.remove();
				continue;
			}
			line.x += line.vx * dt;
			line.y += line.vy * dt;
			float t = line.age / line.life;
			float alpha = t < 0.06f ? 0f : (t - 0.06f) / 0.94f;
			float back = Math.min(line.age, TRAIL_RATIO * line.life);
			Vec3 head = centre.add(right.scale(line.x * scale * fit)).add(up.scale(line.y * scale * fit));
			Vec3 tail = centre.add(right.scale((line.x - line.vx * back) * scale * fit)).add(up.scale((line.y - line.vy * back) * scale * fit));
			beams.add(new Vec3[] {tail, head});
			looks.add(new float[] {alpha, (float) (TRAIL_WIDTH * 0.199 * scale * fit), (float) (TRAIL_WIDTH * 0.058 * scale * fit)});
		}
		context.submitNodeCollector().submitCustomGeometry(context.poseStack(), RenderTypes.debugQuads(), (pose, buffer) -> {
			for (int i = 0; i < beams.size(); i++) {
				Vec3[] beam = beams.get(i);
				float[] look = looks.get(i);
				int color = Draw.argb((int) (255 * look[0]), 255, 255, 255);
				Draw.beam(pose, buffer, beam[0], beam[1], look[2], look[1], color, color);
			}
		});
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/client/Viewmodel.java ====
package karlson.client;

import karlson.GunGeometry;
import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.KarlsonSounds;
import karlson.item.GrapplerItem;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.joml.Quaternionf;
import org.joml.Vector3f;

public final class Viewmodel {
	private Viewmodel() {
	}

	public static final float FOV_FIT = (float) (Math.tan(Math.toRadians(35.0)) / Math.tan(Math.toRadians(40.0)));

	private static final Vector3f DEFAULT_POS = new Vector3f((float) GunGeometry.HOLD_RIGHT, (float) GunGeometry.HOLD_UP, (float) GunGeometry.HOLD_FORWARD);
	private static final float SPEED = 8f;
	private static final float POS_SPEED = 0.075f;
	private static final float POS_OFFSET = 0.004f;
	private static final float X_BOB = 0.12f;
	private static final float Y_BOB = 0.08f;
	private static final float Z_BOB = 0.1f;
	private static final float BOB_SPEED = 0.45f;

	private static boolean active;
	private static Item heldItem;
	private static final Vector3f pos = new Vector3f(DEFAULT_POS);
	private static final Vector3f posVel = new Vector3f();
	private static final Vector3f wantBob = new Vector3f();
	private static final Quaternionf worldRot = new Quaternionf();
	private static final Quaternionf camRot = new Quaternionf();
	private static float rotationOffset;
	private static float rotVel;
	private static float prevYaw;
	private static double time;
	private static final Vector3f wpnPos = new Vector3f();
	private static final Vector3f wpnVel = new Vector3f();

	private static final Quaternionf localRot = new Quaternionf();
	private static final Vector3f viewPos = new Vector3f();
	private static final Vector3f tipView = new Vector3f();

	public static boolean isActive() {
		return active;
	}

	public static Quaternionf localRotation() {
		return localRot;
	}

	public static Vector3f viewPosition() {
		return viewPos;
	}

	public static Vector3f tipViewPosition() {
		return tipView;
	}

	public static void shoot(float recoil) {
		if (!active) {
			return;
		}
		pos.add(0f, 0.25f * recoil, -recoil / 1.5f);
		worldRot.set(camRot).rotateX(60f * recoil * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD);
	}

	private static float pingPong(double t, float length) {
		double m = t % (2.0 * length);
		return (float) (length - Math.abs(m - length));
	}

	public static void frame(float dt) {
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		LocalPlayer player = mc.player;
		if (player == null) {
			active = false;
			heldItem = null;
			return;
		}
		float partial = mc.getDeltaTracker().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(true);
		float yaw = player.getViewYRot(partial);
		float pitch = player.getViewXRot(partial);
		camRot.rotationYXZ(Mth.PI - yaw * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD, -pitch * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD, CameraFx.roll());

		Item item = player.getMainHandItem().getItem();
		if (!KarlsonClient.isWeapon(item)) {
			active = false;
			heldItem = null;
			return;
		}
		if (!active) {
			pos.set(DEFAULT_POS);
			posVel.zero();
			worldRot.set(camRot);
			rotationOffset = 0;
			rotVel = 0;
			prevYaw = yaw;
			active = true;
		}
		if (item != heldItem) {
			heldItem = item;
			wpnPos.set(0.15f, -0.9f, -0.35f);
			wpnVel.zero();
			player.level().playLocalSound(player, KarlsonSounds.GUN_PICKUP, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.5f, 1.0f);
		}

		if (dt > 0) {
			time += dt;
			KarlsonMovement movement = KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE;
			Vec3 velocity = (movement.active ? movement.velocity : player.getDeltaMovement().scale(20.0)).scale(1.0 / KarlsonConst.SCALE);
			boolean grounded = movement.active ? movement.grounded : player.onGround();

			if (grounded) {
				if (velocity.length() < 4.0) {
					wantBob.zero();
				} else {
					wantBob.set(
							pingPong(time * BOB_SPEED, X_BOB) - X_BOB / 2f,
							pingPong(time * BOB_SPEED, Y_BOB) - Y_BOB / 2f,
							pingPong(time * BOB_SPEED, Z_BOB) - Z_BOB / 2f);
				}
			}

			Quaternionf target = new Quaternionf();
			GrappleController grapple = GrappleController.INSTANCE;
			if (item instanceof GrapplerItem && grapple.isGrappling()) {
				Vec3 dir = grapple.point().subtract(player.getEyePosition(partial)).normalize();
				float aimYaw = (float) Math.atan2(-dir.x, dir.z);
				float aimPitch = (float) -Math.asin(Mth.clamp(dir.y, -1.0, 1.0));
				target.rotationYXZ(Mth.PI - aimYaw, -aimPitch, 0f);
			} else {
				rotationOffset += Mth.wrapDegrees(prevYaw - yaw);
				rotationOffset = Mth.clamp(rotationOffset, -90f, 90f);
				float[] damped = CameraFx.smoothDamp(rotationOffset, 0f, rotVel, 0.025f, dt);
				rotationOffset = damped[0];
				rotVel = damped[1];
				target.rotationY(rotationOffset * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD).mul(camRot).rotateZ(-rotationOffset * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD);
			}
			worldRot.slerp(target, Math.min(1f, dt * SPEED));

			float yawRad = yaw * Mth.DEG_TO_RAD;
			double forwardVel = velocity.x * -Mth.sin(yawRad) + velocity.z * Mth.cos(yawRad);
			double rightVel = velocity.x * -Mth.cos(yawRad) + velocity.z * -Mth.sin(yawRad);
			float fall = (float) velocity.y * POS_OFFSET;
			if (fall < -0.08f) {
				fall = -0.08f;
			}
			float tx = DEFAULT_POS.x - (float) rightVel * POS_OFFSET + wantBob.x;
			float ty = DEFAULT_POS.y - fall + wantBob.y;
			float tz = DEFAULT_POS.z - (float) forwardVel * POS_OFFSET + wantBob.z;
			float[] dx = CameraFx.smoothDamp(pos.x, tx, posVel.x, POS_SPEED, dt);
			float[] dy = CameraFx.smoothDamp(pos.y, ty, posVel.y, POS_SPEED, dt);
			float[] dz = CameraFx.smoothDamp(pos.z, tz, posVel.z, POS_SPEED, dt);
			pos.set(dx[0], dy[0], dz[0]);
			posVel.set(dx[1], dy[1], dz[1]);

			float[] wx = CameraFx.smoothDamp(wpnPos.x, 0f, wpnVel.x, 0.1f, dt);
			float[] wy = CameraFx.smoothDamp(wpnPos.y, 0f, wpnVel.y, 0.1f, dt);
			float[] wz = CameraFx.smoothDamp(wpnPos.z, 0f, wpnVel.z, 0.1f, dt);
			wpnPos.set(wx[0], wy[0], wz[0]);
			wpnVel.set(wx[1], wy[1], wz[1]);
			prevYaw = yaw;
		}

		float scale = (float) KarlsonConst.SCALE;
		camRot.conjugate(localRot).mul(worldRot);
		Vector3f offset = new Vector3f(wpnPos.x, wpnPos.y, -wpnPos.z).mul(scale);
		localRot.transform(offset);
		viewPos.set(pos.x * scale, pos.y * scale, -pos.z * scale).add(offset);
		Vec3 tip = GunGeometry.tip(GunGeometry.name(item));
		tipView.set((float) tip.x, (float) tip.y, (float) tip.z);
		localRot.transform(tipView);
		tipView.add(viewPos);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/entity/KarlsonEnemy.java ====
package karlson.entity;

import java.util.EnumSet;

import karlson.KarlsonItems;
import karlson.item.GunItem;
import karlson.server.Bullets;
import karlson.server.KarlsonServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.DifficultyInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntitySpawnReason;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EquipmentSlot;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.SpawnGroupData;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeSupplier;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.FloatGoal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.Goal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.LookAtPlayerGoal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.RandomLookAroundGoal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.WaterAvoidingRandomStrollGoal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.target.HurtByTargetGoal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.goal.target.NearestAttackableTargetGoal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Monster;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ServerLevelAccessor;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public class KarlsonEnemy extends Monster {
	private static final double RANGE = 24.0;
	private static final float DAMAGE_SCALE = 0.3f;
	private static final double AIM_ERROR = 0.02;
	private static final int AIM_TICKS = 8;
	private static final float GUN_DROP_CHANCE = 0.35f;
	private static final double RUNNING_SQ = 0.03 * 0.03;
	private static final float TRANSITION = 0.35f;
	public static int debugAnimation;

	public float runAmount;
	public float runAmountO;
	public float aimAmount;
	public float aimAmountO;
	public Object animFrame;

	public KarlsonEnemy(EntityType<? extends KarlsonEnemy> type, Level level) {
		super(type, level);
	}

	public static AttributeSupplier.Builder createAttributes() {
		return Monster.createMonsterAttributes()
				.add(Attributes.MAX_HEALTH, 20.0)
				.add(Attributes.MOVEMENT_SPEED, 0.3)
				.add(Attributes.FOLLOW_RANGE, 32.0);
	}

	@Override
	protected void registerGoals() {
		goalSelector.addGoal(1, new FloatGoal(this));
		goalSelector.addGoal(2, new ShootGoal(this));
		goalSelector.addGoal(5, new WaterAvoidingRandomStrollGoal(this, 0.8));
		goalSelector.addGoal(6, new LookAtPlayerGoal(this, Player.class, 12.0f));
		goalSelector.addGoal(6, new RandomLookAroundGoal(this));
		targetSelector.addGoal(1, new HurtByTargetGoal(this));
		targetSelector.addGoal(2, new NearestAttackableTargetGoal<>(this, Player.class, true));
	}

	@Override
	public void tick() {
		super.tick();
		if (level().isClientSide()) {
			double dx = getX() - xo;
			double dz = getZ() - zo;
			boolean running = dx * dx + dz * dz > RUNNING_SQ;
			boolean aiming = isAggressive() && !running;
			if (debugAnimation != 0) {
				running = debugAnimation == 1;
				aiming = debugAnimation == 2;
			}
			runAmountO = runAmount;
			aimAmountO = aimAmount;
			runAmount += ((running ? 1f : 0f) - runAmount) * TRANSITION;
			aimAmount += ((aiming ? 1f : 0f) - aimAmount) * TRANSITION;
		}
	}

	@Override
	protected void dropCustomDeathLoot(ServerLevel level, DamageSource src, boolean killedByPlayer) {
		super.dropCustomDeathLoot(level, src, killedByPlayer);
		RandomSource rnd = getRandom();
		if (rnd.nextFloat() < GUN_DROP_CHANCE) {
			float roll = rnd.nextFloat();
			Item gun = roll < 0.30f ? KarlsonItems.PISTOL : roll < 0.55f ? KarlsonItems.UZI : roll < 0.77f ? KarlsonItems.SHOTGUN
					: roll < 0.92f ? KarlsonItems.BOOMER : KarlsonItems.GRAPPLER;
			spawnAtLocation(level, new ItemStack(gun));
		}
	}

	@Override
	public SpawnGroupData finalizeSpawn(ServerLevelAccessor level, DifficultyInstance diff, EntitySpawnReason reason, SpawnGroupData groupData) {
		SpawnGroupData data = super.finalizeSpawn(level, diff, reason, groupData);
		float roll = level.getRandom().nextFloat();
		Item gun = roll < 0.5f ? KarlsonItems.PISTOL : roll < 0.8f ? KarlsonItems.UZI : KarlsonItems.SHOTGUN;
		setItemSlot(EquipmentSlot.MAINHAND, new ItemStack(gun));
		setDropChance(EquipmentSlot.MAINHAND, 0f);
		return data;
	}

	private void shoot(LivingEntity target, GunItem.Stats stats) {
		if (!(level() instanceof ServerLevel level)) {
			return;
		}
		Vec3 eye = getEyePosition();
		Vec3 look = target.position().add(0, target.getBbHeight() * 0.5, 0).subtract(eye).normalize();
		Vec3 right = look.cross(new Vec3(0, 1, 0));
		right = right.lengthSqr() < 1.0e-6 ? new Vec3(1, 0, 0) : right.normalize();
		Vec3 up = right.cross(look);
		Vec3 origin = eye.add(look.scale(0.7)).add(right.scale(0.25)).add(up.scale(-0.2));
		RandomSource rnd = getRandom();
		double spread = stats.accuracy() + AIM_ERROR;
		for (int i = 0; i < stats.bullets(); i++) {
			Vec3 dir = look
					.add(up.scale((rnd.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * spread))
					.add(right.scale((rnd.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * spread));
			Bullets.spawn(level, this, origin, dir.scale(stats.bulletSpeed()), stats.explosive(), stats.damage() * DAMAGE_SCALE);
		}
		KarlsonServer.shotSound(level, origin, stats.explosive());
	}

	private static final class ShootGoal extends Goal {
		private final KarlsonEnemy mob;
		private int cooldown;
		private int seen;

		ShootGoal(KarlsonEnemy mob) {
			this.mob = mob;
			setFlags(EnumSet.of(Flag.MOVE, Flag.LOOK));
		}

		@Override
		public boolean canUse() {
			LivingEntity target = mob.getTarget();
			return target != null && target.isAlive() && mob.getMainHandItem().getItem() instanceof GunItem;
		}

		@Override
		public void start() {
			mob.setAggressive(true);
			cooldown = 15;
			seen = 0;
		}

		@Override
		public void stop() {
			mob.setAggressive(false);
			mob.getNavigation().stop();
		}

		@Override
		public boolean requiresUpdateEveryTick() {
			return true;
		}

		@Override
		public void tick() {
			LivingEntity target = mob.getTarget();
			if (target == null || !(mob.getMainHandItem().getItem() instanceof GunItem gun)) {
				return;
			}
			boolean sees = mob.getSensing().hasLineOfSight(target);
			seen = sees ? seen + 1 : 0;
			boolean inRange = mob.distanceToSqr(target) < RANGE * RANGE;
			if (sees && inRange) {
				mob.getNavigation().stop();
			} else {
				mob.getNavigation().moveTo(target, 1.0);
			}
			mob.getLookControl().setLookAt(target, 30.0f, 30.0f);
			if (cooldown > 0) {
				cooldown--;
			}
			if (cooldown <= 0 && sees && inRange && seen >= AIM_TICKS) {
				mob.shoot(target, gun.stats);
				double speed = gun.stats.attackSpeed();
				cooldown = (int) Math.round((speed * 2.0 + mob.getRandom().nextDouble() * speed * 4.0) * 20.0);
			}
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/item/GunItem.java ====
package karlson.item;

import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;

public class GunItem extends Item {
	public final Stats stats;

	public GunItem(Properties props, Stats stats) {
		super(props);
		this.stats = stats;
	}

	public record Stats(double attackSpeed, double force, double accuracy, int bullets, double boostRecoil, double recoil, boolean explosive, float damage) {
		public int cooldownTicks() {
			return Math.max(1, (int) Math.round(attackSpeed * 20.0));
		}

		public double bulletSpeed() {
			return 2.0 * force * KarlsonConst.FIXED_DT * KarlsonConst.SCALE;
		}

		public double boostDv() {
			return boostRecoil / KarlsonConst.MASS * KarlsonConst.SCALE;
		}
	}

	public static final Stats PISTOL = new Stats(0.4, 2000, 0.0, 1, 0, 1.0, false, 14f);
	public static final Stats UZI = new Stats(0.15, 2000, 0.05, 1, 0, 0.2, false, 7f);
	public static final Stats SHOTGUN = new Stats(1.0, 1500, 0.07, 6, 30, 0.5, false, 8f);
	public static final Stats BOOMER = new Stats(1.0, 1500, 0.0, 1, 0, 1.0, true, 0f);
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/mixin/AvatarDimensionsMixin.java ====
package karlson.mixin;

import karlson.Karlson;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Avatar;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityDimensions;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Pose;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Unique;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

@Mixin(Avatar.class)
public abstract class AvatarDimensionsMixin {
	@Unique
	private static final EntityDimensions KARLSON_SLIDE = EntityDimensions.scalable(0.6f, 0.6f).withEyeHeight(0.5f);

	@Inject(method = "getDefaultDimensions", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
	private void karlson$slideHeight(Pose pose, CallbackInfoReturnable<EntityDimensions> cir) {
		if (pose == Pose.CROUCHING && (Object) this instanceof Player player && Karlson.isKarlson(player)) {
			cir.setReturnValue(KARLSON_SLIDE);
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/mixin/client/CameraMixin.java ====
package karlson.mixin.client;

import karlson.client.CameraFx;
import net.minecraft.client.Camera;
import net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.ModifyArg;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

@Mixin(Camera.class)
public abstract class CameraMixin {
	private static final String ROTATION_YXZ = "Lorg/joml/Quaternionf;rotationYXZ(FFF)Lorg/joml/Quaternionf;";

	@Inject(method = "update", at = @At("HEAD"))
	private void karlson$frame(DeltaTracker deltaTracker, CallbackInfo ci) {
		CameraFx.frame();
	}

	@ModifyArg(method = "setRotation", at = @At(value = "INVOKE", target = ROTATION_YXZ, remap = false), index = 0)
	private float karlson$yaw(float angleY) {
		return angleY + CameraFx.yaw();
	}

	@ModifyArg(method = "setRotation", at = @At(value = "INVOKE", target = ROTATION_YXZ, remap = false), index = 1)
	private float karlson$pitch(float angleX) {
		return angleX + CameraFx.pitch();
	}

	@ModifyArg(method = "setRotation", at = @At(value = "INVOKE", target = ROTATION_YXZ, remap = false), index = 2)
	private float karlson$roll(float angleZ) {
		return angleZ + CameraFx.roll();
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/mixin/client/FirstPersonHandsMixin.java ====
package karlson.mixin.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.math.Axis;
import karlson.client.KarlsonClient;
import karlson.client.Viewmodel;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.FirstPersonHandsAndItemsRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.SubmitNodeCollector;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.FirstPersonHandsAndItemsRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.PlayerRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.texture.OverlayTexture;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import org.joml.Vector3f;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

@Mixin(FirstPersonHandsAndItemsRenderer.class)
public abstract class FirstPersonHandsMixin {
	@Inject(method = "submitArmWithItem", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
	private void karlson$viewmodel(PlayerRenderState playerState, FirstPersonHandsAndItemsRenderState state, float partialTicks, float xRot,
			InteractionHand hand, float attack, ItemStack itemStack, float inverseArmHeight, PoseStack poseStack,
			SubmitNodeCollector submitNodeCollector, int lightCoords, CallbackInfo ci) {
		if (hand != InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND || state.isScoping) {
			return;
		}
		if (!KarlsonClient.isWeapon(itemStack.getItem()) || !Viewmodel.isActive()) {
			return;
		}
		ci.cancel();
		poseStack.pushPose();
		poseStack.rotateDegrees(Axis.YP, -(state.viewYRot - state.yBob) * 0.1f);
		poseStack.rotateDegrees(Axis.XP, -(state.viewXRot - state.xBob) * 0.1f);
		poseStack.scale(Viewmodel.FOV_FIT, Viewmodel.FOV_FIT, 1f);
		Vector3f pos = Viewmodel.viewPosition();
		poseStack.translate(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z);
		poseStack.rotate(Viewmodel.localRotation());
		state.mainHandRenderState.submit(poseStack, submitNodeCollector, lightCoords, OverlayTexture.NO_OVERLAY, 0);
		poseStack.popPose();
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/mixin/client/ItemEntityRendererMixin.java ====
package karlson.mixin.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import karlson.client.GunDropState;
import karlson.client.GunDrops;
import karlson.client.GunMesh;
import karlson.client.KarlsonClient;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.SubmitNodeCollector;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.ItemEntityRenderer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.entity.state.ItemEntityRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.level.CameraRenderState;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.item.ItemEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

@Mixin(ItemEntityRenderer.class)
public abstract class ItemEntityRendererMixin {
	@Inject(method = "extractRenderState(Lnet/minecraft/world/entity/item/ItemEntity;Lnet/minecraft/client/renderer/entity/state/ItemEntityRenderState;F)V",
			at = @At("TAIL"))
	private void karlson$gunState(ItemEntity entity, ItemEntityRenderState state, float partialTicks, CallbackInfo ci) {
		GunDropState drop = (GunDropState) state;
		Item item = entity.getItem().getItem();
		drop.karlson$setGun(KarlsonClient.isWeapon(item) ? GunMesh.get(BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.getKey(item).getPath()) : null);
		drop.karlson$setResting(entity.onGround());
	}

	@Inject(method = "submit(Lnet/minecraft/client/renderer/entity/state/ItemEntityRenderState;Lcom/mojang/blaze3d/vertex/PoseStack;Lnet/minecraft/client/renderer/SubmitNodeCollector;Lnet/minecraft/client/renderer/state/level/CameraRenderState;)V",
			at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
	private void karlson$gunOnTheGround(ItemEntityRenderState state, PoseStack poseStack, SubmitNodeCollector collector, CameraRenderState camera, CallbackInfo ci) {
		if (((GunDropState) state).karlson$gun() != null) {
			GunDrops.submit(state, poseStack, collector, camera);
			ci.cancel();
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/mixin/client/KeyboardInputMixin.java ====
package karlson.mixin.client;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import karlson.client.KarlsonMovement;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.keymapping.v1.KeyMappingHelper;
import net.minecraft.client.KeyMapping;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.ClientInput;
import net.minecraft.client.player.KeyboardInput;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Unique;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

@Mixin(KeyboardInput.class)
public abstract class KeyboardInputMixin {
	@Inject(method = "tick", at = @At("TAIL"))
	private void karlson$slideKeys(CallbackInfo ci) {
		Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
		LocalPlayer player = mc.player;
		if (player == null || !KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.shouldControl(player)) {
			return;
		}
		ClientInput self = (ClientInput) (Object) this;
		Input keys = self.keyPresses;
		boolean slide = karlson$held(mc, mc.options.keyShift, mc.options.toggleCrouch().get())
				|| karlson$held(mc, mc.options.keySprint, mc.options.toggleSprint().get());
		if (slide != keys.shift() || keys.sprint()) {
			self.keyPresses = new Input(keys.forward(), keys.backward(), keys.left(), keys.right(), keys.jump(), slide, false);
		}
	}

	@Unique
	private static boolean karlson$held(Minecraft mc, KeyMapping key, boolean toggles) {
		if (!toggles) {
			return key.isDown();
		}
		if (mc.gui.screen() != null) {
			return false;
		}
		InputConstants.Key bound = KeyMappingHelper.getBoundKeyOf(key);
		int value = bound.getValue();
		if (bound.getType() == InputConstants.Type.MOUSE) {
			return value == 0 ? mc.mouseHandler.isLeftPressed() : value == 1 ? mc.mouseHandler.isRightPressed() : value == 2 && mc.mouseHandler.isMiddlePressed();
		}
		return value > 0 && InputConstants.isKeyDown(value);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/mixin/client/MinecraftMusicMixin.java ====
package karlson.mixin.client;

import com.llamalad7.mixinextras.injector.wrapoperation.Operation;
import com.llamalad7.mixinextras.injector.wrapoperation.WrapOperation;
import karlson.client.KarlsonMusic;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.sounds.SoundManager;
import net.minecraft.sounds.Music;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

@Mixin(Minecraft.class)
public abstract class MinecraftMusicMixin {
	@Inject(method = "getSituationalMusic", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
	private void karlson$noVanillaMusic(CallbackInfoReturnable<Music> cir) {
		if (KarlsonMusic.hasTracks()) {
			cir.setReturnValue(null);
		}
	}

	@WrapOperation(method = "updateLevelInEngines(Lnet/minecraft/client/multiplayer/ClientLevel;Z)V",
			at = @At(value = "INVOKE", target = "Lnet/minecraft/client/sounds/SoundManager;stop()V"))
	private void karlson$musicNeverStops(SoundManager sounds, Operation<Void> original) {
		if (!KarlsonMusic.isPlaying()) {
			original.call(sounds);
			return;
		}
		for (SoundSource source : SoundSource.values()) {
			if (source != SoundSource.MUSIC) {
				sounds.stop(null, source);
			}
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/mixin/client/ModelFeatureRendererMixin.java ====
package karlson.mixin.client;

import com.llamalad7.mixinextras.injector.wrapoperation.Operation;
import com.llamalad7.mixinextras.injector.wrapoperation.WrapOperation;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import karlson.client.EnemyModel;
import karlson.client.Ragdolls;
import net.minecraft.client.model.Model;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.feature.ModelFeatureRenderer;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Final;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

@Mixin(ModelFeatureRenderer.class)
public abstract class ModelFeatureRendererMixin {
	@Shadow
	@Final
	private PoseStack poseStack;

	@Inject(method = "prepareModel", at = @At(value = "INVOKE", target = "Lnet/minecraft/client/model/Model;setupAnim(Ljava/lang/Object;)V", shift = At.Shift.AFTER))
	private void karlson$ragdollPose(ModelFeatureRenderer.Submit<?> submit, CallbackInfo ci) {
		Ragdolls.pose(submit.state(), submit.model(), this.poseStack);
	}

	@WrapOperation(method = "prepareModel", at = @At(value = "INVOKE",
			target = "Lnet/minecraft/client/model/Model;renderToBuffer(Lcom/mojang/blaze3d/vertex/PoseStack;Lcom/mojang/blaze3d/vertex/VertexConsumer;III)V"))
	private void karlson$enemyMesh(Model<?> model, PoseStack poseStack, VertexConsumer buffer, int light, int overlay, int color,
			Operation<Void> original) {
		if (model instanceof EnemyModel enemy) {
			enemy.renderMesh(poseStack, buffer, light, overlay, color);
		} else {
			original.call(model, poseStack, buffer, light, overlay, color);
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/mixin/client/PlayerTravelMixin.java ====
package karlson.mixin.client;

import karlson.client.GrappleController;
import karlson.client.KarlsonMovement;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfoReturnable;

@Mixin(Player.class)
public abstract class PlayerTravelMixin {
	@Inject(method = "travel", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
	private void karlson$travel(Vec3 input, CallbackInfo ci) {
		if ((Object) this instanceof LocalPlayer player) {
			KarlsonMovement movement = KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE;
			if (movement.shouldControl(player)) {
				movement.travel(player);
				ci.cancel();
				return;
			}
			if (movement.active) {
				movement.reset();
			}
			if (GrappleController.INSTANCE.isGrappling() && !player.getAbilities().flying && !player.isPassenger() && !player.isSpectator()) {
				Vec3 velocity = player.getDeltaMovement().scale(20.0);
				Vec3 center = player.position().add(0, player.getBbHeight() * 0.5, 0);
				Vec3 dv = GrappleController.INSTANCE.springDv(center, velocity, 0.05, true);
				player.setDeltaMovement(velocity.add(dv).scale(1.0 / 20.0));
			}
		}
	}

	@Inject(method = "isStayingOnGroundSurface", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true)
	private void karlson$slideOffEdges(CallbackInfoReturnable<Boolean> cir) {
		if ((Object) this instanceof LocalPlayer player && KarlsonMovement.INSTANCE.shouldControl(player)) {
			cir.setReturnValue(false);
		}
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/net/Payloads.java ====
package karlson.net;

import karlson.Karlson;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.networking.v1.PayloadTypeRegistry;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.RegistryFriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.network.codec.StreamCodec;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.common.custom.CustomPacketPayload;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public final class Payloads {
	private Payloads() {
	}

	private static <T extends CustomPacketPayload> CustomPacketPayload.Type<T> payloadType(String name) {
		return new CustomPacketPayload.Type<>(Karlson.id(name));
	}

	private static void writeVec(RegistryFriendlyByteBuf buf, Vec3 v) {
		buf.writeDouble(v.x);
		buf.writeDouble(v.y);
		buf.writeDouble(v.z);
	}

	private static Vec3 readVec(RegistryFriendlyByteBuf buf) {
		return new Vec3(buf.readDouble(), buf.readDouble(), buf.readDouble());
	}

	public record Mode(boolean enabled) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<Mode> TYPE = payloadType("mode");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, Mode> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> b.writeBoolean(p.enabled), b -> new Mode(b.readBoolean()));

		@Override
		public Type<Mode> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public record Shoot(Vec3 target) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<Shoot> TYPE = payloadType("shoot");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, Shoot> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> writeVec(b, p.target), b -> new Shoot(readVec(b)));

		@Override
		public Type<Shoot> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public record Grapple(boolean active, Vec3 point) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<Grapple> TYPE = payloadType("grapple");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, Grapple> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> {
			b.writeBoolean(p.active);
			writeVec(b, p.point);
		}, b -> new Grapple(b.readBoolean(), readVec(b)));

		@Override
		public Type<Grapple> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public record Slam(int entityId, Vec3 velocity) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<Slam> TYPE = payloadType("slam");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, Slam> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> {
			b.writeVarInt(p.entityId);
			writeVec(b, p.velocity);
		}, b -> new Slam(b.readVarInt(), readVec(b)));

		@Override
		public Type<Slam> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public record Smash(BlockPos pos) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<Smash> TYPE = payloadType("smash");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, Smash> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> b.writeBlockPos(p.pos), b -> new Smash(b.readBlockPos()));

		@Override
		public Type<Smash> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public record BulletSpawn(int id, int ownerId, Vec3 pos, Vec3 velocity, boolean explosive) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<BulletSpawn> TYPE = payloadType("bullet_spawn");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, BulletSpawn> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> {
			b.writeVarInt(p.id);
			b.writeVarInt(p.ownerId);
			writeVec(b, p.pos);
			writeVec(b, p.velocity);
			b.writeBoolean(p.explosive);
		}, b -> new BulletSpawn(b.readVarInt(), b.readVarInt(), readVec(b), readVec(b), b.readBoolean()));

		@Override
		public Type<BulletSpawn> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public record BulletEnd(int id, Vec3 pos, int kind, Vec3 normal, int entityId) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<BulletEnd> TYPE = payloadType("bullet_end");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, BulletEnd> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> {
			b.writeVarInt(p.id);
			writeVec(b, p.pos);
			b.writeVarInt(p.kind);
			writeVec(b, p.normal);
			b.writeVarInt(p.entityId);
		}, b -> new BulletEnd(b.readVarInt(), readVec(b), b.readVarInt(), readVec(b), b.readVarInt()));

		@Override
		public Type<BulletEnd> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public record Blast(Vec3 pos, Vec3 impulse) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<Blast> TYPE = payloadType("blast");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, Blast> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> {
			writeVec(b, p.pos);
			writeVec(b, p.impulse);
		}, b -> new Blast(readVec(b), readVec(b)));

		@Override
		public Type<Blast> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public record GrappleSync(int entityId, boolean active, Vec3 point) implements CustomPacketPayload {
		public static final Type<GrappleSync> TYPE = payloadType("grapple_sync");
		public static final StreamCodec<RegistryFriendlyByteBuf, GrappleSync> CODEC = CustomPacketPayload.codec((p, b) -> {
			b.writeVarInt(p.entityId);
			b.writeBoolean(p.active);
			writeVec(b, p.point);
		}, b -> new GrappleSync(b.readVarInt(), b.readBoolean(), readVec(b)));

		@Override
		public Type<GrappleSync> type() {
			return TYPE;
		}
	}

	public static void register() {
		PayloadTypeRegistry.serverboundPlay().register(Mode.TYPE, Mode.CODEC);
		PayloadTypeRegistry.serverboundPlay().register(Shoot.TYPE, Shoot.CODEC);
		PayloadTypeRegistry.serverboundPlay().register(Grapple.TYPE, Grapple.CODEC);
		PayloadTypeRegistry.serverboundPlay().register(Slam.TYPE, Slam.CODEC);
		PayloadTypeRegistry.serverboundPlay().register(Smash.TYPE, Smash.CODEC);
		PayloadTypeRegistry.clientboundPlay().register(BulletSpawn.TYPE, BulletSpawn.CODEC);
		PayloadTypeRegistry.clientboundPlay().register(BulletEnd.TYPE, BulletEnd.CODEC);
		PayloadTypeRegistry.clientboundPlay().register(Blast.TYPE, Blast.CODEC);
		PayloadTypeRegistry.clientboundPlay().register(GrappleSync.TYPE, GrappleSync.CODEC);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/server/Bullets.java ====
package karlson.server;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.WeakHashMap;

import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.KarlsonSounds;
import karlson.entity.KarlsonEnemy;
import karlson.net.Payloads;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.networking.v1.PlayerLookup;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.networking.v1.ServerPlayNetworking;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.ProjectileUtil;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ClipContext;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.TntBlock;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.CollisionContext;

public final class Bullets {
	private Bullets() {
	}

	private static final double TICK = 0.05;
	private static final int MAX_AGE = 100;
	private static final Map<ServerLevel, List<Bullet>> ACTIVE = new WeakHashMap<>();
	private static int nextId;

	private static final class Bullet {
		final int id;
		final LivingEntity owner;
		final boolean explosive;
		final float damage;
		Vec3 pos;
		Vec3 vel;
		int age;

		Bullet(int id, LivingEntity owner, Vec3 pos, Vec3 vel, boolean explosive, float damage) {
			this.id = id;
			this.owner = owner;
			this.pos = pos;
			this.vel = vel;
			this.explosive = explosive;
			this.damage = damage;
		}
	}

	public static void spawn(ServerLevel level, LivingEntity owner, Vec3 pos, Vec3 vel, boolean explosive, float damage) {
		Bullet blt = new Bullet(nextId++, owner, pos, vel, explosive, damage);
		ACTIVE.computeIfAbsent(level, l -> new ArrayList<>()).add(blt);
		Payloads.BulletSpawn pkt = new Payloads.BulletSpawn(blt.id, owner.getId(), pos, vel, explosive);
		for (ServerPlayer player : PlayerLookup.around(level, pos, 160.0)) {
			ServerPlayNetworking.send(player, pkt);
		}
	}

	public static void tick(ServerLevel level) {
		List<Bullet> bullets = ACTIVE.get(level);
		if (bullets == null || bullets.isEmpty()) {
			return;
		}
		List<Bullet> snap = new ArrayList<>(bullets);
		bullets.clear();
		for (Iterator<Bullet> it = snap.iterator(); it.hasNext();) {
			Bullet blt = it.next();
			if (step(level, blt)) {
				it.remove();
			}
		}
		bullets.addAll(0, snap);
	}

	private static void end(ServerLevel level, Bullet blt, Vec3 pos, int kind, Vec3 normal, int entityId) {
		Payloads.BulletEnd pkt = new Payloads.BulletEnd(blt.id, pos, kind, normal, entityId);
		for (ServerPlayer player : PlayerLookup.around(level, pos, 192.0)) {
			ServerPlayNetworking.send(player, pkt);
		}
	}

	private static boolean step(ServerLevel level, Bullet blt) {
		if (++blt.age > MAX_AGE || blt.owner.isRemoved() || blt.owner.level() != level) {
			end(level, blt, blt.pos, 2, Vec3.ZERO, -1);
			return true;
		}
		if (blt.explosive) {
			blt.vel = blt.vel.add(0, -KarlsonConst.BOOMER_GRAVITY * TICK, 0);
		}
		Vec3 from = blt.pos;
		Vec3 to = from.add(blt.vel.scale(TICK));
		if (!level.isLoaded(BlockPos.containing(to))) {
			end(level, blt, from, 2, Vec3.ZERO, -1);
			return true;
		}
		float margin = (float) ((blt.explosive ? 0.14 : 0.05) * KarlsonConst.SCALE + 0.1);

		for (int pass = 0; pass < 6; pass++) {
			BlockHitResult blockHit = level.clip(new ClipContext(from, to, ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER, ClipContext.Fluid.NONE, CollisionContext.empty()));
			Vec3 reach = blockHit.getType() == HitResult.Type.MISS ? to : blockHit.getLocation();
			EntityHitResult entHit = ProjectileUtil.getEntityHitResult(level, blt.owner, from, reach, new AABB(from, reach).inflate(1.0),
					e -> e != blt.owner && e.isPickable() && e.isAlive() && !e.isSpectator() && !sameSide(blt.owner, e), margin);
			if (entHit != null) {
				hitEntity(level, blt, entHit.getEntity(), entHit.getLocation());
				return true;
			}
			if (blockHit.getType() == HitResult.Type.MISS) {
				break;
			}
			BlockPos blockPos = blockHit.getBlockPos();
			BlockState state = level.getBlockState(blockPos);
			if (!blt.explosive && KarlsonServer.isGlass(state) && mayBreak(blt, level, blockPos)) {
				level.destroyBlock(blockPos, false, blt.owner);
				level.playSound(null, blockPos, KarlsonSounds.WINDOW_BREAK, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 1.0f, 0.9f + level.getRandom().nextFloat() * 0.2f);
				from = reach;
				continue;
			}
			hitBlock(level, blt, blockPos, state, reach, blockHit);
			return true;
		}
		blt.pos = to;
		return false;
	}

	private static boolean mayBreak(Bullet blt, ServerLevel level, BlockPos pos) {
		return blt.owner instanceof Player player && player.mayInteract(level, pos);
	}

	private static boolean sameSide(Entity owner, Entity other) {
		return owner instanceof KarlsonEnemy && other instanceof KarlsonEnemy;
	}

	private static void hitEntity(ServerLevel level, Bullet blt, Entity entity, Vec3 at) {
		Vec3 back = blt.vel.normalize().scale(-1.0);
		if (blt.explosive) {
			end(level, blt, at, 1, back, entity.getId());
			KarlsonServer.explode(level, blt.owner, at);
			return;
		}
		DamageSource src = blt.owner instanceof Player player ? level.damageSources().playerAttack(player)
				: level.damageSources().mobAttack(blt.owner);
		if (blt.owner instanceof Player) {
			entity.setInvulnerableTime(0);
		}
		boolean hurt = entity.hurtServer(level, src, blt.damage);
		if (hurt && entity instanceof LivingEntity living) {
			living.knockback(0.35, back.x, back.z, src, 0.0f);
		}
		level.playSound(null, at.x, at.y, at.z, KarlsonSounds.BULLET_HIT, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, 0.9f + level.getRandom().nextFloat() * 0.2f);
		level.playSound(null, at.x, at.y, at.z, KarlsonSounds.ENEMY_HIT, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 1.0f);
		end(level, blt, at, hurt ? 1 : 0, back, entity.getId());
	}

	private static void hitBlock(ServerLevel level, Bullet blt, BlockPos blockPos, BlockState state, Vec3 at, BlockHitResult hit) {
		Vec3 normal = Vec3.atLowerCornerOf(hit.getDirection().getUnitVec3i());
		if (blt.explosive) {
			end(level, blt, at, 0, normal, -1);
			KarlsonServer.explode(level, blt.owner, at);
			return;
		}
		if (state.is(Blocks.TNT) && mayBreak(blt, level, blockPos)) {
			TntBlock.prime(level, blockPos);
			level.removeBlock(blockPos, false);
		}
		level.playSound(null, at.x, at.y, at.z, KarlsonSounds.BULLET_HIT, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, 0.9f + level.getRandom().nextFloat() * 0.2f);
		end(level, blt, at, 0, normal, -1);
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/server/Guide.java ====
package karlson.server;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import com.mojang.brigadier.CommandDispatcher;
import karlson.Karlson;
import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.KarlsonItems;
import net.minecraft.ChatFormatting;
import net.minecraft.commands.CommandSourceStack;
import net.minecraft.commands.Commands;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.ClickEvent;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.HoverEvent;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.MutableComponent;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceKey;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.server.network.Filterable;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.item.component.WrittenBookContent;
import net.minecraft.world.item.crafting.Recipe;

public final class Guide {
	private Guide() {
	}

	private static final String TAG = "karlson_guide";
	public static final String TITLE = "Karlson Guide";
	public static final String GUNS_COMMAND = "/karlson guns";
	private static final String GUIDE_COMMAND = "/karlson guide";

	private static final int ORANGE = 0xC25E00;
	private static final int KEY = 0x1F4FA8;
	private static final int LINK = 0x1F4FA8;
	private static final int GREEN = 0x0B7A1E;
	private static final int FAINT = 0x6B6B6B;

	private static final String[] RECIPES = {"pistol", "uzi", "shotgun", "boomer", "grappler"};

	static Item[] guns() {
		return new Item[] {KarlsonItems.PISTOL, KarlsonItems.UZI, KarlsonItems.SHOTGUN, KarlsonItems.BOOMER, KarlsonItems.GRAPPLER};
	}

	public static void registerCommands(CommandDispatcher<CommandSourceStack> dispatcher) {
		dispatcher.register(Commands.literal("karlson")
				.then(Commands.literal("guns").executes(context -> giveGuns(context.getSource().getPlayerOrException())))
				.then(Commands.literal("guide").executes(context -> giveBook(context.getSource().getPlayerOrException()))));
	}

	public static void join(ServerPlayer player) {
		List<ResourceKey<Recipe<?>>> recipes = new ArrayList<>();
		for (String name : RECIPES) {
			recipes.add(ResourceKey.create(Registries.RECIPE, Karlson.id(name)));
		}
		player.awardRecipesByKey(recipes);
		if (player.entityTags().contains(TAG)) {
			return;
		}
		player.addTag(TAG);
		hand(player, book());
		player.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("Karlson Guide").withStyle(style -> style.withColor(ChatFormatting.GOLD).withBold(true))
				.append(Component.literal(" added to your inventory. Open it for the moves, the guns and how to get them.")
						.withStyle(style -> style.withColor(ChatFormatting.YELLOW).withBold(false))));
	}

	private static int giveBook(ServerPlayer player) {
		hand(player, book());
		return 1;
	}

	private static int giveGuns(ServerPlayer player) {
		MutableComponent names = Component.empty();
		int given = 0;
		for (Item gun : guns()) {
			if (player.getInventory().contains(new ItemStack(gun)) || player.getOffhandItem().is(gun)) {
				continue;
			}
			ItemStack stack = new ItemStack(gun);
			Component name = stack.getHoverName();
			hand(player, stack);
			names.append(given == 0 ? "" : ", ").append(name);
			given++;
		}
		if (given == 0) {
			player.sendOverlayMessage(Component.literal("You already carry every gun.").withStyle(ChatFormatting.YELLOW));
			return 0;
		}
		player.level().playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ(), SoundEvents.ITEM_PICKUP, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.6f, 1.2f);
		player.sendOverlayMessage(Component.literal("Received: ").withStyle(ChatFormatting.GREEN).append(names));
		return given;
	}

	private static void hand(ServerPlayer player, ItemStack stack) {
		if (!player.getInventory().add(stack)) {
			player.spawnAtLocation(player.level(), stack);
		}
	}

	public static ItemStack book() {
		List<Filterable<Component>> pages = new ArrayList<>();
		for (Component page : pages()) {
			pages.add(Filterable.passThrough(page));
		}
		ItemStack stack = new ItemStack(Items.WRITTEN_BOOK);
		stack.set(DataComponents.WRITTEN_BOOK_CONTENT, new WrittenBookContent(Filterable.passThrough(TITLE), "Karlson", 0, pages, true));
		return stack;
	}

	public static List<Component> pages() {
		List<Component> pages = new ArrayList<>();

		pages.add(Component.empty()
				.append(Component.literal("KARLSON\n").withStyle(style -> style.withColor(ORANGE).withBold(true)))
				.append(faint("in Minecraft\n\n"))
				.append(link("Moving", 2))
				.append(link("More moves", 3))
				.append(link("Guns", 4))
				.append(link("Grappling Gun", 5))
				.append(link("Enemies", 6))
				.append(link("Getting the guns", 7))
				.append(link(Karlson.hasMusic() ? "Music and extras" : "Extras", 8))
				.append(faint("\nClick a line to jump.")));

		pages.add(Component.empty()
				.append(heading("Moving"))
				.append(bold("Slide: ")).append("hold ").append(key("key.sneak")).append(" or ").append(key("key.sprint")).append(". Faster downhill.\n\n")
				.append(bold("Wall run: ")).append("jump at a wall and keep running. ").append(key("key.jump")).append(" kicks you off.\n\n")
				.append(faint("Always on. No toggle.")));

		pages.add(Component.empty()
				.append(heading("More moves"))
				.append(bold("Slam: ")).append("hit an enemy while sliding or airborne.\n\n")
				.append(bold("Smash: ")).append("glass and wooden doors break if you hit them fast.\n\n")
				.append(bold("Grab: ")).append("empty hand, hold ").append(key("key.attack")).append(" on a body."));

		pages.add(Component.empty()
				.append(heading("Guns"))
				.append("Hold ").append(key("key.attack")).append(" to fire. No ammo.\n\n")
				.append(bold("Pistol")).append(" - accurate\n")
				.append(bold("Uzi")).append(" - fast spray\n")
				.append(bold("Shotgun")).append(" - big kick\n")
				.append(bold("Boomer")).append(" - explosive\n\n")
				.append("Boomer at your feet: rocket jump."));

		pages.add(Component.empty()
				.append(heading("Grappling Gun"))
				.append("Aim at a block or a mob within " + (int) KarlsonConst.GRAPPLE_RANGE + " blocks.\n\n")
				.append(bold("Swing ")).append(key("key.attack")).append("\n")
				.append(bold("Reel in ")).append(key("key.use")).append("\n")
				.append("Release to let go.\n\n")
				.append("A hooked mob drags the rope along. Works while gliding too."));

		pages.add(Component.empty()
				.append(heading("Enemies"))
				.append("Yellow gunmen spawn in the dark across the Overworld.\n\n")
				.append("Each carries a Pistol, Uzi or Shotgun and aims when he sees you.\n\n")
				.append("Shoot them, blow them up or slam into them."));

		pages.add(Component.empty()
				.append(heading("Getting the guns"))
				.append(bold("Legit: ")).append("one enemy in three drops a random gun. Or craft them from your recipe book.\n\n")
				.append(bold("Or right now:\n"))
				.append(Component.literal("\u00bb RECEIVE GUNS \u00ab").withStyle(style -> style.withColor(GREEN).withBold(true).withUnderlined(true)
						.withClickEvent(new ClickEvent.RunCommand(GUNS_COMMAND))
						.withHoverEvent(new HoverEvent.ShowText(Component.literal("Click for every gun you are not carrying")))))
				.append(faint("\n\nOptional: all five at once.")));

		MutableComponent extras = Component.empty();
		if (Karlson.hasMusic()) {
			extras.append(heading("Music and extras"))
					.append(key("key.karlson.music_down")).append(" and ").append(key("key.karlson.music_up")).append(" turn Karlson's music down and up, ")
					.append(key("key.karlson.music_next")).append(" skips the track.\n\n");
		} else {
			extras.append(heading("Extras"));
		}
		pages.add(extras
				.append("Lost this book? Type\n")
				.append(Component.literal(GUIDE_COMMAND).withStyle(style -> style.withColor(LINK).withUnderlined(true)
						.withClickEvent(new ClickEvent.RunCommand(GUIDE_COMMAND))
						.withHoverEvent(new HoverEvent.ShowText(Component.literal("Click for another copy")))))
				.append("\n\nThe creative Karlson tab has every gun and a spawn egg."));
		return pages;
	}

	private static Component heading(String text) {
		return Component.literal(text + "\n\n").withStyle(style -> style.withColor(ORANGE).withBold(true));
	}

	private static Component bold(String text) {
		return Component.literal(text).withStyle(style -> style.withBold(true));
	}

	private static Component faint(String text) {
		return Component.literal(text).withStyle(style -> style.withColor(FAINT).withItalic(true));
	}

	private static Component key(String mapping) {
		return Component.keybind(mapping).withStyle(style -> style.withColor(KEY));
	}

	private static Component link(String text, int page) {
		return Component.literal(page + "  " + text + "\n").withStyle(style -> style.withColor(LINK)
				.withClickEvent(new ClickEvent.ChangePage(page))
				.withHoverEvent(new HoverEvent.ShowText(Component.literal("Page " + page))));
	}
}

==== src/main/java/karlson/server/KarlsonServer.java ====
package karlson.server;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

import karlson.GunGeometry;
import karlson.KarlsonConst;
import karlson.KarlsonItems;
import karlson.KarlsonSounds;
import karlson.item.GunItem;
import karlson.net.Payloads;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.command.v2.CommandRegistrationCallback;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.event.lifecycle.v1.ServerTickEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.networking.v1.PlayerLookup;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.networking.v1.ServerPlayConnectionEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.networking.v1.ServerPlayNetworking;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.server.MinecraftServer;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvent;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.tags.BlockTags;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Enemy;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ClipContext;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Explosion;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ExplosionDamageCalculator;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ServerExplosion;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.shapes.CollisionContext;

public final class KarlsonServer {
	private KarlsonServer() {
	}

	private static final Set<UUID> KARLSON_MODE = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
	private static final Map<ShotKey, Long> LAST_SHOT = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
	private static final Map<UUID, Vec3> LAST_POS = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

	private static final ExplosionDamageCalculator BLAST = new ExplosionDamageCalculator() {
		@Override
		public boolean shouldDamageEntity(Explosion explosion, Entity entity) {
			return !(entity instanceof Player player && isKarlson(player));
		}

		@Override
		public float getKnockbackMultiplier(Entity entity) {
			return entity instanceof Player ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
		}
	};

	private record ShotKey(UUID player, GunItem gun) {
	}

	public static boolean isKarlson(Player player) {
		return KARLSON_MODE.contains(player.getUUID());
	}

	public static void init() {
		ServerPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.Mode.TYPE, (pkt, context) -> {
			ServerPlayer player = context.player();
			if (pkt.enabled()) {
				KARLSON_MODE.add(player.getUUID());
			} else {
				KARLSON_MODE.remove(player.getUUID());
			}
			player.refreshDimensions();
		});
		ServerPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.Shoot.TYPE, (pkt, context) -> shoot(context.player(), pkt.target()));
		ServerPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.Grapple.TYPE, (pkt, context) -> grapple(context.player(), pkt));
		ServerPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.Slam.TYPE, (pkt, context) -> slam(context.player(), pkt));
		ServerPlayNetworking.registerGlobalReceiver(Payloads.Smash.TYPE, (pkt, context) -> smash(context.player(), pkt.pos()));
		ServerPlayConnectionEvents.JOIN.register((listener, sender, server) -> {
			Guide.join(listener.player);
		});
		ServerPlayConnectionEvents.DISCONNECT.register((listener, server) -> {
			UUID uuid = listener.player.getUUID();
			KARLSON_MODE.remove(uuid);
			LAST_POS.remove(uuid);
			LAST_SHOT.keySet().removeIf(key -> key.player().equals(uuid));
		});
		CommandRegistrationCallback.EVENT.register((dispatcher, registries, environment) -> Guide.registerCommands(dispatcher));
		ServerTickEvents.END_LEVEL_TICK.register(Bullets::tick);
		ServerTickEvents.END_SERVER_TICK.register(KarlsonServer::hunger);
	}

	private static void hunger(MinecraftServer server) {
		for (ServerPlayer player : server.getPlayerList().getPlayers()) {
			Vec3 pos = player.position();
			Vec3 last = LAST_POS.put(player.getUUID(), pos);
			if (last == null || !isKarlson(player) || player.isFallFlying() || player.getAbilities().flying || player.isPassenger()
					|| player.isInWater()) {
				continue;
			}
			double dist = Math.hypot(pos.x - last.x, pos.z - last.z);
			if (dist > 0.01 && dist < 5.0) {
				player.causeFoodExhaustion((float) (dist * KarlsonConst.RUN_EXHAUSTION));
			}
		}
	}

	private static void sound(ServerLevel level, Vec3 pos, SoundEvent sound, float volume, float pitch) {
		level.playSound(null, pos.x, pos.y, pos.z, sound, SoundSource.PLAYERS, volume, pitch);
	}

	private static void shoot(ServerPlayer player, Vec3 target) {
		ItemStack stack = player.getMainHandItem();
		if (!(stack.getItem() instanceof GunItem gun) || player.isSpectator() || !player.isAlive()) {
			return;
		}
		if (!Double.isFinite(target.lengthSqr())) {
			return;
		}
		GunItem.Stats stats = gun.stats;
		long now = player.level().getServer().getTickCount();
		ShotKey key = new ShotKey(player.getUUID(), gun);
		Long last = LAST_SHOT.get(key);
		if (last != null && now >= last && now - last < stats.cooldownTicks() - 2) {
			return;
		}
		LAST_SHOT.put(key, now);

		ServerLevel level = player.level();
		Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
		Vec3 look = target.subtract(eye);
		if (look.lengthSqr() < 1.0e-6) {
			look = player.getLookAngle();
		}
		double reach = look.length();
		look = look.normalize();
		Vec3 right = look.cross(new Vec3(0, 1, 0));
		right = right.lengthSqr() < 1.0e-6 ? new Vec3(1, 0, 0) : right.normalize();
		Vec3 up = right.cross(look);

		Vec3 muzzle = GunGeometry.muzzle(gun);
		double out = Math.clamp((reach - 0.75 * muzzle.z) / muzzle.z, 0.0, 1.0);
		Vec3 origin = eye.add(right.scale(muzzle.x * out)).add(up.scale(muzzle.y * out)).add(look.scale(muzzle.z * out));
		if (level.clip(new ClipContext(eye, origin, ClipContext.Block.COLLIDER, ClipContext.Fluid.NONE, CollisionContext.empty())).getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS) {
			origin = eye;
		}
		Vec3 aim = target.subtract(origin);
		aim = aim.lengthSqr() < muzzle.z * muzzle.z ? look : aim.normalize();

		RandomSource rnd = level.getRandom();
		double speed = stats.bulletSpeed();
		for (int i = 0; i < stats.bullets(); i++) {
			Vec3 dir = aim
					.add(up.scale((rnd.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * stats.accuracy()))
					.add(right.scale((rnd.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * stats.accuracy()));
			Bullets.spawn(level, player, origin, dir.scale(speed), stats.explosive(), stats.damage());
		}

		shotSound(level, origin, stats.explosive());
	}

	public static void shotSound(ServerLevel level, Vec3 origin, boolean explosive) {
		if (explosive) {
			sound(level, origin, KarlsonSounds.THUMP, 1.0f, 1.0f);
		} else {
			RandomSource rnd = level.getRandom();
			sound(level, origin, KarlsonSounds.SHOOT_BASS, 0.7f, 0.7f + rnd.nextFloat() * 0.6f);
			sound(level, origin, KarlsonSounds.SHOOT_HIGH, 0.7f, 0.7f + rnd.nextFloat() * 0.6f);
			sound(level, origin, KarlsonSounds.SHOOT_LOW, 0.7f, 0.7f + rnd.nextFloat() * 0.6f);
		}
	}

	private static void grapple(ServerPlayer player, Payloads.Grapple pkt) {
		Payloads.GrappleSync sync = new Payloads.GrappleSync(player.getId(), pkt.active(), pkt.point());
		for (ServerPlayer other : PlayerLookup.tracking(player)) {
			if (other != player) {
				ServerPlayNetworking.send(other, sync);
			}
		}
		if (pkt.active()) {
			sound(player.level(), player.position(), KarlsonSounds.GRAPPLE, 1.0f, 0.8f + player.getRandom().nextFloat() * 0.4f);
		}
	}

	private static void slam(ServerPlayer player, Payloads.Slam pkt) {
		ServerLevel level = player.level();
		Entity entity = level.getEntity(pkt.entityId());
		if (!(entity instanceof LivingEntity target) || !(entity instanceof Enemy) || !target.isAlive() || player.isSpectator()) {
			return;
		}
		if (target.distanceToSqr(player) > 5.0 * 5.0) {
			return;
		}
		Vec3 velocity = pkt.velocity();
		double speed = Math.min(velocity.length(), 60.0);
		if (!(speed >= KarlsonConst.SLAM_MIN_SPEED)) {
			return;
		}
		DamageSource src = level.damageSources().playerAttack(player);
		if (target.hurtServer(level, src, (float) (4.0 + speed))) {
			Vec3 dir = velocity.normalize();
			target.knockback(Math.min(0.4 + speed * 0.06, 2.0), -dir.x, -dir.z, src, 0.0f);
			sound(level, target.position(), KarlsonSounds.ENEMY_HIT, 1.0f, 1.0f);
		}
	}

	public static boolean isGlass(BlockState state) {
		return BuiltInRegistries.BLOCK.getKey(state.getBlock()).getPath().contains("glass");
	}

	public static boolean isBreakableDoor(BlockState state) {
		return state.is(BlockTags.WOODEN_DOORS) || state.is(BlockTags.WOODEN_TRAPDOORS);
	}

	private static void smash(ServerPlayer player, BlockPos pos) {
		ServerLevel level = player.level();
		if (player.isSpectator() || !level.isLoaded(pos) || !player.mayInteract(level, pos)) {
			return;
		}
		if (pos.distToCenterSqr(player.position()) > 4.5 * 4.5) {
			return;
		}
		BlockState state = level.getBlockState(pos);
		Vec3 center = Vec3.atCenterOf(pos);
		if (isGlass(state)) {
			level.destroyBlock(pos, false, player);
			sound(level, center, KarlsonSounds.WINDOW_BREAK, 1.0f, 1.0f);
		} else if (isBreakableDoor(state)) {
			level.destroyBlock(pos, true, player);
			sound(level, center, KarlsonSounds.DESTRUCTION, 0.8f, 1.0f);
		}
	}

	public static void explode(ServerLevel level, Entity owner, Vec3 pos) {
		new ServerExplosion(level, owner, null, BLAST, pos, KarlsonConst.BOOMER_POWER, false, Explosion.BlockInteraction.DESTROY).explode();
		sound(level, pos, KarlsonSounds.EXPLOSION, 4.0f * 0.8f, 0.94f);
		sound(level, pos, KarlsonSounds.EXPLOSION_HIGH, 4.0f * 0.8f, 0.94f);
		sound(level, pos, KarlsonSounds.EXPLOSION_MAGIC, 4.0f * 0.411f, 0.94f);
		double radius = KarlsonConst.EXPLOSION_RADIUS;
		for (ServerPlayer player : level.players()) {
			Vec3 center = player.position().add(0, player.getBbHeight() * 0.5, 0);
			double dist = center.distanceTo(pos);
			if (dist > 96.0) {
				continue;
			}
			Vec3 impulse = Vec3.ZERO;
			if (dist <= radius + player.getBbWidth() * 0.5 && !player.isSpectator()) {
				Vec3 dir = dist < 1.0e-4 ? new Vec3(0, 1, 0) : center.subtract(pos).scale(1.0 / dist);
				impulse = dir.scale(KarlsonConst.EXPLOSION_IMPULSE / Math.max(dist, KarlsonConst.EXPLOSION_MIN_DIST));
			}
			ServerPlayNetworking.send(player, new Payloads.Blast(pos, impulse));
		}
	}
}
