==== tools/build_enemy_assets.py ====
"""Turn Karlson's enemy (the skinned "Cube" mesh on every Enemy in the levels) into the mod's enemy model.

    python tools/build_enemy_assets.py src/main/resources

The mesh is put in its bind pose, cut into the six parts a Minecraft humanoid animates (head, body, arms,
legs) by each triangle's dominant bones, and the limbs are turned to hang straight down from their joints,
which is the rest pose Minecraft's animations expect. Units are model pixels (1/16 block), y down, feet at 24.

File format: "enemy <height> <armLength>", then for each part
"part <name> <pivot xyz> <min xyz> <max xyz> <triangles>" followed by three "x y z nx ny nz u v" lines per
triangle, relative to the pivot. Needs UnityPy and numpy.
"""
import os
import re
import sys

import numpy as np
import UnityPy
from UnityPy.helpers.MeshHelper import MeshHandler

DATA = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "..", "Karlson", "Karlson_Data")
HEIGHT = 1.95 * 16.0
PARTS = ["head", "body", "right_arm", "left_arm", "right_leg", "left_leg"]


def load():
    files = [os.path.join(DATA, f) for f in os.listdir(DATA) if f.endswith(".assets") or re.fullmatch(r"level\d+", f)]
    env = UnityPy.load(*files)
    byid = {(o.assets_file.name.lower(), o.path_id): o for o in env.objects}

    def resolve(o, ptr):
        if not ptr or ptr.get("m_PathID", 0) == 0:
            return None
        f = o.assets_file
        if ptr["m_FileID"] == 0:
            return byid.get((f.name.lower(), ptr["m_PathID"]))
        name = f.externals[ptr["m_FileID"] - 1].path.split("/")[-1].lower()
        return byid.get((name, ptr["m_PathID"]))

    for o in env.objects:
        if o.type.name == "SkinnedMeshRenderer":
            return o, resolve
    raise SystemExit("no skinned mesh in Karlson's data")


def trs(t):
    p, q, s = t["m_LocalPosition"], t["m_LocalRotation"], t["m_LocalScale"]
    x, y, z, w = q["x"], q["y"], q["z"], q["w"]
    r = np.array([
        [1 - 2 * (y * y + z * z), 2 * (x * y - z * w), 2 * (x * z + y * w)],
        [2 * (x * y + z * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + z * z), 2 * (y * z - x * w)],
        [2 * (x * z - y * w), 2 * (y * z + x * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + y * y)],
    ])
    m = np.eye(4)
    m[:3, :3] = r * np.array([s["x"], s["y"], s["z"]])
    m[:3, 3] = [p["x"], p["y"], p["z"]]
    return m


def part_of(bone):
    side = "right" if bone.endswith(".R") else "left" if bone.endswith(".L") else None
    if re.search(r"Arm|Hand|Elbow", bone) and side:
        return side + "_arm"
    if re.search(r"Leg|Toe|Knee", bone) and side:
        return side + "_leg"
    if re.search(r"Neck|Head", bone):
        return "head"
    return "body"


def rotation_between(a, b):
    a = a / np.linalg.norm(a)
    b = b / np.linalg.norm(b)
    v = np.cross(a, b)
    c = float(np.dot(a, b))
    if np.linalg.norm(v) < 1e-8:
        return np.eye(3)
    k = np.array([[0, -v[2], v[1]], [v[2], 0, -v[0]], [-v[1], v[0], 0]])
    return np.eye(3) + k + k @ k / (1 + c)


def main(resources):
    smr, resolve = load()
    tree = smr.read_typetree()
    mesh_obj = resolve(smr, tree["m_Mesh"])
    handler = MeshHandler(mesh_obj.read())
    handler.process()
    verts = np.array(handler.m_Vertices, dtype=float)
    normals = np.array(handler.m_Normals, dtype=float)
    uvs = np.array(handler.m_UV0, dtype=float)[:, :2]
    weights = np.array(handler.m_BoneWeights, dtype=float)
    indices = np.array(handler.m_BoneIndices, dtype=int)
    tris = np.array(handler.get_triangles()[0], dtype=int).reshape(-1, 3)
    binds = [np.array([[b["e%d%d" % (r, c)] for c in range(4)] for r in range(4)]) for b in mesh_obj.read_typetree()["m_BindPose"]]

    def game_object(transform):
        return resolve(transform, transform.read_typetree()["m_GameObject"]).read_typetree()

    def has_animator(transform):
        return any(resolve(transform, c["component"]).type.name == "Animator" for c in game_object(transform)["m_Component"])

    cache = {}
    root_scale = 1.0

    def world(transform):
        nonlocal root_scale
        key = transform.path_id
        if key not in cache:
            t = transform.read_typetree()
            father = resolve(transform, t["m_Father"])
            if father is None or has_animator(transform):
                cache[key] = np.eye(4)
                if father is not None:
                    root_scale = father.read_typetree()["m_LocalScale"]["y"]
            else:
                cache[key] = world(father) @ trs(t)
        return cache[key]

    names, mats = [], []
    for ptr in tree["m_Bones"]:
        bone = resolve(smr, ptr)
        names.append(game_object(bone)["m_Name"])
        mats.append(world(bone))
    skin = [mats[i] @ binds[i] for i in range(len(mats))]
    bone_pos = {names[i]: mats[i][:3, 3] for i in range(len(mats))}

    pos = np.zeros_like(verts)
    nrm = np.zeros_like(normals)
    for i in range(len(verts)):
        for w, b in zip(weights[i], indices[i]):
            if w > 0:
                pos[i] += w * (skin[b] @ np.append(verts[i], 1.0))[:3]
                nrm[i] += w * (skin[b][:3, :3] @ normals[i])
    nrm /= np.maximum(np.linalg.norm(nrm, axis=1, keepdims=True), 1e-9)

    ground = pos[:, 1].min()
    height = pos[:, 1].max() - ground
    k = HEIGHT / height
    low = pos[pos[:, 1] < ground + height * 0.05]
    facing = 1.0 if low[:, 2].mean() >= bone_pos["Hips"][2] else -1.0

    def to_model(p):
        return np.array([-facing * p[0] * k, 24.0 - (p[1] - ground) * k, -facing * p[2] * k])

    def to_model_dir(n):
        return np.array([-facing * n[0], -n[1], -facing * n[2]])

    mpos = np.array([to_model(p) for p in pos])
    mnrm = np.array([to_model_dir(n) for n in nrm])
    joints = {name: to_model(p) for name, p in bone_pos.items()}

    if os.environ.get("ENEMY_DEBUG"):
        dom = [names[indices[i][int(np.argmax(weights[i]))]] for i in range(len(verts))]
        for name in names:
            sel = np.array([mpos[i] for i in range(len(verts)) if dom[i] == name])
            print("%-20s joint %s  verts %3d  %s" % (name, np.round(joints[name], 1), len(sel),
                  "" if not len(sel) else "%s .. %s" % (np.round(sel.min(0), 1), np.round(sel.max(0), 1))))

    vert_part = [part_of(names[indices[i][int(np.argmax(weights[i]))]]) for i in range(len(verts))]
    right_x = np.mean([mpos[i][0] for i in range(len(verts)) if vert_part[i] == "right_arm"])
    swap = right_x > 0
    if swap:
        flip = {"right_arm": "left_arm", "left_arm": "right_arm", "right_leg": "left_leg", "left_leg": "right_leg"}
        vert_part = [flip.get(p, p) for p in vert_part]

    def joint(side, base):
        suffix = ".R" if (side == "right") != swap else ".L"
        return joints[base + suffix]

    frames = {"body": (np.zeros(3), np.eye(3)), "head": (joints["Neck"], np.eye(3))}
    arm_length = 0.0
    for side in ("right", "left"):
        shoulder, hand = joint(side, "UpperArm"), joint(side, "Hand")
        frames[side + "_arm"] = (shoulder, rotation_between(hand - shoulder, np.array([0.0, 1.0, 0.0])))
        arm_length = float(np.linalg.norm(hand - shoulder))
        frames[side + "_leg"] = (joint(side, "UpperLeg"), np.eye(3))

    out = {p: [] for p in PARTS}
    for tri in tris:
        votes = [vert_part[i] for i in tri]
        part = max(set(votes), key=votes.count)
        pivot, rot = frames[part]
        for i in tri:
            p = rot @ (mpos[i] - pivot)
            n = rot @ mnrm[i]
            out[part].append((p[0], p[1], p[2], n[0], n[1], n[2], uvs[i][0], 1.0 - uvs[i][1]))

    folder = os.path.join(resources, "assets", "karlson", "enemymesh")
    os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(folder, "enemy.txt"), "w", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write("enemy %.3f %.3f\n" % (height * k, arm_length))
        for part in PARTS:
            rows = np.array(out[part])
            pivot = frames[part][0]
            lo, hi = rows[:, :3].min(0), rows[:, :3].max(0)
            f.write("part %s %s %s %s %d\n" % (part, " ".join("%.3f" % v for v in pivot), " ".join("%.3f" % v for v in lo),
                                              " ".join("%.3f" % v for v in hi), len(rows) // 3))
            for row in rows:
                f.write(" ".join("%.4f" % v for v in row) + "\n")
            print("%-9s pivot %s  box %s .. %s  %d tris" % (part, np.round(pivot, 2), np.round(lo, 2), np.round(hi, 2), len(rows) // 3))

    material = resolve(smr, tree["m_Materials"][0]).read_typetree()
    for name, tex in material["m_SavedProperties"]["m_TexEnvs"]:
        if name == "_MainTex":
            texture = resolve(resolve(smr, tree["m_Materials"][0]), tex["m_Texture"])
            texture.read().image.convert("RGBA").save(os.path.join(resources, "assets", "karlson", "textures", "entity", "enemy.png"))
    print("height %.2f px (%.2f blocks), arm %.2f px, facing %+d, swapped sides %s, scale in its level %.3f"
          % (height * k, height * k / 16.0, arm_length, facing, swap, root_scale))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main(sys.argv[1])

==== tools/build_enemy_rig.py ====
"""Export Karlson's enemy as he really is: the skinned mesh, its skeleton and the Animator's three clips.

    python tools/build_enemy_rig.py src/main/resources

build_enemy_assets.py cuts the mesh into rigid parts for the ragdoll; this writes what the living enemy is drawn
from, so he moves with Karlson's own animations (Idle, Walk - the arms-back run - and Aim).

Everything is in the space of the object that carries the Enemy script (x right, y up, z forward, Unity's
left-handed axes). File format (assets/karlson/enemymesh/rig.txt):

    rig <transforms> <bones> <vertices> <triangles> <gun> <head> <rightArm> <leftArm> <unitsPerGunBlock>
    root <12 floats>                     3x4 matrix from that space to model space (blocks, y down, face to -z)
    t <parent> <name> px py pz qx qy qz qw sx sy sz     one per transform, parents first
    b <transform> <12 floats>            one per bone: the mesh's bind matrix
    v x y z nx ny nz u v i0 i1 i2 i3 w0 w1 w2 w3
    f a b c
    clip <name> <frames> <seconds> <bound transforms...>
    k px py pz qx qy qz qw ...           one line per frame, seven numbers per bound transform

Needs UnityPy and numpy.
"""
import math
import os
import re
import struct
import sys
import zlib

import numpy as np
import UnityPy
from UnityPy.helpers.MeshHelper import MeshHandler

DATA = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "..", "Karlson", "Karlson_Data")
HEIGHT = 1.95
SCALE = 1.8 / 3.4
FPS = 30.0
DEBUG = bool(os.environ.get("ENEMY_DEBUG"))


def quat_mat(q):
    x, y, z, w = q
    return np.array([
        [1 - 2 * (y * y + z * z), 2 * (x * y - z * w), 2 * (x * z + y * w)],
        [2 * (x * y + z * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + z * z), 2 * (y * z - x * w)],
        [2 * (x * z - y * w), 2 * (y * z + x * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + y * y)],
    ])


def quat_mul(a, b):
    ax, ay, az, aw = a
    bx, by, bz, bw = b
    return (aw * bx + ax * bw + ay * bz - az * by,
            aw * by - ax * bz + ay * bw + az * bx,
            aw * bz + ax * by - ay * bx + az * bw,
            aw * bw - ax * bx - ay * by - az * bz)


def euler_quat(x, y, z):
    """Unity's Quaternion.Euler: z first, then x, then y. Angles in radians."""
    qx = (math.sin(x / 2), 0, 0, math.cos(x / 2))
    qy = (0, math.sin(y / 2), 0, math.cos(y / 2))
    qz = (0, 0, math.sin(z / 2), math.cos(z / 2))
    return quat_mul(qy, quat_mul(qx, qz))


def f32(word):
    return struct.unpack("<f", struct.pack("<I", word & 0xFFFFFFFF))[0]


class Clip:
    def __init__(self, tree):
        self.name = tree["m_Name"]
        muscle = tree["m_MuscleClip"]
        self.stop = muscle["m_StopTime"]
        clip = muscle["m_Clip"]["data"] if "data" in muscle["m_Clip"] else muscle["m_Clip"]
        words = clip["m_StreamedClip"]["data"]
        self.streamed_count = clip["m_StreamedClip"]["curveCount"]
        self.frames = []
        pos = 0
        while pos < len(words):
            time = f32(words[pos])
            count = words[pos + 1]
            pos += 2
            keys = []
            for _ in range(count):
                keys.append((words[pos], [f32(w) for w in words[pos + 1:pos + 5]]))
                pos += 5
            self.frames.append((time, keys))
        dense = clip["m_DenseClip"]
        self.dense_count = dense["m_CurveCount"]
        self.dense = dense
        self.constant = clip["m_ConstantClip"]["data"]
        self.bindings = tree["m_ClipBindingConstant"]["genericBindings"]

    def values(self, t):
        """Every curve's value at time t, in binding order."""
        current = {}
        for time, keys in self.frames:
            if time > t:
                break
            for index, coeff in keys:
                current[index] = (time, coeff)
        out = []
        for i in range(self.streamed_count):
            time, c = current[i]
            if math.isinf(time):
                out.append(c[3])
            else:
                dt = t - time
                out.append(((c[0] * dt + c[1]) * dt + c[2]) * dt + c[3])
        if self.dense_count:
            frame = (t - self.dense["m_BeginTime"]) * self.dense["m_SampleRate"]
            lo = int(max(0, min(self.dense["m_FrameCount"] - 1, math.floor(frame))))
            hi = min(self.dense["m_FrameCount"] - 1, lo + 1)
            k = frame - lo
            samples = self.dense["m_SampleArray"]
            for i in range(self.dense_count):
                out.append(samples[lo * self.dense_count + i] * (1 - k) + samples[hi * self.dense_count + i] * k)
        out.extend(self.constant)
        return out


def main(resources):
    files = [os.path.join(DATA, f) for f in os.listdir(DATA) if f.endswith(".assets") or re.fullmatch(r"level\d+", f)]
    env = UnityPy.load(*files)
    byid = {(o.assets_file.name.lower(), o.path_id): o for o in env.objects}

    def resolve(o, ptr):
        if not ptr or ptr.get("m_PathID", 0) == 0:
            return None
        f = o.assets_file
        if ptr["m_FileID"] == 0:
            return byid.get((f.name.lower(), ptr["m_PathID"]))
        name = f.externals[ptr["m_FileID"] - 1].path.split("/")[-1].lower()
        return byid.get((name, ptr["m_PathID"]))

    smr = next(o for o in env.objects if o.type.name == "SkinnedMeshRenderer")
    tree = smr.read_typetree()
    mesh_obj = resolve(smr, tree["m_Mesh"])
    handler = MeshHandler(mesh_obj.read())
    handler.process()
    verts = np.array(handler.m_Vertices, dtype=float)
    normals = np.array(handler.m_Normals, dtype=float)
    uvs = np.array(handler.m_UV0, dtype=float)[:, :2]
    weights = np.array(handler.m_BoneWeights, dtype=float)
    indices = np.array(handler.m_BoneIndices, dtype=int)
    tris = np.array(handler.get_triangles()[0], dtype=int).reshape(-1, 3)
    binds = [np.array([[b["e%d%d" % (r, c)] for c in range(4)] for r in range(4)]) for b in mesh_obj.read_typetree()["m_BindPose"]]

    trees = {}

    def tt(o):
        key = (o.assets_file.name, o.path_id)
        if key not in trees:
            trees[key] = o.read_typetree()
        return trees[key]

    def game_object(transform):
        return tt(resolve(transform, tt(transform)["m_GameObject"]))

    def components(transform):
        return [resolve(transform, c["component"]) for c in game_object(transform)["m_Component"]]

    renderer_transform = next(c for c in [resolve(smr, c["component"]) for c in tt(resolve(smr, tree["m_GameObject"]))["m_Component"]]
                              if c.type.name == "Transform")
    root = renderer_transform
    while resolve(root, tt(root)["m_Father"]) is not None:
        root = resolve(root, tt(root)["m_Father"])
    root_scale = tt(root)["m_LocalScale"]["y"]

    order, parent, names, paths = [], {}, {}, {}
    animator = None

    def walk(transform, up):
        nonlocal animator
        key = transform.path_id
        order.append(transform)
        parent[key] = up
        names[key] = game_object(transform)["m_Name"]
        if any(c.type.name == "Animator" for c in components(transform)):
            animator = transform
        for child in tt(transform)["m_Children"]:
            walk(resolve(transform, child), key)

    for child in tt(root)["m_Children"]:
        walk(resolve(root, child), None)
    index = {t.path_id: i for i, t in enumerate(order)}

    def path_from_animator(transform):
        parts = []
        key = transform.path_id
        while key is not None and key != animator.path_id:
            parts.append(names[key])
            key = parent[key]
        return "/".join(reversed(parts)) if key is not None else None

    by_hash = {}
    for t in order:
        p = path_from_animator(t)
        if p is not None:
            by_hash[zlib.crc32(p.encode()) & 0xFFFFFFFF] = t

    def local(t):
        d = tt(t)
        p, q, s = d["m_LocalPosition"], d["m_LocalRotation"], d["m_LocalScale"]
        return [p["x"], p["y"], p["z"]], [q["x"], q["y"], q["z"], q["w"]], [s["x"], s["y"], s["z"]]

    rest = [local(t) for t in order]

    def matrices(pose):
        out = []
        for i, t in enumerate(order):
            p, q, s = pose[i]
            m = np.eye(4)
            m[:3, :3] = quat_mat(q) * np.array(s)
            m[:3, 3] = p
            up = parent[t.path_id]
            out.append(m if up is None else out[index[up]] @ m)
        return out

    bone_index = [index[resolve(smr, ptr).path_id] for ptr in tree["m_Bones"]]

    def skinned(pose):
        world = matrices(pose)
        skin = [world[bone_index[i]] @ binds[i] for i in range(len(binds))]
        pos = np.zeros_like(verts)
        for i in range(len(verts)):
            for w, b in zip(weights[i], indices[i]):
                if w > 0:
                    pos[i] += w * (skin[b] @ np.append(verts[i], 1.0))[:3]
        return pos, world

    pos, world = skinned(rest)
    ground = pos[:, 1].min()
    height = pos[:, 1].max() - ground
    k = HEIGHT / height
    root_matrix = np.array([[-k, 0, 0, 0], [0, -k, 0, 1.5 + ground * k], [0, 0, -k, 0]])

    enemy_fields = None
    for c in components(root):
        if c.type.name != "MonoBehaviour":
            continue
        raw = c.get_raw_data()
        script = resolve(c, {"m_FileID": struct.unpack_from("<i", raw, 16)[0], "m_PathID": struct.unpack_from("<q", raw, 20)[0]})
        if script is not None and tt(script)["m_ClassName"] == "Enemy":
            off = 32 + 4
            ptrs = []
            for _ in range(8):
                fid, pid = struct.unpack_from("<iq", raw, off)
                off += 12
                ptrs.append(resolve(c, {"m_FileID": fid, "m_PathID": pid}))
            enemy_fields = dict(zip(["startGun", "gunPosition", "currentGun", "leftArm", "rightArm", "head", "hips", "player"], ptrs))
    if enemy_fields is None:
        raise SystemExit("no Enemy script on " + game_object(root)["m_Name"])
    special = {name: index[enemy_fields[name].path_id] for name in ("gunPosition", "head", "rightArm", "leftArm")}

    clips = []
    for o in env.objects:
        if o.type.name == "AnimationClip":
            clip = Clip(o.read_typetree())
            if clip.name in ("Idle", "Walk", "Aim"):
                clips.append(clip)

    if DEBUG:
        print("root", game_object(root)["m_Name"], "scale", root_scale, "animator", names[animator.path_id], "height", height, "ground", ground)
        for i, t in enumerate(order):
            p, q, s = rest[i]
            print("%2d parent %2s %-18s pos %s rot %s scale %s" % (i, "-" if parent[t.path_id] is None else index[parent[t.path_id]],
                  names[t.path_id], np.round(p, 3), np.round(q, 3), np.round(s, 3)))
        print({name: names[order[i].path_id] for name, i in special.items()})
        for clip in clips:
            print(clip.name, clip.stop, [(names[by_hash[b["path"]].path_id] if b["path"] in by_hash else b["path"], b["attribute"], b["customType"])
                                         for b in clip.bindings])
            print("   t=0", np.round(clip.values(0.0), 3))

    folder = os.path.join(resources, "assets", "karlson", "enemymesh")
    os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(folder, "rig.txt"), "w", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write("rig %d %d %d %d %d %d %d %d %.5f\n" % (len(order), len(binds), len(verts), len(tris), special["gunPosition"], special["head"],
                                                       special["rightArm"], special["leftArm"], 1.0 / (SCALE * root_scale)))
        f.write("root " + " ".join("%.6f" % v for v in root_matrix.flatten()) + "\n")
        for i, t in enumerate(order):
            p, q, s = rest[i]
            up = parent[t.path_id]
            f.write("t %d %s %s\n" % (-1 if up is None else index[up], names[t.path_id].replace(" ", "_"), " ".join("%.6f" % v for v in p + q + s)))
        for i, b in enumerate(binds):
            f.write("b %d %s\n" % (bone_index[i], " ".join("%.6f" % v for v in b[:3].flatten())))
        for i in range(len(verts)):
            f.write("v %s %s %.5f %.5f %s %s\n" % (" ".join("%.5f" % v for v in verts[i]), " ".join("%.4f" % v for v in normals[i]),
                                                 uvs[i][0], 1.0 - uvs[i][1], " ".join(str(int(b)) for b in indices[i]),
                                                 " ".join("%.4f" % w for w in weights[i])))
        for tri in tris:
            f.write("f %d %d %d\n" % tuple(tri))
        for clip in clips:
            bound = []
            for b in clip.bindings:
                t = by_hash.get(b["path"])
                if t is not None and index[t.path_id] not in bound:
                    bound.append(index[t.path_id])
            frames = max(1, int(round(clip.stop * FPS)))
            f.write("clip %s %d %.5f %s\n" % (clip.name, frames, clip.stop, " ".join(str(b) for b in bound)))
            for frame in range(frames):
                values = clip.values(clip.stop * frame / frames)
                pose = {b: [list(rest[b][0]), list(rest[b][1])] for b in bound}
                at = 0
                for b in clip.bindings:
                    size = {1: 3, 2: 4, 3: 3, 4: 3}.get(b["attribute"], 1)
                    if b["path"] not in by_hash:
                        at += size
                        continue
                    target = index[by_hash[b["path"]].path_id]
                    if b["attribute"] == 1:
                        pose[target][0] = values[at:at + 3]
                        at += 3
                    elif b["attribute"] == 2:
                        pose[target][1] = values[at:at + 4]
                        at += 4
                    elif b["attribute"] == 4:
                        pose[target][1] = list(euler_quat(*[math.radians(v) for v in values[at:at + 3]]))
                        at += 3
                    elif b["attribute"] == 3:
                        at += 3
                    else:
                        at += 1
                f.write("k " + " ".join("%.5f" % v for b in bound for v in pose[b][0] + pose[b][1]) + "\n")
            print("%s: %d frames over %.3fs, %d transforms" % (clip.name, frames, clip.stop, len(bound)))
    print("%d transforms, %d bones, %d vertices, %d triangles; %.2f units tall -> %.2f blocks; gun at %s"
          % (len(order), len(binds), len(verts), len(tris), height, HEIGHT, names[order[special["gunPosition"]].path_id]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main(sys.argv[1])

==== tools/build_gun_assets.py ====
"""Turn Karlson's gun meshes (OBJ exports of the Unity meshes) into the mod's resources.

For every gun this writes
  assets/karlson/gunmesh/<name>.txt   triangles in "gun space", with normals and material colours
  assets/karlson/models/item/<name>.json  display transforms (the geometry is drawn by GunSpecialRenderer)
  assets/karlson/items/<name>.json
  assets/karlson/textures/item/<name>.png  break particle colour
and prints the muzzle offsets used by GunGeometry.java.

Gun space is the gun exactly as Karlson holds it (DetectWeapons: localRotation = Euler(0, 90, 0), prefab scale kept),
in Minecraft axes and blocks: x right, y up, barrel towards -z, origin at the weapon's pivot.

Usage: python build_gun_assets.py <mod resources dir>
"""
import json
import math
import os
import sys

from PIL import Image

SCALE = 1.8 / 3.4
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

GUN = (74, 74, 82)
WOOD = (75, 26, 3)
GRAPPLE = (43, 38, 34)
PROTO_GREY = (68, 68, 68)

GUNS = {
    "pistol": ("pistol.obj", (0.09, 0.09, 0.09), [GUN], (-4.41, 2.03, 0.0)),
    "uzi": ("ak47.obj", (0.1, 0.08, 0.1), [GUN], (-3.12, 0.0, 0.0)),
    "shotgun": ("shotgun.obj", (0.08, 0.1, 0.1), [GUN, WOOD, GUN, WOOD], (-4.1, -0.111, 0.0)),
    "boomer": ("boomer.obj", (0.1, 0.1, 0.1), [GUN, PROTO_GREY], (-4.94, 0.0, 0.0)),
    "grappler": ("grappler.obj", (0.09, 0.09, 0.09), [GRAPPLE], (-4.809, 1.715, 0.0)),
}


def load_obj(path):
    """The OBJ export mirrors x, so (x, y, z) here is Unity's (-x, y, z)."""
    verts, normals, groups, cur = [], [], [], None
    for line in open(path):
        p = line.split()
        if not p:
            continue
        if p[0] == "v":
            verts.append(tuple(float(c) for c in p[1:4]))
        elif p[0] == "vn":
            normals.append(tuple(float(c) for c in p[1:4]))
        elif p[0] == "g":
            cur = []
            groups.append(cur)
        elif p[0] == "f":
            idx = [tuple(int(i) - 1 if i else -1 for i in (s.split("/") + ["", ""])[:3]) for s in p[1:]]
            for i in range(1, len(idx) - 1):
                cur.append((idx[0], idx[i], idx[i + 1]))
    return verts, normals, [g for g in groups if g]


def main():
    res = sys.argv[1]
    base = os.path.join(res, "assets", "karlson")
    for sub in ("gunmesh", "models/item", "items", "textures/item"):
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(base, sub), exist_ok=True)
    for name, (objfile, (sx, sy, sz), colors, tip) in GUNS.items():
        verts, normals, groups = load_obj(os.path.join(HERE, "meshes", objfile))

        def pos(v):
            return (v[2] * sz * SCALE, v[1] * sy * SCALE, -v[0] * sx * SCALE)

        def nrm(n):
            x, y, z = n[2] / sz, n[1] / sy, -n[0] / sx
            l = math.sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z) or 1.0
            return (x / l, y / l, z / l)

        lines = []
        pts = []
        for gi, tris in enumerate(groups):
            r, g, b = colors[min(gi, len(colors) - 1)]
            for tri in tris:
                for vi, _, ni in tri:
                    p = pos(verts[vi])
                    n = nrm(normals[ni])
                    pts.append(p)
                    lines.append("%.5f %.5f %.5f %.3f %.3f %.3f %d %d %d" % (p + n + (r, g, b)))
        with open(os.path.join(base, "gunmesh", name + ".txt"), "w", newline="\n") as f:
            f.write("%d\n" % (len(lines) // 3))
            f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

        mn = [min(p[i] for p in pts) for i in range(3)]
        mx = [max(p[i] for p in pts) for i in range(3)]
        c = [(mn[i] + mx[i]) / 2 for i in range(3)]
        length = mx[2] - mn[2]
        height = mx[1] - mn[1]
        low = [p for p in pts if p[1] < mn[1] + height * 0.45 and (name != "shotgun" or p[2] > c[2] + length * 0.15)]
        grip = [sum(p[i] for p in low) / len(low) for i in range(3)]
        grip[1] = mn[1] + height * (0.5 if name != "shotgun" else 0.35)
        tip_gs = (tip[2] * sz * SCALE, tip[1] * sy * SCALE, tip[0] * sx * SCALE)

        def centred(scale, rot_y, extra=(0, 0, 0)):
            """translation (in 1/16 block) that puts the middle of the gun at the item origin after rotate+scale."""
            a = math.radians(rot_y)
            x = c[0] * math.cos(a) + c[2] * math.sin(a)
            z = -c[0] * math.sin(a) + c[2] * math.cos(a)
            return [round((-scale * x + extra[0]) * 16, 3), round((-scale * c[1] + extra[1]) * 16, 3), round((-scale * z + extra[2]) * 16, 3)]

        gui = round(min(4.0, 0.92 / max(length, height)), 3)

        def isometric(yaw, pitch, roll, fill=0.98, flipped=False, rolled=False):
            """The inventory icon: the gun seen from above its left side, barrel up and to the left.

            Turned about y, tipped towards the viewer, then rolled on the screen; item transforms take x-y-z
            Euler angles, so that rotation is converted, and the gun is scaled and centred to fill the slot."""
            y, x, z = math.radians(yaw), math.radians(pitch), math.radians(roll)
            ry = [[math.cos(y), 0, math.sin(y)], [0, 1, 0], [-math.sin(y), 0, math.cos(y)]]
            rx = [[1, 0, 0], [0, math.cos(x), -math.sin(x)], [0, math.sin(x), math.cos(x)]]
            rz = [[math.cos(z), -math.sin(z), 0], [math.sin(z), math.cos(z), 0], [0, 0, 1]]

            def mul(a, b):
                return [[sum(a[i][k] * b[k][j] for k in range(3)) for j in range(3)] for i in range(3)]

            m = mul(rz, mul(rx, ry))
            aim = [sum(m[i][j] * (tip_gs[j] - c[j]) for j in range(3)) for i in range(2)]
            if aim[0] > 0 and not flipped:
                return isometric(yaw + 180, pitch, roll, fill, True)
            if aim[1] < 0 and not rolled:
                return isometric(yaw, pitch, -roll, fill, flipped, True)
            turned = [[sum(m[i][j] * p[j] for j in range(3)) for i in range(3)] for p in pts]
            lo = [min(t[i] for t in turned) for i in range(3)]
            hi = [max(t[i] for t in turned) for i in range(3)]
            scale = round(fill / max(hi[0] - lo[0], hi[1] - lo[1]), 3)
            angles = [math.degrees(math.atan2(-m[1][2], m[2][2])), math.degrees(math.asin(max(-1.0, min(1.0, m[0][2])))),
                      math.degrees(math.atan2(-m[0][1], m[0][0]))]
            return {"rotation": [round(a, 2) for a in angles],
                    "translation": [round(-scale * (lo[i] + hi[i]) / 2 * 16, 3) for i in range(3)],
                    "scale": [scale] * 3}
        ground = 1.6
        third = 1.5
        display = {
            "gui": isometric(-118, 28, -38),
            "fixed": {"rotation": [0, 90, 0], "translation": centred(gui * 0.9, 90), "scale": [round(gui * 0.9, 3)] * 3},
            "on_shelf": {"rotation": [0, 90, 0], "translation": centred(gui * 0.9, 90), "scale": [round(gui * 0.9, 3)] * 3},
            "ground": {"rotation": [0, 0, 0], "translation": centred(ground, 0, (0, 0.12, 0)), "scale": [ground] * 3},
            "thirdperson_righthand": {"rotation": [0, 0, 0], "translation": [round(-third * grip[0] * 16, 3), round(-third * grip[1] * 16 - 2.0, 3), round(-third * grip[2] * 16 + 1.5, 3)], "scale": [third] * 3},
            "thirdperson_lefthand": {"rotation": [0, 0, 0], "translation": [round(-third * grip[0] * 16, 3), round(-third * grip[1] * 16 - 2.0, 3), round(-third * grip[2] * 16 + 1.5, 3)], "scale": [third] * 3},
        }
        with open(os.path.join(base, "models", "item", name + ".json"), "w", newline="\n") as f:
            json.dump({"gui_light": "front", "textures": {"particle": f"karlson:item/{name}"}, "display": display}, f, indent=2)
        with open(os.path.join(base, "items", name + ".json"), "w", newline="\n") as f:
            json.dump({"model": {"type": "minecraft:special", "base": f"karlson:item/{name}", "model": {"type": "karlson:gun", "gun": name}}}, f, indent=2)
        Image.new("RGBA", (16, 16), colors[0] + (255,)).save(os.path.join(base, "textures", "item", name + ".png"))
        print(f'{name}: {len(lines) // 3} tris, size {[round(mx[i] - mn[i], 3) for i in range(3)]}, tip {[round(t, 4) for t in tip_gs]}, grip {[round(t, 3) for t in grip]}, gui scale {gui}')
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(base, "textures", "entity"), exist_ok=True)
    Image.new("RGBA", (4, 4), (255, 255, 255, 255)).save(os.path.join(base, "textures", "entity", "white.png"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==== tools/build_music.py ====
"""Build the mod's music from the installed copy of Karlson, plus anything dropped into tools/extra_music.

    python tools/build_music.py src/main/resources

Every AudioClip longer than 30 seconds in Karlson's data is treated as music. Extra songs (ogg/mp3/wav/flac/m4a)
placed in tools/extra_music are converted and added to the same playlist. Needs UnityPy and ffmpeg.
"""
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys

DATA = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "..", "Karlson", "Karlson_Data")
MIN_SECONDS = 30.0
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
EXTRA = os.path.join(HERE, "extra_music")


def slug(name):
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", name.lower()).strip("_") or "track"


def peak(src):
    """The loudest sample of a file in dB below full scale (a negative number)."""
    out = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-i", src, "-vn", "-af", "volumedetect", "-f", "null", "-"],
                         capture_output=True, text=True).stderr
    for line in out.splitlines():
        if "max_volume:" in line:
            return float(line.split("max_volume:")[1].split("dB")[0])
    return 0.0


def encode(src, dst):
    gain = -1.0 - peak(src)
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-i", src, "-vn", "-ac", "2", "-ar", "44100",
                    "-af", "volume=%.2fdB" % gain, "-c:a", "libvorbis", "-q:a", "4", dst], check=True)


def main(resources):
    out = os.path.join(resources, "assets", "karlson", "sounds", "music")
    os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
    for old in os.listdir(out):
        os.remove(os.path.join(out, old))
    tracks = []

    import UnityPy
    files = [os.path.join(DATA, f) for f in os.listdir(DATA)
             if f.endswith(".assets") or f.endswith(".resource") or re.fullmatch(r"level\d+", f)]
    env = UnityPy.load(*[f for f in files if not f.endswith(".resource")])
    seen = set()
    for obj in env.objects:
        if obj.type.name != "AudioClip":
            continue
        clip = obj.read()
        length = getattr(clip, "m_Length", 0.0)
        name = slug(clip.m_Name)
        if length < MIN_SECONDS or name in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(name)
        for _, data in clip.samples.items():
            tmp = os.path.join(out, name + ".wav")
            with open(tmp, "wb") as f:
                f.write(data)
            encode(tmp, os.path.join(out, name + ".ogg"))
            os.remove(tmp)
            tracks.append(name)
            print("karlson: %s (%.0fs)" % (clip.m_Name, length))
            break

    if os.path.isdir(EXTRA):
        for file in sorted(os.listdir(EXTRA)):
            base, ext = os.path.splitext(file)
            if ext.lower() not in (".ogg", ".mp3", ".wav", ".flac", ".m4a"):
                continue
            name = slug(base)
            while name in seen:
                name += "_x"
            seen.add(name)
            encode(os.path.join(EXTRA, file), os.path.join(out, name + ".ogg"))
            tracks.append(name)
            print("extra: %s" % file)

    path = os.path.join(resources, "assets", "karlson", "sounds.json")
    with open(path, encoding="utf8") as f:
        sounds = json.load(f)
    for key in [k for k in sounds if k == "music" or k.startswith("music.")]:
        del sounds[key]
    sounds["music"] = {"sounds": [{"name": "karlson:music/" + t, "stream": True} for t in tracks]}
    for t in tracks:
        sounds["music." + t] = {"sounds": [{"name": "karlson:music/" + t, "stream": True}]}
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf8", newline="\n") as f:
        json.dump(sounds, f, indent="\t")
        f.write("\n")
    print("%d tracks" % len(tracks))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main(sys.argv[1])

==== tools/build_spawn_egg.py ====
"""Build the enemy's spawn egg from Minecraft's old spawn egg art.

    python tools/build_spawn_egg.py src/main/resources

Before 1.21.5 every spawn egg was the same two grey textures (the egg and its spots) multiplied by two colours;
the creeper's was green with black spots. This does the same with Karlson yellow and black, reading the two
textures from an older Minecraft client jar that the launcher already has. Needs Pillow.
"""
import io
import os
import sys
import zipfile

JAR = os.path.expandvars(r"%APPDATA%\PrismLauncher\libraries\com\mojang\minecraft\1.20.1\minecraft-1.20.1-client.jar")
EGG = (246, 190, 38)
SPOTS = (0, 0, 0)


def tinted(image, color):
    out = image.copy()
    px = out.load()
    for y in range(out.height):
        for x in range(out.width):
            r, g, b, a = px[x, y]
            px[x, y] = (r * color[0] // 255, g * color[1] // 255, b * color[2] // 255, a)
    return out


def main(resources):
    from PIL import Image
    with zipfile.ZipFile(JAR) as jar:
        egg = Image.open(io.BytesIO(jar.read("assets/minecraft/textures/item/spawn_egg.png"))).convert("RGBA")
        spots = Image.open(io.BytesIO(jar.read("assets/minecraft/textures/item/spawn_egg_overlay.png"))).convert("RGBA")
    out = Image.alpha_composite(tinted(egg, EGG), tinted(spots, SPOTS))
    path = os.path.join(resources, "assets", "karlson", "textures", "item", "enemy_spawn_egg.png")
    out.save(path)
    print(path, out.size)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main(sys.argv[1])

==== tools/make_release.py ====
"""Build "Karlson Mod Release" next to the project folder: a copy of the project with comments removed and long local names abbreviated,
plus the same code as one text file.

    python tools/make_release.py

The jar is built afterwards from its source folder with its own gradlew.
"""
import io
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import sys
import tokenize

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(ROOT), "Karlson Mod Release")
SRC_OUT = os.path.join(OUT, "source")
SINGLE = os.path.join(OUT, "KarlsonCraft-source.txt")

KEYWORDS = set("""abstract assert boolean break byte case catch char class const continue default do double else enum
extends final finally float for goto if implements import instanceof int interface long native new package private
protected public return short static strictfp super switch synchronized this throw throws transient try void volatile
while var record yield true false null sealed permits""".split())

WORDS = {
    "velocity": "vel", "position": "pos", "direction": "dir", "distance": "dist", "rotation": "rot",
    "context": "ctx", "movement": "move", "current": "cur", "previous": "prev", "forward": "fwd",
    "buffer": "buf", "renderer": "rend", "camera": "cam", "entity": "ent", "normal": "norm", "length": "len",
    "index": "idx", "count": "cnt", "offset": "off", "origin": "orig", "horizontal": "horiz",
    "vertical": "vert", "explosion": "expl", "impulse": "imp", "strength": "str", "amount": "amt",
    "random": "rnd", "message": "msg", "value": "val", "result": "res", "source": "src", "config": "cfg",
    "server": "srv", "weapon": "wpn", "payload": "pkt", "inventory": "inv", "particle": "ptc",
    "ragdoll": "doll", "target": "tgt", "center": "ctr", "centre": "ctr", "damage": "dmg", "player": "plr",
    "quaternion": "quat", "matrix": "mat", "vector": "vec", "segment": "seg", "segments": "segs",
    "lifetime": "life", "gravity": "grav", "friction": "fric", "collision": "coll", "constraint": "cons",
    "partial": "part", "speed": "spd", "acceleration": "accel", "angle": "ang", "radius": "rad",
    "color": "col", "colour": "col", "translation": "trans", "transform": "xform", "listener": "lsnr",
    "sender": "sndr", "bullet": "blt", "bullets": "blts", "minecraft": "mc", "connection": "conn",
    "difficulty": "diff", "attributes": "attrs", "parameters": "params", "properties": "props",
    "predicate": "pred", "iterator": "iter", "snapshot": "snap", "elapsed": "dt", "progress": "prog",
    "interpolated": "lerped", "smoothed": "smooth", "desired": "want", "wanted": "want", "margin": "pad",
    "location": "loc", "dimension": "dim", "dimensions": "dims", "texture": "tex", "vertices": "verts",
    "vertex": "vert", "triangle": "tri", "triangles": "tris", "stack": "stk", "level": "lvl",
}

TOKEN = re.compile(r'"""(?:[^\\]|\\.)*?"""|"(?:[^"\\\n]|\\.)*"|\'(?:[^\'\\\n]|\\.)*\'|[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*|\s+|.', re.S)
IDENT = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*\Z")


def strip_c_comments(text):
    out = []
    i, n = 0, len(text)
    while i < n:
        c = text[i]
        if text.startswith('"""', i):
            j = text.index('"""', i + 3) + 3
            out.append(text[i:j])
            i = j
        elif c in "\"'":
            j = i + 1
            while j < n and text[j] != c:
                j += 2 if text[j] == "\\" else 1
            out.append(text[i:j + 1])
            i = j + 1
        elif text.startswith("//", i):
            j = text.find("\n", i)
            j = n if j < 0 else j
            out.append("\0")
            i = j
        elif text.startswith("/*", i):
            j = text.index("*/", i) + 2
            out.append("".join(ch if ch == "\n" else "\0" for ch in text[i:j]))
            i = j
        else:
            out.append(c)
            i += 1
    return tidy("".join(out))


def tidy(text):
    lines = []
    for line in text.split("\n"):
        if "\0" in line:
            line = line.replace("\0", "")
            if not line.strip():
                continue
        lines.append(line.rstrip())
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", "\n".join(lines))
    text = re.sub(r"\{\n\n", "{\n", text)
    return text.strip("\n") + "\n"


def strip_py_comments(text):
    lines = text.split("\n")
    cut = {}
    for tok in tokenize.generate_tokens(io.StringIO(text).readline):
        if tok.type == tokenize.COMMENT:
            cut[tok.start[0] - 1] = tok.start[1]
    out = []
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if i in cut:
            line = line[:cut[i]].rstrip()
            if not line:
                continue
        out.append(line)
    return re.sub(r"\n{4,}", "\n\n\n", "\n".join(out)).strip("\n") + "\n"


def shorten_word(name):
    parts = re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+|[A-Z][a-z0-9]*", name)
    if "".join(parts) != name:
        return name
    res = []
    for k, part in enumerate(parts):
        low = part.lower()
        short = WORDS.get(low, low)
        res.append(short if k == 0 else short[0].upper() + short[1:])
    return "".join(res)


def tokens(text):
    return TOKEN.findall(text)


def scan(files):
    """Names that are only ever plain local/unqualified identifiers and are declared somewhere in the mod."""
    every, bad, declared = set(), set(), set()
    for text in files.values():
        toks = [t for t in tokens(text) if not t.isspace()]
        line_start = True
        skip = False
        for i, t in enumerate(toks):
            if not IDENT.match(t):
                continue
            every.add(t)
            prev = toks[i - 1] if i else ""
            nxt = toks[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(toks) else ""
            if prev in (".", ":") or nxt == "(" or prev == "@":
                bad.add(t)
            if (IDENT.match(prev) and prev not in KEYWORDS or prev in (">", "]")) and nxt in ("=", ";", ",", ")", ":"):
                declared.add(t)
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            if line.startswith("import ") or line.startswith("package "):
                bad.update(re.findall(r"[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*", line))
    return every, bad, declared


def build_map(files, exclude):
    every, bad, declared = scan(files)
    mapping = {}
    used = set(every) | KEYWORDS
    for name in sorted(declared - bad - KEYWORDS):
        if name in exclude or not name[0].islower() or len(name) < 6:
            continue
        short = shorten_word(name)
        if len(short) > len(name) - 2 or short in used:
            continue
        mapping[name] = short
        used.add(short)
    return mapping


def rename(text, mapping):
    return "".join(mapping.get(t, t) if IDENT.match(t) else t for t in tokens(text))


def main(exclude):
    if os.path.isdir(SRC_OUT):
        shutil.rmtree(SRC_OUT)
    os.makedirs(SRC_OUT)

    java = {}
    for base in ("src/main/java", "src/gametest/java"):
        for folder, _, names in os.walk(os.path.join(ROOT, base)):
            for name in names:
                if name.endswith(".java"):
                    path = os.path.join(folder, name)
                    rel = os.path.relpath(path, ROOT).replace("\\", "/")
                    with open(path, encoding="utf8") as f:
                        java[rel] = strip_c_comments(f.read())
    mapping = build_map(java, exclude)
    for rel in java:
        if "/mixin/" not in rel:
            java[rel] = rename(java[rel], mapping)

    text_files = dict(java)
    for rel in ("build.gradle", "settings.gradle"):
        with open(os.path.join(ROOT, rel), encoding="utf8") as f:
            text_files[rel] = strip_c_comments(f.read())
    with open(os.path.join(ROOT, "gradle.properties"), encoding="utf8") as f:
        text_files["gradle.properties"] = "\n".join(l for l in f.read().split("\n") if l.strip() and not l.lstrip().startswith("#")) + "\n"
    for name in sorted(os.listdir(os.path.join(ROOT, "tools"))):
        if name.endswith(".py"):
            with open(os.path.join(ROOT, "tools", name), encoding="utf8") as f:
                text_files["tools/" + name] = strip_py_comments(f.read())

    for rel, text in text_files.items():
        dst = os.path.join(SRC_OUT, rel)
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
        with open(dst, "w", encoding="utf8", newline="\n") as f:
            f.write(text)

    shutil.copytree(os.path.join(ROOT, "src/main/resources"), os.path.join(SRC_OUT, "src/main/resources"))
    gametest_res = os.path.join(ROOT, "src/gametest/resources")
    if os.path.isdir(gametest_res):
        shutil.copytree(gametest_res, os.path.join(SRC_OUT, "src/gametest/resources"))
    shutil.copytree(os.path.join(ROOT, "gradle"), os.path.join(SRC_OUT, "gradle"))
    for name in ("gradlew", "gradlew.bat"):
        if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(ROOT, name)):
            shutil.copy2(os.path.join(ROOT, name), os.path.join(SRC_OUT, name))
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(SRC_OUT, "tools", "extra_music"), exist_ok=True)

    music = os.path.join(SRC_OUT, "src/main/resources/assets/karlson/sounds/music")
    if os.path.isdir(music):
        shutil.rmtree(music)
    sounds_rel = "src/main/resources/assets/karlson/sounds.json"
    with open(os.path.join(ROOT, sounds_rel), encoding="utf8") as f:
        sounds = json.load(f)
    sounds = {k: v for k, v in sounds.items() if k != "music" and not k.startswith("music.")}
    with open(os.path.join(SRC_OUT, sounds_rel), "w", encoding="utf8", newline="\n") as f:
        json.dump(sounds, f, indent=2)
        f.write("\n")

    single = dict(text_files)
    for rel in ("src/main/resources/fabric.mod.json", "src/main/resources/karlson.mixins.json",
                sounds_rel, "src/main/resources/assets/karlson/lang/en_us.json"):
        with open(os.path.join(SRC_OUT if rel == sounds_rel else ROOT, rel), encoding="utf8") as f:
            single[rel] = f.read().strip("\n") + "\n"
    with open(SINGLE, "w", encoding="utf8", newline="\n") as f:
        for rel in sorted(single):
            f.write("==== " + rel + " ====\n" + single[rel] + "\n")

    before = 0
    for rel in java:
        with open(os.path.join(ROOT, rel), encoding="utf8") as f:
            before += len(f.read())
    after = sum(len(t) for t in java.values())
    print("%d java files, %d -> %d chars, %d names shortened" % (len(java), before, after, len(mapping)))
    print(" ".join("%s>%s" % kv for kv in sorted(mapping.items())))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main(set(sys.argv[1:]))
